\# 1. Purpose

F1 menetapkan konstitusi lifecycle bagi PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

Jika Part D mendefinisikan bagaimana PD-03 beroperasi ketika organizational and operating model berlaku, maka Part F mendefinisikan bagaimana kondisi organisasi dan operating boundary PD-03 dapat mengalami perubahan yang terkendali sepanjang lifecycle-nya.

F1 menetapkan prinsip konstitusional untuk:

\- lifecycle;

\- lifecycle change;

\- lifecycle state;

\- lifecycle transition;

\- creation;

\- restructuring;

\- transition;

\- retirement;

\- dissolution;

\- lifecycle governance;

\- lifecycle traceability;

\- lifecycle integrity;

\- lifecycle continuity.

F1 menjadi constitutional parent bagi:

\- F2 — Lifecycle Model;

\- F3 — Lifecycle States;

\- F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

\- F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance;

\- F6 — Lifecycle Review;

\- F7 — Lifecycle Control;

\- F8 — Lifecycle Implementation;

\- F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity;

\- F10 — Lifecycle Success.

\---

\# 2. Lifecycle Constitutional Position

Part F merupakan boundary untuk \*\*controlled lifecycle change\*\*.

Part F tidak menggantikan:

\- Platform Identity;

\- Organization Architecture;

\- Governance Architecture;

\- Governance Operations;

\- Performance Architecture.

Relationship:

\`\`\`text

Part A

Platform Identity & Strategic Foundation

↓

Part B

Organization Architecture

↓

Part C

Governance Architecture

↓

Part D

Governance Operations

↓

Part E

Performance Architecture

↓

Part F

Lifecycle Architecture

Part F menggunakan hasil dari Part A–E sebagai constitutional and operational context.

Part F kemudian menyediakan mechanism untuk mengelola perubahan lifecycle tanpa menciptakan architectural drift.

⸻

3\. Lifecycle Boundary

Source baseline menempatkan organizational lifecycle concerns berikut pada Part F:

• creation;

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• dissolution.

Part D secara eksplisit membedakan lifecycle tersebut dari operating model.

Dengan demikian:

Current Operating Model

↓

Part D

Lifecycle Change

↓

Part F

Part F tidak mengatur bagaimana pekerjaan harian dilakukan.

Part F mengatur bagaimana kondisi lifecycle dari organizational architecture dapat berubah secara sah dan terkendali.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Lifecycle Principle

No material lifecycle change shall occur without an identifiable lifecycle context, applicable authority, sufficient evidence, controlled transition, and traceable outcome.

Lifecycle change tidak boleh dilakukan hanya karena:

• convenience;

• informal agreement;

• operational preference;

• undocumented restructuring;

• temporary workaround;

• unrecorded authority.

Setiap material lifecycle change harus dapat ditelusuri kepada basis yang sah.

⸻

5\. Lifecycle and Organizational Identity

Lifecycle tidak boleh mengubah identitas PD-03 secara informal.

PD-03 tetap:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

selama tidak terdapat keputusan yang sah untuk mengubah Platform identity tersebut.

Lifecycle change dapat memengaruhi:

• organizational structure;

• responsibility allocation;

• capability arrangement;

• team arrangement;

• reporting arrangement;

• operating configuration;

tetapi perubahan tersebut harus tetap berada dalam applicable constitutional authority.

⸻

6\. Lifecycle and Domain Boundary

Lifecycle change tidak boleh digunakan untuk memperluas domain PD-03 secara informal.

Primary domain tetap:

Governance & Compliance

dengan responsibility utama:

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Perubahan organizational configuration tidak otomatis memberikan PD-03 ownership atas:

• Architecture;

• Knowledge & Intelligence;

• Runtime & Execution;

• AI Engineering;

• Infrastructure & Platform;

• Security;

• Quality & Evaluation;

• Developer Experience.

Platform lain tetap mempertahankan domain ownership masing-masing.

⸻

7\. Lifecycle Change vs Operational Change

Lifecycle change harus dibedakan dari operational change.

Operational Change

↓

Mengubah cara pekerjaan dijalankan

↓

Part D

sedangkan:

Lifecycle Change

↓

Mengubah lifecycle condition,

organizational configuration,

atau status struktural

↓

Part F

Tidak semua operational improvement merupakan lifecycle change.

Tidak semua lifecycle change merupakan operational improvement.

⸻

8\. Lifecycle Change Categories

Secara constitutional, Part F mencakup sekurang-kurangnya lima kategori lifecycle change:

8.1 Creation

Pembentukan entity, organizational configuration, atau lifecycle subject yang sebelumnya belum berlaku.

8.2 Restructuring

Perubahan material terhadap struktur atau configuration tanpa menganggap subject lama otomatis dissolved.

8.3 Transition

Perpindahan dari satu lifecycle condition ke condition lain.

8.4 Retirement

Penghentian status aktif atau penggunaan suatu lifecycle subject tanpa langsung menganggap seluruh historical record hilang.

8.5 Dissolution

Pengakhiran eksistensi organizational atau lifecycle subject sesuai authority yang berlaku.

Detail eligibility dan transition semantics ditetapkan pada F2–F4.

⸻

9\. Lifecycle Authority Principle

Lifecycle authority harus berasal dari applicable governance and authority architecture.

Model:

Lifecycle Matter

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Lifecycle Decision

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Controlled Transition

Lifecycle architecture tidak menciptakan authority baru.

F1 hanya menetapkan bahwa setiap material lifecycle action harus memiliki applicable authority.

Authority detail tetap berasal dari:

• Part C;

• applicable governance charter;

• authority and mandate;

• delegation;

• higher-level constitutional authority.

⸻

10\. Lifecycle Accountability

Lifecycle change harus memiliki accountable owner.

Minimum distinction:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Authority memberikan right to decide atau authorize.

Accountability menetapkan responsibility terhadap outcome.

Execution melaksanakan authorized change.

Ketiganya tidak boleh diasumsikan identik.

⸻

11\. Lifecycle Evidence Principle

Material lifecycle decision harus memiliki evidence yang proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• impact;

• organizational scope;

• dependency;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• cross-platform effect.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• current state;

• target state;

• rationale;

• dependency impact;

• governance assessment;

• performance evidence;

• organizational impact;

• implementation readiness;

• transition result.

Tidak semua lifecycle action membutuhkan tingkat evidence yang sama.

⸻

12\. Lifecycle Traceability

Setiap material lifecycle change harus dapat ditelusuri melalui:

Lifecycle Matter

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Verification

↓

Result

↓

Canonical Record

Traceability harus mempertahankan hubungan antara:

• initiating matter;

• evidence;

• authority;

• decision;

• implementation;

• resulting state.

⸻

13\. Lifecycle State Principle

Lifecycle subject harus memiliki identifiable state.

State harus:

• dapat dibedakan;

• memiliki definisi;

• memiliki entry condition;

• memiliki exit condition apabila berlaku;

• memiliki owner;

• dapat diverifikasi;

• dapat direkam.

F1 tidak menetapkan daftar final lifecycle states.

Daftar state canonical, semantics, dan transition matrix ditetapkan pada F3–F4 setelah source reconciliation.

⸻

14\. Lifecycle Transition Principle

Transition tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai perubahan status administratif semata.

Material transition harus menjawab:

1\. dari state apa;

2\. menuju state apa;

3\. mengapa transition diperlukan;

4\. siapa yang memiliki authority;

5\. evidence apa yang diperlukan;

6\. dependency apa yang terdampak;

7\. bagaimana transition diverifikasi;

8\. bagaimana resulting state dicatat.

Model:

Current State

↓

Transition Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

⸻

15\. Lifecycle Integrity

Lifecycle integrity berarti:

• lifecycle state benar;

• transition sah;

• authority valid;

• ownership tetap jelas;

• evidence tersedia;

• record dapat ditelusuri;

• boundary tetap terjaga;

• resulting state konsisten dengan decision.

Lifecycle integrity tidak boleh dikorbankan demi kecepatan perubahan.

⸻

16\. Lifecycle Continuity

Retirement atau restructuring tidak boleh secara otomatis menghapus:

• historical decisions;

• evidence;

• accountability records;

• governance records;

• architectural records;

• compliance records;

• performance records;

• lifecycle history.

Historical traceability harus tetap dipertahankan sesuai applicable retention and governance requirements.

⸻

17\. Lifecycle and Governance

Part F bergantung pada Governance Architecture untuk:

• authority;

• decision;

• delegation;

• accountability;

• review;

• escalation;

• compliance.

Relationship:

Governance Architecture

↓

Lifecycle Governance

↓

Lifecycle Decision

↓

Lifecycle Transition

Part F tidak boleh menggantikan Part C.

⸻

18\. Lifecycle and Organization Architecture

Part F dapat menyebabkan perubahan terhadap organization architecture.

Namun perubahan tersebut harus melalui applicable organizational architecture mechanism.

Relationship:

Lifecycle Decision

↓

Authorized Organizational Change

↓

Part B

↓

Updated Organization State

Lifecycle Architecture menentukan lifecycle concern.

Organization Architecture menentukan resulting organizational structure.

Keduanya tidak boleh dicampur.

⸻

19\. Lifecycle and Operations

Part D mengoperasikan kondisi yang sedang berlaku.

Part F mengatur perubahan menuju kondisi lifecycle berikutnya.

Part D

Operate Current State

↓

Part E

Observe / Measure

↓

Part F

Evaluate / Authorize Change

↓

Part D

Operate Resulting State

Dengan demikian lifecycle change harus mempertimbangkan operational evidence tanpa mengambil alih operating model.

⸻

20\. Lifecycle and Performance

Performance Architecture menyediakan evidence yang dapat digunakan untuk lifecycle assessment.

Relationship:

Performance Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Assessment

↓

Change Decision

↓

Lifecycle Transition

Performance result tidak otomatis memerintahkan lifecycle change.

Performance evidence adalah input.

Lifecycle authority tetap berada pada applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

21\. Cross-Platform Lifecycle Boundary

PD-03 dapat mengalami lifecycle change yang memiliki dependency atau impact terhadap Platform lain.

Contoh:

PD-03 Lifecycle Change

↓

Cross-Platform Impact

↓

PD-02 Architecture

PD-08 Security

PD-09 Quality

Other Applicable Platforms

Cross-platform impact tidak berarti PD-03 memperoleh ownership atas Platform lain.

Setiap Platform mempertahankan:

• domain ownership;

• execution responsibility;

• applicable authority;

• compliance responsibility.

⸻

22\. Lifecycle Change and Platform Autonomy

Lifecycle change harus menghormati Platform autonomy.

PD-03 tidak boleh menggunakan lifecycle authority untuk:

• mengambil alih execution Platform lain;

• mengubah architecture Platform lain tanpa applicable authority;

• mengubah security ownership;

• mengubah quality ownership;

• mengubah runtime ownership;

• memindahkan domain ownership secara informal.

Cross-platform change harus menggunakan applicable coordination and governance mechanism.

⸻

23\. Lifecycle Control Principle

Material lifecycle change harus dikendalikan melalui:

• authorization;

• state validation;

• transition validation;

• evidence;

• traceability;

• review;

• exception handling;

• canonical recording.

Control harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

24\. Lifecycle Exception Principle

Lifecycle exception dapat terjadi apabila normal transition mechanism tidak dapat digunakan.

Exception harus:

1\. diidentifikasi;

2\. diklasifikasikan;

3\. memiliki rationale;

4\. memiliki owner;

5\. memiliki applicable authority;

6\. memiliki mitigation;

7\. memiliki disposition;

8\. dicatat;

9\. direview apabila material.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi mekanisme permanen untuk menghindari lifecycle governance.

⸻

25\. Lifecycle Reversibility

Apabila secara teknis dan governance memungkinkan, transition harus mempertimbangkan reversibility.

Namun:

Reversibility is not universally required.

Jika transition irreversible:

• irreversibility harus diketahui sebelum authorization;

• consequence harus dinilai;

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• resulting state harus dapat diverifikasi.

⸻

26\. Lifecycle Change Impact

Material lifecycle change harus mempertimbangkan dampak terhadap:

• organizational structure;

• authority;

• accountability;

• governance;

• compliance;

• operations;

• performance;

• dependencies;

• canonical artifacts;

• cross-platform interfaces.

Impact assessment harus proporsional terhadap scope perubahan.

⸻

Material lifecycle change harus menghasilkan canonical record yang sekurang-kurangnya dapat mengidentifikasi:

Field tambahan dapat ditetapkan oleh child lifecycle sections.

⸻

⸻

29\. Lifecycle Maturity Principle

Lifecycle capability harus dapat berkembang dari:

Ad Hoc

↓

Defined

↓

Managed

↓

Controlled

↓

Adaptive

Namun F1 tidak menetapkan maturity score.

Maturity assessment ditetapkan pada F9.

⸻

30\. Lifecycle Success Boundary

Lifecycle success tidak hanya berarti:

transition berhasil dieksekusi.

Lifecycle success harus mempertimbangkan:

• correctness;

• authority compliance;

• boundary preservation;

• traceability;

• continuity;

• operational readiness;

• governance integrity;

• resulting-state validity;

• cross-platform integrity.

Success criteria detail ditetapkan pada F10.

⸻

31\. Out of Scope

F1 tidak menetapkan:

• detailed lifecycle state catalogue;

• detailed transition matrix;

• organizational unit names;

• team structure;

• role assignments;

• operational workflow;

• performance KPI;

• detailed compliance controls;

• technical implementation;

• infrastructure mechanism;

• individual workforce assignment;

• compensation;

• implementation-specific tooling.

Hal-hal tersebut berada pada applicable child architecture atau domain owner.

⸻

32\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline source yang tersedia secara eksplisit mendukung bahwa lifecycle/change concern untuk organizational architecture berada pada Part F dan mencakup:

• creation;

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• dissolution.

Baseline juga menetapkan separation antara lifecycle concern dan operating model.

Namun source yang tersedia belum memberikan canonical literal definition lengkap untuk F1 atau definitive F1–F10 state/transition taxonomy.

Karena itu:

F1 menggunakan Bounded Canonical Synthesis.

Tidak ada lifecycle state atau transition vocabulary yang belum dibuktikan source yang boleh diperlakukan sebagai immutable canonical fact hanya berdasarkan pola konstruksi.

⸻

33\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Jika implementasi canonical repository membutuhkan detail yang belum tersedia dalam source, Claude Code harus:

1\. melakukan source reconciliation terhadap seluruh canonical corpus;

2\. mencari existing lifecycle terminology dan architecture decision yang relevan;

3\. memeriksa hubungan F1 dengan Part A–E;

4\. membedakan organizational lifecycle dari native entity lifecycle;

5\. tidak mengadopsi lifecycle state dari native core secara otomatis;

6\. tidak menciptakan authority baru;

7\. tidak memperluas PD-03 organizational boundary;

8\. mendokumentasikan setiap bounded synthesis;

9\. membuat Architecture Decision Record apabila terdapat material semantic choice;

10\. mengembalikan unresolved ambiguity sebagai explicit architecture issue apabila tidak dapat diselesaikan secara evidence-backed.

Jika ambiguity tersebut berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter yang berlaku.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh Founder Reserved Authority, Constitution, Canonical Architecture supremacy, atau perubahan fundamental terhadap governance boundary, Claude Code harus melakukan escalation sesuai applicable governance.

⸻

34\. Success Criteria

F1 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. lifecycle memiliki constitutional definition yang jelas;

2\. lifecycle dibedakan dari operating model;

3\. creation tercakup;

4\. restructuring tercakup;

5\. transition tercakup;

6\. retirement tercakup;

7\. dissolution tercakup;

8\. lifecycle authority tetap berasal dari applicable governance;

9\. lifecycle accountability dapat ditelusuri;

10\. lifecycle evidence requirement jelas;

11\. lifecycle state memiliki prinsip definitional yang jelas;

12\. transition membutuhkan current state dan resulting state yang dapat diidentifikasi;

13\. material lifecycle change memiliki traceability;

14\. historical lifecycle record tidak hilang tanpa authority;

15\. organizational ownership tetap terpisah dari lifecycle authority;

16\. cross-platform ownership tetap terjaga;

17\. operational change tidak otomatis dianggap lifecycle change;

18\. performance evidence tidak otomatis menjadi lifecycle decision;

19\. lifecycle exception memiliki governance;

20\. irreversible transition memiliki additional scrutiny;

21\. lifecycle record memiliki minimum canonical fields;

22\. F2–F10 memiliki constitutional parent yang jelas;

23\. tidak ada authority baru yang diciptakan oleh F1;

24\. tidak ada domain expansion PD-03;

25\. tidak terdapat material contradiction dengan Part A–E;

26\. unresolved source gaps ditandai secara eksplisit;

27\. lifecycle constitution dapat diwariskan sebagai pattern tanpa memaksakan domain-specific content kepada Platform lain.

36\. Canonical Constitution Statement

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution establishes the constitutional framework through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance may create, restructure, transition, retire, or dissolve applicable organizational lifecycle subjects in a governed, evidence-based, traceable, and bounded manner while preserving the authority model, organizational ownership, Platform autonomy, governance integrity, operational continuity, and performance feedback established by the preceding architecture.

Lifecycle change shall not be treated as informal operational change. Material lifecycle change requires identifiable lifecycle context, applicable authority, proportional evidence, controlled transition, verification, and canonical traceability.

F1 does not create lifecycle authority, organizational ownership, or technical execution authority. It establishes the constitutional boundary within which subsequent lifecycle architecture sections define the model, states, transitions, governance, controls, implementation, improvement, maturity, and success conditions.

\# 1. Purpose

F2 menetapkan model lifecycle tingkat arsitektural bagi PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

F2 menerjemahkan prinsip constitutional yang ditetapkan pada F1 menjadi model yang menjelaskan:

\- apa yang dimaksud dengan lifecycle subject;

\- bagaimana lifecycle dipandang sebagai rangkaian kondisi;

\- bagaimana lifecycle change berhubungan dengan current state dan resulting state;

\- bagaimana creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, dan dissolution ditempatkan dalam lifecycle;

\- bagaimana lifecycle berhubungan dengan governance, organization, operations, dan performance;

\- bagaimana lifecycle model mempertahankan continuity dan traceability.

F2 tidak menetapkan lifecycle state catalogue final.

F2 menyediakan \*\*model konseptual\*\* yang menjadi parent bagi:

\- F3 — Lifecycle States;

\- F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

\- F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance;

\- F6 — Lifecycle Review;

\- F7 — Lifecycle Control;

\- F8 — Lifecycle Implementation;

\- F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity;

\- F10 — Lifecycle Success.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

F2 berada langsung di bawah F1 — Lifecycle Constitution.

↓

↓

mengapa lifecycle harus dikendalikan dan boundary apa yang berlaku.

F2 menetapkan:

bagaimana lifecycle dimodelkan.

state apa yang digunakan.

F4 kemudian menetapkan:

bagaimana perpindahan antar-state dilakukan.

⸻

3\. Lifecycle Model Definition

Lifecycle adalah model yang merepresentasikan perubahan kondisi suatu lifecycle subject sepanjang keberadaan, perubahan, penggunaan, penghentian, dan pengakhiran subject tersebut.

Secara constitutional:

Lifecycle

\=

Sequence of Governed Conditions

-

Authorized Change

-

Evidence

-

Traceability

Lifecycle tidak hanya berarti urutan waktu.

Lifecycle juga merepresentasikan:

• condition;

• authority;

• responsibility;

• evidence;

• transition;

• resulting condition;

• historical continuity.

⸻

4\. Lifecycle Subject

Lifecycle Model berlaku terhadap lifecycle subject yang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Lifecycle subject dapat berupa organizational atau structural subject yang memiliki:

• identifiable identity;

• defined scope;

• ownership;

• lifecycle state;

• governance context;

• historical record.

F2 tidak secara otomatis menetapkan seluruh kemungkinan lifecycle subject.

Jenis lifecycle subject harus ditentukan berdasarkan applicable organizational architecture.

⸻

5\. Lifecycle Subject Identity

Setiap lifecycle subject harus dapat dibedakan dari subject lain.

Minimum identity harus memungkinkan:

• unique identification;

• ownership identification;

• lifecycle state identification;

• historical traceability;

• relationship identification;

• lifecycle change tracking.

Perubahan lifecycle tidak boleh menyebabkan hilangnya identity history tanpa applicable authority.

⸻

6\. Lifecycle as a State Model

F2 memodelkan lifecycle sebagai perubahan antar kondisi.

Model dasar:

Current Lifecycle Condition

↓

Lifecycle Need / Trigger

↓

Assessment

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Transition

↓

Resulting Lifecycle Condition

Model ini merupakan abstraction.

State names, transition rules, dan eligibility criteria ditetapkan pada F3–F4.

⸻

7\. Lifecycle Continuity

Lifecycle bersifat continuous.

Selesainya satu transition tidak berarti lifecycle berhenti.

Model:

State

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• resulting condition harus diverifikasi;

• historical record harus dipertahankan.

36\. Gold Standard Review

￼

⸻

37\. Canonical Lifecycle Model Statement

F2 — Lifecycle Model defines the lifecycle of PD-03 Governance & Compliance as a governed sequence of identifiable lifecycle conditions connected through authorized lifecycle events and transitions. The model encompasses creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, and dissolution while preserving identity, authority, accountability, evidence, historical continuity, organizational boundary, operational separation, and cross-platform ownership.

Lifecycle events initiate or implement change; lifecycle states represent the resulting condition of the lifecycle subject. A trigger does not constitute a decision, evidence does not constitute authorization, and operational change does not automatically constitute lifecycle change.

F2 establishes the lifecycle model but does not prematurely fix the canonical state catalogue or transition matrix. Those details belong to F3 and F4 and must be established through source reconciliation and applicable architecture authority.

⸻

\# 1. Purpose

F2 menetapkan model lifecycle tingkat arsitektural bagi PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

F2 menerjemahkan prinsip constitutional yang ditetapkan pada F1 menjadi model yang menjelaskan:

\- apa yang dimaksud dengan lifecycle subject;

\- bagaimana lifecycle dipandang sebagai rangkaian kondisi;

\- bagaimana lifecycle change berhubungan dengan current state dan resulting state;

\- bagaimana creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, dan dissolution ditempatkan dalam lifecycle;

\- bagaimana lifecycle berhubungan dengan governance, organization, operations, dan performance;

\- bagaimana lifecycle model mempertahankan continuity dan traceability.

F2 tidak menetapkan lifecycle state catalogue final.

F2 menyediakan \*\*model konseptual\*\* yang menjadi parent bagi:

\- F3 — Lifecycle States;

\- F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

\- F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance;

\- F6 — Lifecycle Review;

\- F7 — Lifecycle Control;

\- F8 — Lifecycle Implementation;

\- F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity;

\- F10 — Lifecycle Success.

\---

↓

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5–F10

mengapa lifecycle harus dikendalikan dan boundary apa yang berlaku.

F2 menetapkan:

F3 kemudian menetapkan:

state apa yang digunakan.

F4 kemudian menetapkan:

bagaimana perpindahan antar-state dilakukan.

3\. Lifecycle Model Definition

Lifecycle adalah model yang merepresentasikan perubahan kondisi suatu lifecycle subject sepanjang keberadaan, perubahan, penggunaan, penghentian, dan pengakhiran subject tersebut.

Secara constitutional:

Lifecycle

\=

Sequence of Governed Conditions

-

Authorized Change

-

Evidence

-

Traceability

Lifecycle tidak hanya berarti urutan waktu.

Lifecycle juga merepresentasikan:

• condition;

• authority;

• responsibility;

• evidence;

• transition;

• resulting condition;

• historical continuity.

⸻

4\. Lifecycle Subject

Lifecycle Model berlaku terhadap lifecycle subject yang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Lifecycle subject dapat berupa organizational atau structural subject yang memiliki:

• identifiable identity;

• defined scope;

• ownership;

• lifecycle state;

• governance context;

• historical record.

F2 tidak secara otomatis menetapkan seluruh kemungkinan lifecycle subject.

Jenis lifecycle subject harus ditentukan berdasarkan applicable organizational architecture.

⸻

• unique identification;

• ownership identification;

• lifecycle state identification;

• historical traceability;

• relationship identification;

• lifecycle change tracking.

Perubahan lifecycle tidak boleh menyebabkan hilangnya identity history tanpa applicable authority.

Jika repository canonical membutuhkan detail lifecycle model yang belum tersedia dalam source:

1\. lakukan source reconciliation terhadap seluruh canonical corpus;

2\. cari existing lifecycle terminology;

3\. cari existing lifecycle/entity model;

5\. bandingkan lifecycle model AIOS dengan lifecycle model khusus PD-03;

6\. jangan mengadopsi state model native AIOS secara otomatis;

7\. jangan mengubah lifecycle event menjadi lifecycle state tanpa semantic justification;

8\. jangan menciptakan authority baru;

9\. dokumentasikan bounded synthesis;

10\. buat Architecture Decision Record untuk material semantic choice;

11\. lanjutkan detail state pada F3;

12\. lanjutkan transition semantics pada F4.

Jika detail dapat diputuskan dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai delegation.

Jika keputusan mengubah:

• Constitution;

• Founder Reserved Authority;

• Canonical Architecture supremacy;

• fundamental governance boundary;

• cross-platform ownership;

maka Claude Code harus melakukan escalation sesuai applicable governance.

4\. lifecycle event dibedakan dari lifecycle state;

18\. lifecycle model tidak mengambil alih operational ownership;

37\. Canonical Lifecycle Model Statement

F2 — Lifecycle Model defines the lifecycle of PD-03 Governance & Compliance as a governed sequence of identifiable lifecycle conditions connected through authorized lifecycle events and transitions. The model encompasses creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, and dissolution while preserving identity, authority, accountability, evidence, historical continuity, organizational boundary, operational separation, and cross-platform ownership.

Lifecycle events initiate or implement change; lifecycle states represent the resulting condition of the lifecycle subject. A trigger does not constitute a decision, evidence does not constitute authorization, and operational change does not automatically constitute lifecycle change.

F2 establishes the lifecycle model but does not prematurely fix the canonical state catalogue or transition matrix. Those details belong to F3 and F4 and must be established through source reconciliation and applicable architecture authority.

\# 1. Purpose

F2 menetapkan model lifecycle tingkat arsitektural bagi PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

F2 menerjemahkan prinsip constitutional yang ditetapkan pada F1 menjadi model yang menjelaskan:

\- apa yang dimaksud dengan lifecycle subject;

\- bagaimana lifecycle dipandang sebagai rangkaian kondisi;

\- bagaimana lifecycle change berhubungan dengan current state dan resulting state;

\- bagaimana creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, dan dissolution ditempatkan dalam lifecycle;

\- bagaimana lifecycle berhubungan dengan governance, organization, operations, dan performance;

\- bagaimana lifecycle model mempertahankan continuity dan traceability.

F2 tidak menetapkan lifecycle state catalogue final.

F2 menyediakan \*\*model konseptual\*\* yang menjadi parent bagi:

\- F3 — Lifecycle States;

\- F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

\- F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance;

\- F6 — Lifecycle Review;

\- F7 — Lifecycle Control;

\- F8 — Lifecycle Implementation;

\- F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity;

\- F10 — Lifecycle Success.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

F2 berada langsung di bawah F1 — Lifecycle Constitution.

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5–F10

mengapa lifecycle harus dikendalikan dan boundary apa yang berlaku.

F2 menetapkan:

bagaimana lifecycle dimodelkan.

F3 kemudian menetapkan:

state apa yang digunakan.

F4 kemudian menetapkan:

bagaimana perpindahan antar-state dilakukan.

⸻

3\. Lifecycle Model Definition

Lifecycle adalah model yang merepresentasikan perubahan kondisi suatu lifecycle subject sepanjang keberadaan, perubahan, penggunaan, penghentian, dan pengakhiran subject tersebut.

Secara constitutional:

Lifecycle

\=

Sequence of Governed Conditions

-

Authorized Change

-

Evidence

-

Traceability

Lifecycle tidak hanya berarti urutan waktu.

Lifecycle juga merepresentasikan:

• condition;

• authority;

• responsibility;

• evidence;

• transition;

• resulting condition;

• historical continuity.

⸻

4\. Lifecycle Subject

Lifecycle Model berlaku terhadap lifecycle subject yang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Lifecycle subject dapat berupa organizational atau structural subject yang memiliki:

• identifiable identity;

• defined scope;

• ownership;

• lifecycle state;

• governance context;

• historical record.

F2 tidak secara otomatis menetapkan seluruh kemungkinan lifecycle subject.

Jenis lifecycle subject harus ditentukan berdasarkan applicable organizational architecture.

⸻

5\. Lifecycle Subject Identity

Setiap lifecycle subject harus dapat dibedakan dari subject lain.

Minimum identity harus memungkinkan:

• unique identification;

• ownership identification;

• lifecycle state identification;

• historical traceability;

• relationship identification;

• lifecycle change tracking.

Perubahan lifecycle tidak boleh menyebabkan hilangnya identity history tanpa applicable authority.

⸻

6\. Lifecycle as a State Model

F2 memodelkan lifecycle sebagai perubahan antar kondisi.

Model dasar:

Current Lifecycle Condition

↓

Lifecycle Need / Trigger

↓

Assessment

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Transition

↓

Resulting Lifecycle Condition

Model ini merupakan abstraction.

State names, transition rules, dan eligibility criteria ditetapkan pada F3–F4.

⸻

7\. Lifecycle Continuity

Lifecycle bersifat continuous.

Selesainya satu transition tidak berarti lifecycle berhenti.

Model:

State

↓

Change

↓

Transition

↓

New State

↓

Operation

↓

Observation

↓

Future Change

↓

New Transition

Dengan demikian:

Lifecycle is an ongoing governed process, not a single change event.

⸻

1\. Creation

2\. Restructuring

3\. Transition

4\. Retirement

5\. Dissolution

Kategori tersebut bukan daftar state.

Kategori tersebut merupakan types of lifecycle change or lifecycle event.

10\. Restructuring

Restructuring merupakan material change terhadap configuration atau structure lifecycle subject tanpa secara otomatis memperlakukan subject tersebut sebagai dissolved.

Contoh concern:

• responsibility reallocation;

• organizational restructuring;

• capability restructuring;

• reporting restructuring;

• structural boundary adjustment.

Existing Subject

↓

↓

↓

Restructuring harus mempertahankan historical continuity.

Transition merupakan perpindahan lifecycle subject dari satu lifecycle condition ke condition lain.

Current State

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition

↓

F2 hanya menetapkan model.

12\. Retirement

Retirement merupakan penghentian status aktif atau penggunaan lifecycle subject sesuai authority yang berlaku.

Retirement tidak otomatis berarti dissolution.

↓

Retirement Decision

↓

Retired Condition

Historical records harus tetap dipertahankan sesuai applicable governance and retention requirements.

13\. Dissolution

Dissolution merupakan pengakhiran eksistensi lifecycle subject sesuai authority yang berlaku.

Dissolution harus dibedakan dari retirement.

Retirement

≠

Dissolution

Retirement dapat menghentikan active condition.

Dissolution mengakhiri existence subject dalam applicable organizational model.

Dissolution harus memiliki:

• applicable authority;

• impact assessment;

• disposition;

• historical continuity;

• canonical record.

F2 menggunakan Bounded Canonical Synthesis.

37\. Canonical Lifecycle Model Statement

F2 — Lifecycle Model defines the lifecycle of PD-03 Governance & Compliance as a governed sequence of identifiable lifecycle conditions connected through authorized lifecycle events and transitions. The model encompasses creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, and dissolution while preserving identity, authority, accountability, evidence, historical continuity, organizational boundary, operational separation, and cross-platform ownership.

Lifecycle events initiate or implement change; lifecycle states represent the resulting condition of the lifecycle subject. A trigger does not constitute a decision, evidence does not constitute authorization, and operational change does not automatically constitute lifecycle change.

F2 establishes the lifecycle model but does not prematurely fix the canonical state catalogue or transition matrix. Those details belong to F3 and F4 and must be established through source reconciliation and applicable architecture authority.

\# 1. Purpose

F2 menetapkan model lifecycle tingkat arsitektural bagi PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

F2 menerjemahkan prinsip constitutional yang ditetapkan pada F1 menjadi model yang menjelaskan:

\- apa yang dimaksud dengan lifecycle subject;

\- bagaimana lifecycle dipandang sebagai rangkaian kondisi;

\- bagaimana lifecycle change berhubungan dengan current state dan resulting state;

\- bagaimana creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, dan dissolution ditempatkan dalam lifecycle;

\- bagaimana lifecycle berhubungan dengan governance, organization, operations, dan performance;

\- bagaimana lifecycle model mempertahankan continuity dan traceability.

F2 tidak menetapkan lifecycle state catalogue final.

F2 menyediakan \*\*model konseptual\*\* yang menjadi parent bagi:

\- F3 — Lifecycle States;

\- F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

\- F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance;

\- F6 — Lifecycle Review;

\- F7 — Lifecycle Control;

\- F8 — Lifecycle Implementation;

\- F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity;

\- F10 — Lifecycle Success.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

F2 berada langsung di bawah F1 — Lifecycle Constitution.

Relationship:

\`\`\`text

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5–F10

F1 menetapkan:

mengapa lifecycle harus dikendalikan dan boundary apa yang berlaku.

F2 menetapkan:

bagaimana lifecycle dimodelkan.

F3 kemudian menetapkan:

state apa yang digunakan.

F4 kemudian menetapkan:

bagaimana perpindahan antar-state dilakukan.

⸻

3\. Lifecycle Model Definition

Lifecycle adalah model yang merepresentasikan perubahan kondisi suatu lifecycle subject sepanjang keberadaan, perubahan, penggunaan, penghentian, dan pengakhiran subject tersebut.

Secara constitutional:

Lifecycle

\=

Sequence of Governed Conditions

-

Authorized Change

-

Evidence

-

Traceability

Lifecycle tidak hanya berarti urutan waktu.

Lifecycle juga merepresentasikan:

• condition;

• authority;

• responsibility;

• evidence;

• transition;

• resulting condition;

• historical continuity.

⸻

4\. Lifecycle Subject

Lifecycle Model berlaku terhadap lifecycle subject yang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Lifecycle subject dapat berupa organizational atau structural subject yang memiliki:

• identifiable identity;

• defined scope;

• ownership;

• lifecycle state;

• governance context;

• historical record.

F2 tidak secara otomatis menetapkan seluruh kemungkinan lifecycle subject.

Jenis lifecycle subject harus ditentukan berdasarkan applicable organizational architecture.

⸻

5\. Lifecycle Subject Identity

Setiap lifecycle subject harus dapat dibedakan dari subject lain.

Minimum identity harus memungkinkan:

• unique identification;

• ownership identification;

• lifecycle state identification;

• historical traceability;

• relationship identification;

• lifecycle change tracking.

Perubahan lifecycle tidak boleh menyebabkan hilangnya identity history tanpa applicable authority.

⸻

6\. Lifecycle as a State Model

F2 memodelkan lifecycle sebagai perubahan antar kondisi.

Model dasar:

Current Lifecycle Condition

↓

Lifecycle Need / Trigger

↓

Assessment

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Transition

↓

Resulting Lifecycle Condition

Model ini merupakan abstraction.

State names, transition rules, dan eligibility criteria ditetapkan pada F3–F4.

⸻

7\. Lifecycle Continuity

Lifecycle bersifat continuous.

Selesainya satu transition tidak berarti lifecycle berhenti.

Model:

State

↓

Change

↓

Transition

↓

New State

↓

Operation

↓

Observation

↓

Future Change

↓

New Transition

Dengan demikian:

Lifecycle is an ongoing governed process, not a single change event.

⸻

8\. Lifecycle Categories

F2 mempertahankan lima lifecycle change categories yang telah ditetapkan secara constitutional pada F1:

1\. Creation

2\. Restructuring

3\. Transition

4\. Retirement

5\. Dissolution

Kategori tersebut bukan daftar state.

Kategori tersebut merupakan types of lifecycle change or lifecycle event.

⸻

9\. Creation

Creation merupakan lifecycle event yang menyebabkan lifecycle subject baru menjadi established dalam applicable organizational architecture.

Secara konseptual:

No Established Subject

↓

Creation Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Creation

↓

Established Lifecycle Subject

Creation harus memiliki:

• applicable authority;

• defined identity;

• defined scope;

• ownership;

• initial lifecycle condition;

• canonical record.

F2 tidak menetapkan detail creation workflow.

⸻

10\. Restructuring

Restructuring merupakan material change terhadap configuration atau structure lifecycle subject tanpa secara otomatis memperlakukan subject tersebut sebagai dissolved.

Contoh concern:

• responsibility reallocation;

• organizational restructuring;

• capability restructuring;

• reporting restructuring;

• structural boundary adjustment.

Secara konseptual:

Existing Subject

↓

Restructuring Assessment

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Restructured Subject

Restructuring harus mempertahankan historical continuity.

⸻

11\. Transition

Transition merupakan perpindahan lifecycle subject dari satu lifecycle condition ke condition lain.

Secara konseptual:

Current State

↓

Transition Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition

↓

Resulting State

Transition merupakan concern utama F4.

F2 hanya menetapkan model.

⸻

12\. Retirement

Retirement merupakan penghentian status aktif atau penggunaan lifecycle subject sesuai authority yang berlaku.

Retirement tidak otomatis berarti dissolution.

Relationship:

Active Subject

↓

Retirement Decision

↓

Retired Condition

Historical records harus tetap dipertahankan sesuai applicable governance and retention requirements.

⸻

13\. Dissolution

Dissolution merupakan pengakhiran eksistensi lifecycle subject sesuai authority yang berlaku.

Dissolution harus dibedakan dari retirement.

Retirement

≠

Dissolution

Retirement dapat menghentikan active condition.

Dissolution mengakhiri existence subject dalam applicable organizational model.

Dissolution harus memiliki:

• applicable authority;

• impact assessment;

• disposition;

• historical continuity;

• canonical record.

⸻

14\. Lifecycle Model and Operating Model

F2 harus mempertahankan separation antara lifecycle architecture dan governance operations.

Part D

Operate Current Condition

↓

Part E

Measure / Observe

↓

Part F

Lifecycle Assessment / Change

↓

Part D

Operate Resulting Condition

Lifecycle Model tidak menggantikan operating model.

Lifecycle Model tidak menentukan daily work.

⸻

15\. Lifecycle Model and Organization Architecture

Lifecycle change dapat menghasilkan perubahan terhadap organizational architecture.

Relationship:

Lifecycle Change

↓

Authorized Organizational Change

↓

Part B

↓

Resulting Organizational Configuration

F2 tidak menentukan:

• organizational unit names;

• team structure;

• role structure;

• reporting hierarchy.

Hal tersebut tetap berada pada Part B.

⸻

16\. Lifecycle Model and Governance Architecture

Lifecycle Model bergantung pada Governance Architecture untuk:

• authority;

• decision;

• delegation;

• accountability;

• review;

• escalation;

• compliance.

Relationship:

Governance Architecture

↓

Lifecycle Governance

↓

Lifecycle Decision

↓

Lifecycle Change

F2 tidak menciptakan governance authority baru.

⸻

17\. Lifecycle Model and Performance Architecture

Performance evidence dapat menjadi input terhadap lifecycle assessment.

Performance Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Assessment

↓

Lifecycle Decision

↓

Lifecycle Change

Performance result tidak otomatis menyebabkan lifecycle transition.

Performance evidence adalah input.

Lifecycle decision tetap membutuhkan applicable authority.

⸻

18\. Lifecycle Trigger

Lifecycle change dapat dipicu oleh material condition yang membutuhkan perubahan lifecycle.

Potential trigger categories include:

• strategic change;

• organizational change;

• governance requirement;

• compliance requirement;

• performance evidence;

• material risk;

• dependency change;

• structural change;

• platform evolution;

• retirement requirement.

Trigger tidak otomatis memberikan authority.

Trigger hanya memulai assessment.

⸻

19\. Trigger-to-Decision Separation

F2 harus mempertahankan:

Trigger

≠

Decision

dan:

Evidence

≠

Authorization

Model:

Trigger

↓

Assessment

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

↓

Authority Decision

Hal ini mencegah lifecycle transition dilakukan hanya karena sebuah trigger muncul.

⸻

20\. Lifecycle Change Qualification

Tidak semua perubahan merupakan lifecycle change.

Perubahan harus diklasifikasikan berdasarkan materiality dan architectural effect.

Minor Operational Change

↓

Part D

Performance Observation

↓

Part E

Material Lifecycle Change

↓

Part F

Materiality harus mempertimbangkan:

• structural impact;

• authority impact;

• ownership impact;

• dependency impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• reversibility;

• governance significance.

⸻

21\. Lifecycle Model and Materiality

Material lifecycle change harus memiliki level control yang proporsional.

Model:

Low Materiality

↓

Simplified Lifecycle Handling

High Materiality

↓

Enhanced Assessment

↓

Enhanced Governance

↓

Enhanced Evidence

↓

Enhanced Verification

F2 tidak menetapkan numerical materiality threshold.

Threshold harus ditentukan pada applicable governance and control architecture.

⸻

22\. Lifecycle Dependency Model

Lifecycle change dapat memiliki dependency terhadap:

• organization;

• governance;

• operations;

• performance;

• architecture;

• security;

• quality;

• runtime;

• other Platform Divisions.

Dependency tidak berarti ownership transfer.

Model:

Lifecycle Subject

↓

Dependency Assessment

↓

Impact Identification

↓

Coordination

↓

Authorized Change

⸻

23\. Cross-Platform Lifecycle

Apabila lifecycle change memiliki material cross-platform impact:

PD-03 Lifecycle Matter

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Cross-Platform Coordination

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Lifecycle Decision

PD-03 tidak memperoleh ownership terhadap Platform yang terdampak.

Setiap Platform mempertahankan domain ownership dan execution responsibility masing-masing.

⸻

24\. Lifecycle State Independence

Lifecycle state harus merepresentasikan condition lifecycle.

State tidak boleh hanya menjadi:

• workflow status;

• ticket status;

• task status;

• project status;

• approval status.

Lifecycle state harus menggambarkan condition of the lifecycle subject.

Dengan demikian:

Workflow State

≠

Lifecycle State

⸻

25\. Lifecycle Event vs Lifecycle State

F2 membedakan:

Lifecycle Event

↓

Something happens

dengan:

Lifecycle State

↓

Condition after / around the event

Contoh konseptual:

Creation

↓

Established Condition

dan:

Retirement Event

↓

Retired Condition

Terminology final harus dikonfirmasi pada F3–F4.

⸻

26\. Lifecycle Transition Semantics

Setiap transition harus dapat menjelaskan:

• source condition;

• trigger;

• assessment;

• authority;

• transition;

• verification;

• resulting condition.

Model:

Source Condition

↓

Trigger

↓

Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting Condition

F2 menetapkan model ini.

F4 menetapkan transition semantics secara detail.

⸻

27\. Lifecycle Model and Irreversibility

Lifecycle model harus dapat membedakan transition yang:

• reversible;

• conditionally reversible;

• irreversible.

Irreversibility harus diketahui sebelum authorization apabila dapat diketahui.

Untuk irreversible transition:

• impact harus dinilai;

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• resulting condition harus diverifikasi;

• historical record harus dipertahankan.

⸻

28\. Lifecycle History

Lifecycle model harus mempertahankan historical continuity.

Minimum historical chain:

Subject Identity

↓

Previous Condition

↓

Lifecycle Event

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Resulting Condition

Historical record tidak boleh dihapus hanya karena lifecycle subject berubah condition.

⸻

29\. Lifecycle Model and Canonical Record

Lifecycle Model membutuhkan canonical representation yang memungkinkan setiap material lifecycle change ditelusuri.

Minimum conceptual fields:

Field Requirement

Lifecycle Subject Required

Current Condition Required

Change Category Required

Trigger Required when applicable

Authority Required

Decision Reference Required

Evidence Required

Resulting Condition Required when applicable

Verification Required

Effective Date Required

Historical Reference Required

Field detail dapat diperluas pada F4–F8.

⸻

30\. Lifecycle Model and Governance Loop

F2 menggunakan lifecycle loop:

Current Condition

↓

Lifecycle Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Governance Review

↓

Authority Decision

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Transition

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting Condition

↓

Operation

↓

Performance Observation

│

└──────────────► Future Lifecycle Need

Loop tersebut mempertahankan hubungan:

• governance;

• lifecycle;

• operations;

• performance.

⸻

31\. Lifecycle Model Boundaries

F2 does define:

• lifecycle conceptual model;

• lifecycle subject concept;

• lifecycle change categories;

• event/state distinction;

• current/resulting condition relationship;

• lifecycle continuity;

• lifecycle dependency;

• lifecycle trigger concept;

• lifecycle transition abstraction.

F2 does not define:

• final state names;

• complete state catalogue;

• transition matrix;

• detailed authorization matrix;

• workflow implementation;

• team ownership;

• technical lifecycle engine;

• KPI;

• maturity scoring.

⸻

32\. Out of Scope

• detailed lifecycle state definitions;

• state entry criteria;

• state exit criteria;

• operational workflow;

• implementation tooling;

• technical state machine;

• infrastructure lifecycle;

• workforce lifecycle;

• employment lifecycle;

• compensation lifecycle.

Hal-hal tersebut berada pada applicable child architecture atau domain owner.

37\. Canonical Lifecycle Model Statement

F2 — Lifecycle Model defines the lifecycle of PD-03 Governance & Compliance as a governed sequence of identifiable lifecycle conditions connected through authorized lifecycle events and transitions. The model encompasses creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, and dissolution while preserving identity, authority, accountability, evidence, historical continuity, organizational boundary, operational separation, and cross-platform ownership.

Lifecycle events initiate or implement change; lifecycle states represent the resulting condition of the lifecycle subject. A trigger does not constitute a decision, evidence does not constitute authorization, and operational change does not automatically constitute lifecycle change.

F2 establishes the lifecycle model but does not prematurely fix the canonical state catalogue or transition matrix. Those details belong to F3 and F4 and must be established through source reconciliation and applicable architecture authority.

\# 1. Purpose

F3 menetapkan arsitektur lifecycle state bagi PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

F3 menerjemahkan Lifecycle Model pada F2 menjadi struktur kondisi lifecycle yang dapat:

\- diidentifikasi;

\- dibedakan;

\- dimasuki;

\- ditinggalkan;

\- diverifikasi;

\- direkam;

\- ditelusuri;

\- digunakan sebagai dasar transition governance.

F3 menjadi parent bagi semantic dan transition detail yang akan digunakan pada:

\- F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

\- F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance;

\- F6 — Lifecycle Review;

\- F7 — Lifecycle Control;

\- F8 — Lifecycle Implementation;

\- F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity;

\- F10 — Lifecycle Success.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

F3 berada di bawah:

\`\`\`text

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

F1 menetapkan:

mengapa lifecycle harus dikendalikan.

F2 menetapkan:

bagaimana lifecycle dimodelkan.

F3 menetapkan:

kondisi lifecycle apa yang harus dapat dibedakan.

F4 menetapkan:

bagaimana perpindahan antar-condition dikendalikan.

⸻

3\. Lifecycle State Definition

Lifecycle state adalah kondisi yang secara canonical merepresentasikan keadaan lifecycle suatu lifecycle subject pada titik tertentu.

State bukan sekadar:

• workflow status;

• ticket status;

• approval status;

• task status;

• project status;

• operational status.

Canonical distinction:

Workflow Status

≠

Operational Status

≠

Lifecycle State

Lifecycle state harus menggambarkan condition of the lifecycle subject, bukan hanya progress dari suatu pekerjaan.

⸻

4\. State Integrity Principle

Setiap lifecycle state harus memiliki minimum semantic integrity:

0\. identifiable;

0\. semantically distinct;

0\. defined entry condition;

0\. defined exit condition apabila applicable;

0\. accountable owner;

0\. applicable authority context;

0\. verification basis;

0\. canonical record.

Hal ini mengikuti prinsip F1 bahwa lifecycle state harus dapat dibedakan, memiliki definisi, entry/exit condition apabila berlaku, owner, dapat diverifikasi, dan dapat direkam.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. State Model

F3 menggunakan lifecycle state model berikut sebagai bounded canonical model:

┌─────────────────────┐

│ Pre-Established │

└──────────┬──────────┘

│

│ Authorized Creation

▼

┌─────────────────────┐

│ Established │

└──────────┬──────────┘

│

│ Authorized Lifecycle Change

▼

┌─────────────────────┐

│ Transitional / │

│ Change Condition │

└──────────┬──────────┘

│

│ Transition Verification

▼

┌─────────────────────┐

│ Established / │

│ Resulting Condition │

└──────────┬──────────┘

│

├───────────────┐

│ │

│ Retirement │ Dissolution

▼ ▼

┌─────────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────────┐

│ Retired │ │ Dissolved │

└─────────────────────┘ └─────────────────────┘

Catatan canonical:

Model di atas merupakan state architecture, bukan transition matrix final.

F4 menetapkan transition eligibility dan transition semantics secara rinci.

⸻

6\. State Categories

F3 mengenali lifecycle condition berikut:

￼

State categories ini diturunkan dari lifecycle concerns yang source-backed: creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, dan dissolution.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

7\. Pre-Established State

7.1 Definition

Pre-Established merepresentasikan kondisi ketika lifecycle subject belum menjadi established subject dalam applicable organizational architecture.

State ini memberikan boundary sebelum creation.

7.2 Purpose

State ini mencegah subject dianggap sudah established hanya karena:

• proposal dibuat;

• discussion terjadi;

• requirement muncul;

• approval preparation dimulai;

• implementation task dibuat.

7.3 Entry Condition

Pre-Established merupakan condition sebelum authorized creation menghasilkan established subject.

7.4 Exit Condition

Exit terjadi ketika creation telah:

• memperoleh applicable authority;

• memenuhi required evidence;

• menghasilkan identity;

• menghasilkan canonical record;

• diverifikasi sebagai established.

7.5 Authority

Pre-Established tidak memberikan operational authority kepada subject.

⸻

8\. Established State

8.1 Definition

Established merepresentasikan lifecycle subject yang telah sah dibentuk dan memiliki identity, scope, ownership, dan applicable governance context.

8.2 Minimum Conditions

Established state harus memiliki:

• lifecycle identity;

• defined scope;

• accountable owner;

• applicable authority context;

• organizational relationship;

• canonical record;

• verifiable condition.

8.3 Operational Relationship

Established tidak otomatis berarti:

• active execution;

• fully mature;

• fully optimized;

• permanently stable.

Established hanya berarti subject telah menjadi lifecycle subject yang valid dalam applicable architecture.

8.4 Continuity

Established subject dapat mengalami:

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• other authorized lifecycle change.

⸻

9\. Transitional / Change Condition

9.1 Definition

Transitional / Change Condition merepresentasikan kondisi ketika lifecycle subject sedang mengalami material lifecycle change dan belum mencapai resulting state yang telah diverifikasi.

State ini digunakan untuk menjaga distinction antara:

Current State

dan:

Resulting State

9.2 Purpose

State ini mencegah sistem mencatat resulting condition sebelum transition selesai.

9.3 Required Evidence

Material transition harus dapat menunjukkan:

• source condition;

• target condition;

• authorized change;

• evidence;

• transition execution;

• verification status.

F2 telah menetapkan bahwa material transition harus dapat menjawab current state, resulting state, authority, evidence, dependency, verification, dan recording.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

9.4 Constraint

Transitional / Change Condition tidak boleh digunakan sebagai permanent state.

Jika transition gagal, dibatalkan, atau memerlukan exception, resulting condition harus ditentukan melalui applicable transition governance.

⸻

10\. Resulting Established Condition

Setelah material lifecycle change selesai dan diverifikasi, lifecycle subject kembali berada pada established condition yang sesuai dengan hasil transition.

Secara konseptual:

Established

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting Established Condition

Resulting condition dapat berbeda secara material dari previous condition dalam:

• organizational structure;

• responsibility allocation;

• capability arrangement;

• team arrangement;

• reporting arrangement;

• operating configuration.

Perubahan tersebut tetap harus berada dalam applicable authority.

⸻

11\. Retired State

11.1 Definition

Retired merepresentasikan lifecycle subject yang tidak lagi berada dalam active applicable condition tetapi belum dianggap dissolved.

Retirement dan dissolution harus tetap dibedakan. F1 secara eksplisit membedakan retirement sebagai penghentian status aktif/penggunaan dari dissolution sebagai pengakhiran eksistensi subject.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

11.2 Historical Continuity

Retired subject harus mempertahankan historical traceability terhadap:

• decisions;

• evidence;

• accountability;

• governance records;

• compliance records;

• performance records;

• lifecycle history.

11.3 Operational Constraint

Retired subject tidak boleh kembali ke active operation tanpa applicable lifecycle transition.

11.4 Review

Retired state dapat memiliki:

• review date;

• retention period;

• disposition requirement;

apabila applicable.

⸻

12\. Dissolved State

12.1 Definition

Dissolved merepresentasikan pengakhiran eksistensi lifecycle subject sesuai applicable authority.

12.2 Distinction

Retired

≠

Dissolved

Retired:

subject tidak lagi aktif.

Dissolved:

eksistensi subject telah diakhiri.

12.3 Historical Record

Dissolution tidak berarti historical record dihapus.

Historical record tetap dipertahankan sesuai applicable retention and governance requirements.

12.4 Reinstatement

Dissolved subject tidak boleh dianggap kembali established hanya karena operational need muncul.

Jika re-establishment diperlukan, hal tersebut harus melalui applicable creation mechanism atau lifecycle mechanism yang secara eksplisit mengizinkannya.

⸻

13\. State vs Lifecycle Event

F3 mempertahankan distinction:

Lifecycle Event

↓

Something happens

sedangkan:

Lifecycle State

↓

Condition of the subject

Contoh:

Creation Event

↓

Established State

Retirement Event

↓

Retired State

Dissolution Event

↓

Dissolved State

Event tidak boleh secara otomatis dianggap state.

⸻

14\. State vs Change Type

Lifecycle change category juga bukan state.

Creation

Restructuring

Transition

Retirement

Dissolution

adalah change/event categories.

Sedangkan:

Pre-Established

Established

Transitional / Change

Retired

Dissolved

adalah state categories.

Canonical distinction:

Change Type

≠

Lifecycle State

⸻

15\. State Identity

Setiap lifecycle subject harus memiliki state yang dapat diidentifikasi secara canonical.

Minimum record:

￼

Field tambahan dapat ditetapkan oleh F4–F8.

⸻

16\. State Entry Conditions

State entry harus berdasarkan condition yang dapat diverifikasi.

Entry condition tidak boleh hanya:

• verbal declaration;

• informal agreement;

• task completion;

• calendar date;

• assumption.

State harus berubah hanya ketika applicable criteria telah terpenuhi.

⸻

17\. State Exit Conditions

State exit harus memiliki defined criteria apabila exit tersebut membutuhkan governance control.

Contoh:

Established

↓

Exit only after authorized lifecycle change

Transitional / Change

↓

Exit only after transition result is determined

Retired

↓

Exit only through authorized lifecycle mechanism

Dissolved

↓

Normally terminal unless applicable architecture explicitly provides another mechanism

⸻

18\. State Verification

State verification harus menjawab:

0\. apakah state yang dicatat benar;

0\. apakah entry condition terpenuhi;

0\. apakah authority valid;

0\. apakah evidence cukup;

0\. apakah resulting condition sesuai decision;

0\. apakah canonical record telah diperbarui.

Lifecycle integrity mensyaratkan state benar, authority valid, evidence tersedia, record dapat ditelusuri, dan resulting state konsisten dengan decision.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

19\. State Ownership

State ownership harus dibedakan dari authority.

Authority

↓

Right to authorize

Accountability

↓

Responsibility for outcome

State Ownership

↓

Responsibility for lifecycle condition

Execution

↓

Performs authorized change

F1 secara eksplisit mempertahankan distinction:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

20\. State Authority

Lifecycle state tidak memberikan authority hanya karena subject berada dalam state tertentu.

Contoh:

Established

≠

Unlimited Authority

dan:

Retired

≠

No Historical Responsibility

State hanya menggambarkan condition.

Authority tetap berasal dari Governance Architecture dan applicable authority model.

⸻

21\. State Materiality

Tidak semua state change memiliki materiality yang sama.

Materiality dapat dipengaruhi oleh:

• structural impact;

• authority impact;

• ownership impact;

• dependency impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• risk;

• reversibility.

Control intensity harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

22\. State Transition Boundary

F3 hanya menetapkan state model.

F3 tidak menetapkan final transition matrix.

Relationship:

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

Defines Valid State Semantics

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

Defines Allowed State Changes

F4 harus menetapkan:

• allowed source state;

• allowed target state;

• trigger;

• authority;

• evidence;

• verification;

• failure handling;

• rollback/recovery apabila applicable.

⸻

23\. State Transition Integrity

Tidak boleh terdapat state transition yang:

• melewati required governance;

• tidak memiliki authority;

• tidak memiliki evidence ketika evidence required;

• tidak memiliki resulting-state verification;

• menghapus historical continuity;

• melanggar organizational boundary;

• mengambil alih ownership Platform lain.

⸻

24\. Restructuring and State

Restructuring tidak otomatis membutuhkan state baru.

Model default:

Established

↓

Restructuring

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Established — Resulting Condition

State tetap dapat menjadi Established meskipun internal organizational configuration berubah.

Ini mencegah setiap restructuring menciptakan state taxonomy baru.

⸻

25\. Transition and State

Transition adalah mekanisme perpindahan antar lifecycle conditions.

Contoh konseptual:

Established

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Established — Resulting Condition

Transition semantics final berada pada F4.

⸻

26\. Retirement and State

Retirement mengikuti pola:

Established

↓

Retirement Decision

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Retired

Retirement harus mempertahankan historical continuity.

⸻

27\. Dissolution and State

Dissolution mengikuti pola:

Established

↓

Dissolution Decision

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Dissolved

Dissolution harus memiliki applicable authority dan canonical record.

⸻

28\. Creation and State

Creation mengikuti pola:

Pre-Established

↓

Creation Decision

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Established

Creation tidak dianggap selesai hanya karena authorization telah diberikan.

Resulting established condition harus diverifikasi dan direkam.

⸻

29\. State Continuity

Lifecycle state change harus mempertahankan continuity:

Subject Identity

↓

Previous State

↓

Lifecycle Event

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Resulting State

Historical identity tidak boleh hilang karena state berubah.

⸻

30\. State Record

Canonical state record harus memungkinkan reconstruction atas lifecycle condition pada titik waktu tertentu.

Minimum:

Subject

State

Effective Time

Authority

Decision

Transition

Verification

History

Record harus dapat mendukung audit, governance review, compliance review, dan architecture review apabila applicable.

⸻

31\. Cross-Platform State Boundary

PD-03 lifecycle state tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyatakan lifecycle state Platform lain.

Contoh:

PD-03 State

≠

PD-02 State

≠

PD-08 State

Cross-platform dependency dapat dicatat sebagai relationship atau impact.

Namun state ownership tetap berada pada applicable Platform.

⸻

32\. Performance Relationship

Performance evidence dapat memengaruhi lifecycle assessment, tetapi tidak otomatis mengubah lifecycle state.

Performance Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Governance Decision

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

State Change

Dengan demikian:

Performance Result

≠

Automatic State Transition

Ini konsisten dengan F1/F2 bahwa performance evidence merupakan input, sedangkan lifecycle authority tetap berada pada applicable governance mechanism.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

33\. Operational Relationship

Operating state dan lifecycle state harus tetap dibedakan.

Lifecycle State

↓

Defines lifecycle condition

Operating State

↓

Defines current operational condition

PD-03 tidak boleh mengubah lifecycle state hanya karena operational state berubah tanpa material lifecycle basis.

⸻

34\. State Exceptions

Jika state condition tidak dapat ditentukan secara normal:

0\. ambiguity harus dicatat;

0\. current known state harus dipertahankan apabila valid;

0\. exception harus diklasifikasikan;

0\. authority harus ditentukan;

0\. evidence harus dikumpulkan;

0\. resulting state harus ditetapkan melalui applicable governance.

Tidak boleh mengisi state dengan tebakan hanya untuk menghilangkan ambiguity.

⸻

35\. State Ambiguity Rule

Jika terdapat dua atau lebih candidate states dan evidence tidak cukup untuk membedakannya:

Candidate State A

-

Candidate State B

↓

Insufficient Evidence

↓

DO NOT AUTO-SELECT

System harus:

• preserve known state;

• flag ambiguity;

• request assessment;

• escalate apabila material.

Ini mencegah lifecycle record menjadi false precision.

⸻

36\. Terminal State Principle

Dissolved diperlakukan sebagai terminal state secara default.

Namun F3 tidak mengklaim bahwa seluruh AIOS lifecycle subject secara universal tidak dapat dire-establish.

Jika architecture membutuhkan re-establishment:

• mekanisme harus eksplisit;

• authority harus jelas;

• identity continuity harus ditentukan;

• historical relation harus dipertahankan;

• keputusan harus terdokumentasi.

⸻

37\. State Taxonomy Boundary

F3 does define:

• state concept;

• state categories;

• state semantics;

• state entry/exit principles;

• state identity;

• state ownership distinction;

• state verification;

• state continuity;

• state recording;

• relationship between state and event;

• relationship between state and change type.

F3 does not define:

• role-by-role state authority;

• final transition matrix;

• detailed workflow;

• implementation technology;

• lifecycle engine;

• numeric maturity score;

• lifecycle KPI;

• platform-wide universal lifecycle taxonomy.

⸻

38\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Source baseline secara eksplisit mendukung bahwa lifecycle subject harus memiliki identifiable state dengan:

• definisi;

• entry condition;

• exit condition apabila berlaku;

• owner;

• verification;

• record.

Source juga mendukung lifecycle categories:

• creation;

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• dissolution.

Namun source baseline yang tersedia tidak memberikan definitive literal state catalogue untuk PD-03.

Karena itu F3 menggunakan:

Bounded Canonical Synthesis

State categories yang diperkenalkan di section ini harus diperlakukan sebagai architectural model derived from the frozen F1/F2 boundaries, bukan sebagai klaim bahwa vocabulary tersebut telah ditemukan secara literal dalam historical source.

⸻

Claude Code harus melakukan reconciliation berikut sebelum menganggap F3 state vocabulary sebagai immutable canonical taxonomy:

0\. search seluruh canonical corpus untuk existing lifecycle state terminology;

0\. search PD-01 Reference Implementation;

0\. search native AIOS lifecycle/entity model;

0\. compare terminology dengan F1 dan F2;

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• menghapus historical lifecycle state tanpa authority;

• mengubah state hanya untuk menyederhanakan implementation;

• membuat state baru hanya karena workflow membutuhkan status tambahan;

• menganggap workflow status sebagai lifecycle state;

• mengambil lifecycle state Platform lain tanpa domain adaptation.

Jika ambiguity berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh:

• Constitution;

• Founder Reserved Authority;

• Canonical Architecture supremacy;

• fundamental governance boundary;

• cross-platform ownership;

Claude Code harus melakukan escalation.

40\. Success Criteria

F3 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. lifecycle state memiliki definisi yang jelas;

0\. lifecycle state dibedakan dari workflow status;

0\. lifecycle state dibedakan dari lifecycle event;

0\. lifecycle state dibedakan dari change type;

0\. setiap state memiliki semantic identity;

0\. entry condition dapat ditentukan;

0\. exit condition dapat ditentukan apabila applicable;

0\. state memiliki owner;

0\. state dapat diverifikasi;

0\. state dapat direkam;

0\. Pre-Established condition dapat direpresentasikan;

0\. Established condition dapat direpresentasikan;

0\. Transitional / Change condition dapat direpresentasikan;

0\. Retired condition dapat direpresentasikan;

0\. Dissolved condition dapat direpresentasikan;

0\. creation memiliki resulting established condition;

0\. restructuring dapat terjadi tanpa menciptakan state taxonomy baru secara otomatis;

0\. transition memiliki source dan resulting state;

0\. retirement menghasilkan Retired condition;

0\. dissolution menghasilkan Dissolved condition;

0\. historical continuity dipertahankan;

0\. authority tidak diciptakan oleh state;

0\. state ownership tidak sama dengan authority;

0\. performance evidence tidak otomatis mengubah state;

0\. operational status tidak otomatis mengubah lifecycle state;

0\. cross-platform state ownership tetap terpisah;

0\. ambiguity tidak diselesaikan dengan unsupported assumption;

0\. terminal-state semantics tidak diperluas tanpa authority;

0\. F4 memiliki clear parent semantics;

0\. source gaps ditandai secara eksplisit;

0\. tidak ada material contradiction dengan F1 atau F2;

0\. tidak ada unsupported domain expansion;

0\. tidak ada unsupported governance authority;

0\. lifecycle state taxonomy dapat direconciled oleh Claude Code tanpa merusak F1/F2.

⸻

41\. Gold Standard Review

￼

⸻

42\. Canonical Lifecycle State Statement

F3 — Lifecycle States defines the canonical architectural conditions through which a PD-03 lifecycle subject can be represented as Pre-Established, Established, Transitional / Change, Retired, or Dissolved, while preserving state identity, entry and exit semantics, ownership, authority separation, verification, traceability, historical continuity, and organizational boundary.

Lifecycle events and change categories are not lifecycle states. Creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, and dissolution describe lifecycle change; the state model describes the resulting condition of the lifecycle subject.

F3 does not create lifecycle authority or implementation workflow. Allowed state transitions, transition authorization, evidence requirements, failure handling, and transition matrix semantics are governed by F4 and applicable Governance Architecture.
