\# 1. Purpose

F3 menetapkan arsitektur lifecycle state bagi PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

F3 menerjemahkan Lifecycle Model pada F2 menjadi struktur kondisi lifecycle yang dapat:

\- diidentifikasi;

\- dibedakan;

\- dimasuki;

\- ditinggalkan;

\- diverifikasi;

\- direkam;

\- ditelusuri;

\- digunakan sebagai dasar transition governance.

F3 menjadi parent bagi semantic dan transition detail yang akan digunakan pada:

\- F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

\- F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance;

\- F6 — Lifecycle Review;

\- F7 — Lifecycle Control;

\- F8 — Lifecycle Implementation;

\- F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity;

\- F10 — Lifecycle Success.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

F3 berada di bawah:

\`\`\`text

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

F1 menetapkan:

mengapa lifecycle harus dikendalikan.

F2 menetapkan:

bagaimana lifecycle dimodelkan.

F3 menetapkan:

kondisi lifecycle apa yang harus dapat dibedakan.

F4 menetapkan:

bagaimana perpindahan antar-condition dikendalikan.

⸻

3\. Lifecycle State Definition

Lifecycle state adalah kondisi yang secara canonical merepresentasikan keadaan lifecycle suatu lifecycle subject pada titik tertentu.

State bukan sekadar:

• workflow status;

• ticket status;

• approval status;

• task status;

• project status;

• operational status.

Canonical distinction:

Workflow Status

≠

Operational Status

≠

Lifecycle State

Lifecycle state harus menggambarkan condition of the lifecycle subject, bukan hanya progress dari suatu pekerjaan.

⸻

4\. State Integrity Principle

Setiap lifecycle state harus memiliki minimum semantic integrity:

1\. identifiable;

2\. semantically distinct;

3\. defined entry condition;

4\. defined exit condition apabila applicable;

5\. accountable owner;

6\. applicable authority context;

7\. verification basis;

8\. canonical record.

Hal ini mengikuti prinsip F1 bahwa lifecycle state harus dapat dibedakan, memiliki definisi, entry/exit condition apabila berlaku, owner, dapat diverifikasi, dan dapat direkam.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. State Model

F3 menggunakan lifecycle state model berikut sebagai bounded canonical model:

┌─────────────────────┐

│ Pre-Established │

└──────────┬──────────┘

│

│ Authorized Creation

▼

┌─────────────────────┐

│ Established │

└──────────┬──────────┘

│

│ Authorized Lifecycle Change

▼

┌─────────────────────┐

│ Transitional / │

│ Change Condition │

└──────────┬──────────┘

│

│ Transition Verification

▼

┌─────────────────────┐

│ Established / │

│ Resulting Condition │

└──────────┬──────────┘

│

├───────────────┐

│ │

│ Retirement │ Dissolution

▼ ▼

┌─────────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────────┐

│ Retired │ │ Dissolved │

└─────────────────────┘ └─────────────────────┘

Catatan canonical:

Model di atas merupakan state architecture, bukan transition matrix final.

F4 menetapkan transition eligibility dan transition semantics secara rinci.

⸻

6\. State Categories

F3 mengenali lifecycle condition berikut:

State Category Meaning

Pre-Established Subject belum menjadi established lifecycle subject

Established Subject telah established dan memiliki lifecycle identity yang berlaku

Transitional / Change Condition Subject sedang berada dalam material lifecycle change yang belum menghasilkan resulting condition yang terverifikasi

Retired Subject tidak lagi berada dalam active applicable condition tetapi historical identity dan record tetap dipertahankan

Dissolved Eksistensi subject telah diakhiri sesuai authority yang berlaku

State categories ini diturunkan dari lifecycle concerns yang source-backed: creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, dan dissolution.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

7\. Pre-Established State

7.1 Definition

Pre-Established merepresentasikan kondisi ketika lifecycle subject belum menjadi established subject dalam applicable organizational architecture.

State ini memberikan boundary sebelum creation.

7.2 Purpose

State ini mencegah subject dianggap sudah established hanya karena:

• proposal dibuat;

• discussion terjadi;

• requirement muncul;

• approval preparation dimulai;

• implementation task dibuat.

7.3 Entry Condition

Pre-Established merupakan condition sebelum authorized creation menghasilkan established subject.

7.4 Exit Condition

Exit terjadi ketika creation telah:

• memperoleh applicable authority;

• memenuhi required evidence;

• menghasilkan identity;

• menghasilkan canonical record;

• diverifikasi sebagai established.

7.5 Authority

Pre-Established tidak memberikan operational authority kepada subject.

⸻

8\. Established State

8.1 Definition

Established merepresentasikan lifecycle subject yang telah sah dibentuk dan memiliki identity, scope, ownership, dan applicable governance context.

8.2 Minimum Conditions

Established state harus memiliki:

• lifecycle identity;

• defined scope;

• accountable owner;

• applicable authority context;

• organizational relationship;

• canonical record;

• verifiable condition.

8.3 Operational Relationship

Established tidak otomatis berarti:

• active execution;

• fully mature;

• fully optimized;

• permanently stable.

Established hanya berarti subject telah menjadi lifecycle subject yang valid dalam applicable architecture.

8.4 Continuity

Established subject dapat mengalami:

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• other authorized lifecycle change.

⸻

9\. Transitional / Change Condition

9.1 Definition

Transitional / Change Condition merepresentasikan kondisi ketika lifecycle subject sedang mengalami material lifecycle change dan belum mencapai resulting state yang telah diverifikasi.

State ini digunakan untuk menjaga distinction antara:

Current State

dan:

Resulting State

9.2 Purpose

State ini mencegah sistem mencatat resulting condition sebelum transition selesai.

9.3 Required Evidence

Material transition harus dapat menunjukkan:

• source condition;

• target condition;

• authorized change;

• evidence;

• transition execution;

• verification status.

F2 telah menetapkan bahwa material transition harus dapat menjawab current state, resulting state, authority, evidence, dependency, verification, dan recording.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

9.4 Constraint

Transitional / Change Condition tidak boleh digunakan sebagai permanent state.

Jika transition gagal, dibatalkan, atau memerlukan exception, resulting condition harus ditentukan melalui applicable transition governance.

⸻

10\. Resulting Established Condition

Setelah material lifecycle change selesai dan diverifikasi, lifecycle subject kembali berada pada established condition yang sesuai dengan hasil transition.

Secara konseptual:

Established

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting Established Condition

Resulting condition dapat berbeda secara material dari previous condition dalam:

• organizational structure;

• responsibility allocation;

• capability arrangement;

• team arrangement;

• reporting arrangement;

• operating configuration.

Perubahan tersebut tetap harus berada dalam applicable authority.

⸻

11\. Retired State

11.1 Definition

Retired merepresentasikan lifecycle subject yang tidak lagi berada dalam active applicable condition tetapi belum dianggap dissolved.

Retirement dan dissolution harus tetap dibedakan. F1 secara eksplisit membedakan retirement sebagai penghentian status aktif/penggunaan dari dissolution sebagai pengakhiran eksistensi subject.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

11.2 Historical Continuity

Retired subject harus mempertahankan historical traceability terhadap:

• decisions;

• evidence;

• accountability;

• governance records;

• compliance records;

• performance records;

• lifecycle history.

11.3 Operational Constraint

Retired subject tidak boleh kembali ke active operation tanpa applicable lifecycle transition.

11.4 Review

Retired state dapat memiliki:

• review date;

• retention period;

• disposition requirement;

apabila applicable.

⸻

12\. Dissolved State

12.1 Definition

Dissolved merepresentasikan pengakhiran eksistensi lifecycle subject sesuai applicable authority.

12.2 Distinction

Retired

≠

Dissolved

Retired:

subject tidak lagi aktif.

Dissolved:

eksistensi subject telah diakhiri.

12.3 Historical Record

Dissolution tidak berarti historical record dihapus.

Historical record tetap dipertahankan sesuai applicable retention and governance requirements.

12.4 Reinstatement

Dissolved subject tidak boleh dianggap kembali established hanya karena operational need muncul.

Jika re-establishment diperlukan, hal tersebut harus melalui applicable creation mechanism atau lifecycle mechanism yang secara eksplisit mengizinkannya.

⸻

13\. State vs Lifecycle Event

F3 mempertahankan distinction:

Lifecycle Event

↓

Something happens

sedangkan:

Lifecycle State

↓

Condition of the subject

Contoh:

Creation Event

↓

Established State

Retirement Event

↓

Retired State

Dissolution Event

↓

Dissolved State

Event tidak boleh secara otomatis dianggap state.

⸻

14\. State vs Change Type

Lifecycle change category juga bukan state.

Creation

Restructuring

Transition

Retirement

Dissolution

adalah change/event categories.

Sedangkan:

Pre-Established

Established

Transitional / Change

Retired

Dissolved

adalah state categories.

Canonical distinction:

Change Type

≠

Lifecycle State

⸻

15\. State Identity

Setiap lifecycle subject harus memiliki state yang dapat diidentifikasi secara canonical.

Minimum record:

Field Requirement

Lifecycle Subject Required

Current State Required

State Effective Date Required

State Owner Required

Authority Reference Required

Verification Status Required

Canonical Record Required

Previous State Required when applicable

Transition Reference Required when applicable

Historical Reference Required

Field tambahan dapat ditetapkan oleh F4–F8.

⸻

16\. State Entry Conditions

State entry harus berdasarkan condition yang dapat diverifikasi.

Entry condition tidak boleh hanya:

• verbal declaration;

• informal agreement;

• task completion;

• calendar date;

• assumption.

State harus berubah hanya ketika applicable criteria telah terpenuhi.

⸻

17\. State Exit Conditions

State exit harus memiliki defined criteria apabila exit tersebut membutuhkan governance control.

Contoh:

Established

↓

Exit only after authorized lifecycle change

Transitional / Change

↓

Exit only after transition result is determined

Retired

↓

Exit only through authorized lifecycle mechanism

Dissolved

↓

Normally terminal unless applicable architecture explicitly provides another mechanism

⸻

18\. State Verification

State verification harus menjawab:

1\. apakah state yang dicatat benar;

2\. apakah entry condition terpenuhi;

3\. apakah authority valid;

4\. apakah evidence cukup;

5\. apakah resulting condition sesuai decision;

6\. apakah canonical record telah diperbarui.

Lifecycle integrity mensyaratkan state benar, authority valid, evidence tersedia, record dapat ditelusuri, dan resulting state konsisten dengan decision.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

19\. State Ownership

State ownership harus dibedakan dari authority.

Authority

↓

Right to authorize

Accountability

↓

Responsibility for outcome

State Ownership

↓

Responsibility for lifecycle condition

Execution

↓

Performs authorized change

F1 secara eksplisit mempertahankan distinction:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

20\. State Authority

Lifecycle state tidak memberikan authority hanya karena subject berada dalam state tertentu.

Contoh:

Established

≠

Unlimited Authority

dan:

Retired

≠

No Historical Responsibility

State hanya menggambarkan condition.

Authority tetap berasal dari Governance Architecture dan applicable authority model.

⸻

21\. State Materiality

Tidak semua state change memiliki materiality yang sama.

Materiality dapat dipengaruhi oleh:

• structural impact;

• authority impact;

• ownership impact;

• dependency impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• risk;

• reversibility.

Control intensity harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

22\. State Transition Boundary

F3 hanya menetapkan state model.

F3 tidak menetapkan final transition matrix.

Relationship:

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

Defines Valid State Semantics

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

Defines Allowed State Changes

F4 harus menetapkan:

• allowed source state;

• allowed target state;

• trigger;

• authority;

• evidence;

• verification;

• failure handling;

• rollback/recovery apabila applicable.

⸻

23\. State Transition Integrity

Tidak boleh terdapat state transition yang:

• melewati required governance;

• tidak memiliki authority;

• tidak memiliki evidence ketika evidence required;

• tidak memiliki resulting-state verification;

• menghapus historical continuity;

• melanggar organizational boundary;

• mengambil alih ownership Platform lain.

⸻

24\. Restructuring and State

Restructuring tidak otomatis membutuhkan state baru.

Model default:

Established

↓

Restructuring

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Established — Resulting Condition

State tetap dapat menjadi Established meskipun internal organizational configuration berubah.

Ini mencegah setiap restructuring menciptakan state taxonomy baru.

⸻

25\. Transition and State

Transition adalah mekanisme perpindahan antar lifecycle conditions.

Contoh konseptual:

Established

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Established — Resulting Condition

Transition semantics final berada pada F4.

⸻

26\. Retirement and State

Retirement mengikuti pola:

Established

↓

Retirement Decision

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Retired

Retirement harus mempertahankan historical continuity.

⸻

27\. Dissolution and State

Dissolution mengikuti pola:

Established

↓

Dissolution Decision

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Dissolved

Dissolution harus memiliki applicable authority dan canonical record.

⸻

28\. Creation and State

Creation mengikuti pola:

Pre-Established

↓

Creation Decision

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Verification

↓

Established

Creation tidak dianggap selesai hanya karena authorization telah diberikan.

Resulting established condition harus diverifikasi dan direkam.

⸻

29\. State Continuity

Lifecycle state change harus mempertahankan continuity:

Subject Identity

↓

Previous State

↓

Lifecycle Event

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Resulting State

Historical identity tidak boleh hilang karena state berubah.

⸻

30\. State Record

Canonical state record harus memungkinkan reconstruction atas lifecycle condition pada titik waktu tertentu.

Minimum:

Subject

State

Effective Time

Authority

Decision

Transition

Verification

History

Record harus dapat mendukung audit, governance review, compliance review, dan architecture review apabila applicable.

⸻

31\. Cross-Platform State Boundary

PD-03 lifecycle state tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyatakan lifecycle state Platform lain.

Contoh:

PD-03 State

≠

PD-02 State

≠

PD-08 State

Cross-platform dependency dapat dicatat sebagai relationship atau impact.

Namun state ownership tetap berada pada applicable Platform.

⸻

32\. Performance Relationship

Performance evidence dapat memengaruhi lifecycle assessment, tetapi tidak otomatis mengubah lifecycle state.

Performance Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Governance Decision

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

State Change

Dengan demikian:

Performance Result

≠

Automatic State Transition

Ini konsisten dengan F1/F2 bahwa performance evidence merupakan input, sedangkan lifecycle authority tetap berada pada applicable governance mechanism.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

33\. Operational Relationship

Operating state dan lifecycle state harus tetap dibedakan.

Lifecycle State

↓

Defines lifecycle condition

Operating State

↓

Defines current operational condition

PD-03 tidak boleh mengubah lifecycle state hanya karena operational state berubah tanpa material lifecycle basis.

⸻

34\. State Exceptions

Jika state condition tidak dapat ditentukan secara normal:

1\. ambiguity harus dicatat;

2\. current known state harus dipertahankan apabila valid;

3\. exception harus diklasifikasikan;

4\. authority harus ditentukan;

5\. evidence harus dikumpulkan;

6\. resulting state harus ditetapkan melalui applicable governance.

Tidak boleh mengisi state dengan tebakan hanya untuk menghilangkan ambiguity.

⸻

35\. State Ambiguity Rule

Jika terdapat dua atau lebih candidate states dan evidence tidak cukup untuk membedakannya:

Candidate State A

-

Candidate State B

↓

Insufficient Evidence

↓

DO NOT AUTO-SELECT

System harus:

• preserve known state;

• flag ambiguity;

• request assessment;

• escalate apabila material.

Ini mencegah lifecycle record menjadi false precision.

⸻

36\. Terminal State Principle

Dissolved diperlakukan sebagai terminal state secara default.

Namun F3 tidak mengklaim bahwa seluruh AIOS lifecycle subject secara universal tidak dapat dire-establish.

Jika architecture membutuhkan re-establishment:

• mekanisme harus eksplisit;

• authority harus jelas;

• identity continuity harus ditentukan;

• historical relation harus dipertahankan;

• keputusan harus terdokumentasi.

⸻

37\. State Taxonomy Boundary

F3 does define:

• state concept;

• state categories;

• state semantics;

• state entry/exit principles;

• state identity;

• state ownership distinction;

• state verification;

• state continuity;

• state recording;

• relationship between state and event;

• relationship between state and change type.

F3 does not define:

• role-by-role state authority;

• final transition matrix;

• detailed workflow;

• implementation technology;

• lifecycle engine;

• numeric maturity score;

• lifecycle KPI;

• platform-wide universal lifecycle taxonomy.

⸻

38\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Source baseline secara eksplisit mendukung bahwa lifecycle subject harus memiliki identifiable state dengan:

• definisi;

• entry condition;

• exit condition apabila berlaku;

• owner;

• verification;

• record.

Source juga mendukung lifecycle categories:

• creation;

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• dissolution.

Namun source baseline yang tersedia tidak memberikan definitive literal state catalogue untuk PD-03.

Karena itu F3 menggunakan:

Bounded Canonical Synthesis

State categories yang diperkenalkan di section ini harus diperlakukan sebagai architectural model derived from the frozen F1/F2 boundaries, bukan sebagai klaim bahwa vocabulary tersebut telah ditemukan secara literal dalam historical source.

⸻

39\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Claude Code harus melakukan reconciliation berikut sebelum menganggap F3 state vocabulary sebagai immutable canonical taxonomy:

1\. search seluruh canonical corpus untuk existing lifecycle state terminology;

2\. search PD-01 Reference Implementation;

3\. search native AIOS lifecycle/entity model;

4\. search Architecture Decision Records yang berkaitan dengan lifecycle;

5\. search existing state-machine atau lifecycle implementation;

6\. compare terminology dengan F1 dan F2;

7\. identify semantic collisions;

8\. preserve source-backed terminology apabila ditemukan;

9\. replace bounded terminology apabila source yang lebih authoritative ditemukan;

10\. document any taxonomy change;

11\. create ADR apabila perubahan taxonomy bersifat material;

12\. update F4 transition matrix setelah state taxonomy final.

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• menghapus historical lifecycle state tanpa authority;

• mengubah state hanya untuk menyederhanakan implementation;

• membuat state baru hanya karena workflow membutuhkan status tambahan;

• menganggap workflow status sebagai lifecycle state;

• mengambil lifecycle state Platform lain tanpa domain adaptation.

Jika ambiguity berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh:

• Constitution;

• Founder Reserved Authority;

• Canonical Architecture supremacy;

• fundamental governance boundary;

• cross-platform ownership;

Claude Code harus melakukan escalation.

⸻

40\. Success Criteria

F3 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. lifecycle state memiliki definisi yang jelas;

2\. lifecycle state dibedakan dari workflow status;

3\. lifecycle state dibedakan dari lifecycle event;

4\. lifecycle state dibedakan dari change type;

5\. setiap state memiliki semantic identity;

6\. entry condition dapat ditentukan;

7\. exit condition dapat ditentukan apabila applicable;

8\. state memiliki owner;

9\. state dapat diverifikasi;

10\. state dapat direkam;

11\. Pre-Established condition dapat direpresentasikan;

12\. Established condition dapat direpresentasikan;

13\. Transitional / Change condition dapat direpresentasikan;

14\. Retired condition dapat direpresentasikan;

15\. Dissolved condition dapat direpresentasikan;

16\. creation memiliki resulting established condition;

17\. restructuring dapat terjadi tanpa menciptakan state taxonomy baru secara otomatis;

18\. transition memiliki source dan resulting state;

19\. retirement menghasilkan Retired condition;

20\. dissolution menghasilkan Dissolved condition;

21\. historical continuity dipertahankan;

22\. authority tidak diciptakan oleh state;

23\. state ownership tidak sama dengan authority;

24\. performance evidence tidak otomatis mengubah state;

25\. operational status tidak otomatis mengubah lifecycle state;

26\. cross-platform state ownership tetap terpisah;

27\. ambiguity tidak diselesaikan dengan unsupported assumption;

28\. terminal-state semantics tidak diperluas tanpa authority;

29\. F4 memiliki clear parent semantics;

30\. source gaps ditandai secara eksplisit;

31\. tidak ada material contradiction dengan F1 atau F2;

32\. tidak ada unsupported domain expansion;

33\. tidak ada unsupported governance authority;

34\. lifecycle state taxonomy dapat direconciled oleh Claude Code tanpa merusak F1/F2.

42\. Canonical Lifecycle State Statement

F3 — Lifecycle States defines the canonical architectural conditions through which a PD-03 lifecycle subject can be represented as Pre-Established, Established, Transitional / Change, Retired, or Dissolved, while preserving state identity, entry and exit semantics, ownership, authority separation, verification, traceability, historical continuity, and organizational boundary.

Lifecycle events and change categories are not lifecycle states. Creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, and dissolution describe lifecycle change; the state model describes the resulting condition of the lifecycle subject.

F3 does not create lifecycle authority or implementation workflow. Allowed state transitions, transition authorization, evidence requirements, failure handling, and transition matrix semantics are governed by F4 and applicable Governance Architecture.

44\. Canonical Status

F3 — Lifecycle States is FROZEN.

Freeze berlaku terhadap:

• state architecture;

• state semantics;

• state boundary;

• state/event distinction;

• state/change distinction;

• state integrity principles.

Freeze tidak menganggap transition matrix telah final.

Construction frontier berikutnya:

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

Allowed Transition Matrix

↓

Authority / Evidence / Verification

F4 menjadi titik berikutnya untuk menguji apakah seluruh state yang telah dimodelkan benar-benar memiliki transition semantics yang konsisten dengan Governance Architecture.

\### Gold Standard verdict

\*\*F3: \`FROZEN — BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS\`.\*\*

\---

\# 1. Purpose

F4 menetapkan arsitektur canonical untuk perpindahan lifecycle condition suatu lifecycle subject.

F4 menerjemahkan state architecture pada F3 menjadi mekanisme transition yang dapat:

\- dimulai;

\- dinilai;

\- diotorisasi;

\- dieksekusi;

\- diverifikasi;

\- direkam;

\- ditelusuri;

\- ditutup.

F4 tidak sekadar mendefinisikan perubahan status.

F4 memastikan bahwa material lifecycle transition memiliki:

\- source state;

\- target/resulting state;

\- rationale;

\- authority;

\- evidence;

\- dependency assessment;

\- execution;

\- verification;

\- canonical record.

Prinsip tersebut secara eksplisit ditetapkan oleh baseline PD-03. Performance Architecture Review.txt

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

F4 berada dalam chain:

\`\`\`text

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

Relationship:

F3

Defines State

↓

F4

Defines Movement Between States

↓

F5

Defines Governance of Lifecycle Change

F4 tidak boleh mengubah constitutional boundary yang telah ditetapkan F1.

⸻

3\. Transition Definition

Lifecycle transition adalah perubahan yang terkendali dari satu lifecycle condition menuju resulting lifecycle condition.

Secara canonical:

Source State

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

Resulting State

Transition bukan sekadar:

• status update;

• administrative update;

• workflow completion;

• approval flag;

• task closure.

Transition harus merepresentasikan perubahan lifecycle condition yang dapat dibuktikan.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Transition Principle

No material lifecycle transition shall be recognized as complete until its source condition, authority, transition execution, verification, and resulting condition are identifiable and traceable.

Transition harus mempertahankan:

• lifecycle integrity;

• authority integrity;

• organizational ownership;

• evidence integrity;

• historical continuity;

• resulting-state correctness.

⸻

5\. Canonical Transition Flow

Baseline menetapkan transition flow berikut:

Current State

↓

Transition Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

Performance Architecture Review.txt

F4 memperlakukan flow tersebut sebagai minimum lifecycle transition pattern.

⸻

6\. Transition Stages

6.1 Current State Identification

Identifikasi kondisi lifecycle sebelum transition.

Minimum:

• lifecycle subject;

• current state;

• effective state;

• current owner;

• current authority context;

• relevant record.

Current state tidak boleh diasumsikan.

⸻

6.2 Transition Assessment

Transition harus dinilai sebelum authorization.

Assessment sekurang-kurangnya mempertimbangkan:

• reason;

• target condition;

• materiality;

• impact;

• dependencies;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• organizational effect;

• cross-platform effect;

• evidence requirement.

⸻

6.3 Authorization

Transition material membutuhkan applicable authority.

Model:

Transition Matter

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Decision / Authorization

↓

Authorized Transition

Lifecycle architecture tidak menciptakan authority baru.

Authority berasal dari applicable Governance Architecture, governance charter, mandate, delegation, atau higher-level constitutional authority.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

6.4 Transition Execution

Execution adalah pelaksanaan authorized change.

Execution harus:

• mengikuti authorized scope;

• menjaga boundary;

• menghasilkan evidence;

• mempertahankan traceability;

• tidak mengubah target secara informal.

Execution authority dibedakan dari decision authority:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

6.5 Verification

Verification menentukan apakah transition benar-benar menghasilkan kondisi yang diotorisasi.

Verification harus memeriksa:

• execution completeness;

• resulting condition;

• evidence;

• authority consistency;

• dependency outcome;

• organizational impact;

• canonical record.

⸻

6.6 Resulting State

Transition dianggap selesai hanya ketika resulting state telah ditentukan dan diverifikasi.

Execution Complete

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State Confirmed

Resulting state tidak boleh hanya disimpulkan dari:

• execution completion;

• approval timestamp;

• task closure.

⸻

7\. Transition Identity

Setiap material transition harus dapat diidentifikasi secara canonical.

Minimum:

Field Requirement

Transition ID Required

Lifecycle Subject Required

Source State Required

Target State Required

Change Type Required

Rationale Required

Authority Reference Required

Accountability Owner Required

Evidence Reference Required

Dependency Assessment Required when applicable

Execution Record Required

Verification Result Required

Resulting State Required

Effective Date Required

Historical Reference Required

⸻

8\. Source State Integrity

Source state harus:

1\. identifiable;

2\. current;

3\. valid;

4\. recorded;

5\. owned;

6\. verifiable.

Transition tidak boleh dimulai berdasarkan state yang ambiguous tanpa explicit exception handling.

⸻

9\. Target State Integrity

Target state harus:

• identifiable;

• semantically valid;

• within lifecycle model;

• compatible with applicable architecture;

• within authority scope;

• capable of verification.

Target state tidak boleh diciptakan hanya karena workflow membutuhkan status baru.

⸻

10\. Transition Eligibility

Tidak setiap source state dapat berpindah ke setiap target state.

Secara canonical:

Source State

↓

Eligibility Assessment

↓

Allowed / Not Allowed

Eligibility harus mempertimbangkan:

• state semantics;

• applicable lifecycle rule;

• authority;

• transition type;

• materiality;

• dependencies;

• constraints.

Final literal transition matrix belum source-backed dalam baseline yang tersedia.

Karena itu F4 menetapkan prinsip eligibility, bukan mengklaim seluruh allowed edges sebagai historical canonical fact.

⸻

11\. Transition Matrix Boundary

F4 menggunakan conceptual transition matrix:

Source Target Status

Pre-Established Established Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Established Transitional / Change Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Transitional / Change Established Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Established Retired Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Transitional / Change Retired Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Established Dissolved Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Transitional / Change Dissolved Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Retired Established Not canonical without explicit authority/source

Retired Dissolved Requires applicable lifecycle mechanism

Dissolved Any active state Not canonical by default

Tabel tersebut bukan klaim source literal.

Ia merupakan working architecture derived from F3 state semantics dan F1/F2 lifecycle principles.

Claude Code wajib mengganti atau mengkonfirmasi edge tersebut berdasarkan canonical corpus sebelum final implementation.

⸻

12\. Transition Type

F4 membedakan transition dari change category.

Change category:

Creation

Restructuring

Transition

Retirement

Dissolution

Transition mechanism:

Source State

↓

Transition

↓

Target State

Dengan demikian:

Change Type

≠

Transition State Movement

Creation dapat mengikuti pola:

Pre-Established

↓

Creation Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Creation Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Creation transition harus menghasilkan:

• identity;

• scope;

• owner;

• authority context;

• canonical record.

⸻

14\. Restructuring Transition

Restructuring tidak otomatis berarti dissolution.

Default pattern:

Established

↓

Restructuring Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Established — Resulting Condition

Resulting condition dapat memiliki organizational configuration yang berbeda.

⸻

15\. Standard Lifecycle Transition

Untuk material change yang tidak mengubah lifecycle category:

Established

↓

Transition Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Established — Resulting Condition

16\. Retirement Transition

Retirement dapat mengikuti:

Established

↓

Retirement Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Retirement tidak menghapus historical continuity.

⸻

17\. Dissolution Transition

Dissolution dapat mengikuti:

Established

↓

Dissolution Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Dissolved

Dissolution harus memiliki authority yang sesuai.

⸻

18\. Re-entry / Re-establishment

Re-entry dari Retired atau Dissolved tidak boleh diasumsikan.

Default rule:

Retired

≠

Automatically Re-established

Dissolved

≠

Automatically Re-established

Jika re-establishment diperlukan, mechanism tersebut harus:

• explicit;

• authorized;

• recorded;

• identity-safe;

• historically traceable.

⸻

19\. Transition Materiality

Transition intensity harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Materiality dapat dipengaruhi oleh:

• organizational impact;

• authority impact;

• dependency impact;

• compliance impact;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• cross-platform effect;

• scope.

Model:

Low Materiality

↓

Lightweight Transition Control

High Materiality

↓

Enhanced Assessment

\+ Review

\+ Evidence

\+ Authorization

\+ Verification

⸻

20\. Transition Evidence

Evidence harus proporsional terhadap transition.

Possible evidence:

• current state evidence;

• target state definition;

• rationale;

• dependency analysis;

• organizational assessment;

• governance assessment;

• performance evidence;

• implementation readiness;

• execution evidence;

• verification evidence.

Baseline secara eksplisit menyatakan bahwa evidence harus proporsional terhadap materiality, impact, organizational scope, dependency, risk, reversibility, dan cross-platform effect.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

21\. Transition Dependency Assessment

Material transition harus menilai dependency yang terdampak.

Dependency dapat meliputi:

• organizational dependency;

• governance dependency;

• architecture dependency;

• operational dependency;

• performance dependency;

• cross-platform dependency.

Dependency assessment tidak memindahkan ownership.

⸻

22\. Transition Authority

Authority harus menjawab:

Who may authorize this transition?

Authority tidak boleh disimpulkan hanya dari:

• organizational seniority;

• execution role;

• technical role;

• ownership of implementation.

Authority mengikuti applicable authority model.

⸻

23\. Transition Accountability

Accountable owner bertanggung jawab terhadap outcome transition.

Accountability tidak sama dengan:

• authorizing authority;

• execution role;

• verification role.

Jika segregation diperlukan, transition harus menjaga separation of duties.

⸻

24\. Transition Execution Boundary

Execution harus tetap berada pada applicable owner.

PD-03 tidak memperoleh operational execution ownership atas Platform lain hanya karena transition memiliki governance/compliance impact.

Pattern:

Governance Decision

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

Applicable Domain Owner

↓

Execution

↓

Evidence

↓

PD-03 Governance / Compliance Assessment

⸻

25\. Verification Independence

Jika materiality atau risk memerlukannya, verification harus memiliki independence yang memadai dari execution.

Minimum principle:

Execution

≠

Verification

Independence level harus proporsional terhadap risk dan materiality.

⸻

26\. Transition Failure

Transition dapat gagal karena:

• authorization invalid;

• evidence insufficient;

• execution incomplete;

• dependency failure;

• resulting condition invalid;

• verification failure;

• boundary violation.

Failure tidak boleh ditutupi dengan memaksa resulting state.

⸻

27\. Failed Transition

Jika transition gagal:

Transition Execution

↓

Verification Failure

↓

Transition = NOT COMPLETE

System harus mempertahankan:

• last verified state;

• transition record;

• failure evidence;

• owner;

• disposition.

⸻

28\. Partial Transition

Jika transition hanya selesai sebagian:

Partial Execution

≠

Completed Transition

Current verified state harus tetap dapat diketahui.

Partial state tidak boleh dipromosikan menjadi canonical resulting state tanpa verification.

⸻

29\. Transition Exception

Jika normal transition mechanism tidak dapat digunakan:

1\. exception harus diidentifikasi;

2\. rationale harus dicatat;

3\. authority harus ditentukan;

4\. mitigation harus tersedia;

5\. owner harus ditetapkan;

6\. disposition harus ditentukan;

7\. material exception harus direview.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi shortcut untuk menghindari authorization.

⸻

30\. Transition Reversibility

Jika transition reversible:

Transition

↓

Reversal Mechanism

Jika irreversible:

• irreversibility harus diketahui sebelum authorization;

• consequence harus dinilai;

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• verification harus lebih ketat apabila material.

Reversibility tidak selalu required.

⸻

31\. Transition Traceability

Material transition harus dapat direconstruct:

Lifecycle Matter

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Execution

↓

Evidence

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Canonical Record

Baseline menetapkan traceability chain tersebut sebagai lifecycle requirement.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

32\. Transition Historical Continuity

Transition tidak boleh menghapus:

• previous state;

• previous decision;

• previous authority;

• previous evidence;

• previous accountability;

• previous lifecycle record.

Historical continuity harus dipertahankan sesuai applicable retention and governance requirements.

Part D bertanggung jawab terhadap operation setelah resulting state berlaku.

F4 bertanggung jawab terhadap controlled movement menuju resulting state.

36\. Transition and Performance

Performance evidence dapat menjadi input transition assessment.

Performance Evidence

↓

Transition Assessment

↓

Governance Decision

Namun:

Performance evidence does not automatically authorize lifecycle transition.

Authority tetap berada pada applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

37\. Cross-Platform Transition

Jika transition berdampak lintas Platform:

1\. impact harus diidentifikasi;

2\. affected domains harus diketahui;

3\. applicable owners harus dilibatkan;

4\. authority boundary harus dipertahankan;

5\. evidence harus tersedia;

6\. resulting state harus diverifikasi.

Cross-platform impact tidak menciptakan cross-platform ownership.

⸻

39\. Transition Closure

Transition hanya boleh ditutup apabila:

• authorized scope selesai;

• execution selesai;

• verification selesai;

• resulting state confirmed;

• record lengkap;

• unresolved exception ditangani atau explicitly accepted.

Closure:

Execution Complete

↓

Verification Complete

↓

Resulting State Confirmed

↓

Record Complete

↓

Transition Closed

40\. Transition Status

F4 menggunakan status konseptual berikut:

Proposed

↓

Assessed

↓

Authorized

↓

↓

↓

Exception states:

Blocked

Rejected

Cancelled

Failed

Escalated

Catatan source-fidelity: vocabulary status di atas merupakan bounded synthesis untuk transition record/workflow dan bukan klaim bahwa baseline PD-03 menetapkan literal status taxonomy tersebut.

Status lifecycle subject tetap dibedakan dari transition process status.

⸻

41\. Transition vs Lifecycle State

Canonical distinction:

Lifecycle State

↓

Condition of Subject

Transition Status

↓

Condition of Transition Process

Contoh:

Subject:

Established

Transition:

In Progress

Subject tidak otomatis menjadi:

Lifecycle State = In Progress

In Progress adalah transition-process condition.

⸻

42\. Transition vs Workflow

Workflow dapat membantu execution.

Tetapi:

Workflow Completion

≠

Lifecycle Transition Completion

Lifecycle transition completion memerlukan verification dan resulting-state confirmation.

⸻

43\. Transition Integrity Rules

F4 menetapkan aturan:

1\. Jangan mengubah state tanpa valid transition basis.

2\. Jangan authorize transition tanpa applicable authority.

3\. Jangan menutup transition tanpa verification.

4\. Jangan menyatakan resulting state tanpa evidence yang memadai.

5\. Jangan menghapus previous state.

6\. Jangan mengubah authority melalui transition.

7\. Jangan menggunakan transition untuk memperluas domain.

8\. Jangan mengambil ownership Platform lain melalui transition.

9\. Jangan menggunakan workflow status sebagai lifecycle state.

10\. Jangan menyamakan execution completion dengan transition completion.

⸻

44\. Transition Governance Escalation

Transition harus dieskalasikan apabila:

• authority ambiguous;

• material boundary conflict;

• cross-platform ownership conflict;

• resulting state disputed;

• evidence insufficient untuk material transition;

• constitutional impact detected;

• transition irreversibility memiliki unresolved risk;

• delegated authority tidak cukup.

Escalation mengikuti Part C dan applicable governance.

⸻

45\. Transition Boundary

F4 defines:

• transition concept;

• transition lifecycle;

• transition assessment;

• authorization;

• execution boundary;

• verification;

• resulting state;

• eligibility principle;

• transition integrity;

• failure handling;

• exception handling;

• traceability;

• transition record;

• transition closure.

F4 does not define:

• new authority;

• organizational ownership;

• technical implementation mechanism;

• universal AIOS lifecycle engine;

• final historical transition matrix unless source-backed;

• Platform-independent lifecycle vocabulary.

⸻

46\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline secara eksplisit mendukung:

• material transition harus memiliki current state;

• resulting state harus dapat diidentifikasi;

• transition membutuhkan assessment;

• applicable authority harus ada;

• evidence harus proporsional;

• dependency harus dipertimbangkan;

• transition harus dieksekusi secara terkendali;

• resulting state harus diverifikasi;

• transition harus direkam;

• lifecycle traceability harus dipertahankan.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menyatakan bahwa detailed eligibility dan transition semantics berada pada F2–F4.

Namun:

Definitive literal transition matrix PD-03 tidak ditemukan dalam available baseline.

Karena itu transition edges dan process status vocabulary yang lebih detail pada F4 diperlakukan sebagai Bounded Canonical Synthesis, bukan historical source fact.

47\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Sebelum menganggap transition matrix F4 immutable, Claude Code wajib:

1\. search seluruh canonical corpus untuk lifecycle transition terminology;

2\. search PD-03 baseline;

3\. search F1, F2, dan F3 final versions;

4\. search Architecture Decision Records terkait lifecycle;

5\. search native AIOS lifecycle/entity transition patterns;

6\. membedakan native entity lifecycle dari organizational lifecycle PD-03;

7\. reconcile seluruh candidate source/target edges;

8\. mempertahankan source-backed terminology apabila ditemukan;

9\. menghapus atau mengganti bounded synthesis yang bertentangan dengan higher-authority source;

10\. menetapkan final transition matrix;

11\. mendokumentasikan material semantic choice;

12\. membuat ADR apabila transition taxonomy berubah secara material.

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• membuat transition edge hanya karena workflow memerlukannya;

• menganggap semua state dapat berpindah ke semua state;

• menganggap Retired otomatis dapat kembali Established;

• menganggap Dissolved dapat otomatis kembali active;

• menghapus historical state;

• membuat authority baru melalui transition;

• mengubah ownership Platform lain;

• mengubah constitutional boundary tanpa applicable authority.

Jika ambiguity berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh Constitution, Founder Reserved Authority, Canonical Architecture supremacy, atau fundamental governance boundary, Claude Code wajib melakukan escalation.

48\. Success Criteria

F4 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. transition memiliki definisi canonical;

2\. source state dapat diidentifikasi;

3\. target/resulting state dapat diidentifikasi;

4\. transition assessment tersedia;

5\. applicable authority dapat diidentifikasi;

6\. rationale dapat ditelusuri;

7\. evidence requirement dapat ditentukan;

8\. dependency impact dapat dinilai;

9\. execution dapat dibedakan dari authorization;

10\. verification dapat dibedakan dari execution;

11\. resulting state tidak ditetapkan sebelum verification;

12\. material transition memiliki canonical record;

13\. transition dapat direconstruct;

14\. historical continuity dipertahankan;

15\. transition failure dapat ditangani;

16\. partial transition tidak dipromosikan sebagai complete;

17\. exception memiliki governance;

18\. irreversible transition mendapatkan appropriate scrutiny;

19\. creation transition tercakup;

20\. restructuring transition tercakup;

21\. retirement transition tercakup;

22\. dissolution transition tercakup;

23\. cross-platform transition menjaga ownership boundary;

24\. performance evidence tidak otomatis menjadi authority;

25\. workflow status tidak disamakan dengan lifecycle state;

26\. transition closure membutuhkan verification;

27\. tidak ada unsupported authority;

28\. tidak ada domain expansion;

29\. unresolved source gap ditandai;

30\. final transition matrix dapat direconciled oleh Claude Code.

50\. Canonical Transition Statement

F4 — Lifecycle Transition establishes the controlled mechanism through which a PD-03 lifecycle subject may move from an identifiable current condition to an authorized and verified resulting condition through assessment, applicable authorization, controlled execution, evidence, verification, and canonical recording while preserving governance authority, organizational ownership, lifecycle integrity, historical continuity, and cross-platform boundaries.

A lifecycle transition is not complete merely because execution has finished. Completion requires verification of the resulting condition and canonical recording of the transition outcome.

F4 does not create lifecycle authority or organizational ownership. Authority remains governed by Part C and applicable higher-order governance. Detailed transition eligibility and final transition matrix remain subject to source reconciliation where the available baseline does not provide literal canonical edges.

\# 1. Purpose

F4 menetapkan arsitektur canonical untuk perpindahan lifecycle condition suatu lifecycle subject.

F4 menerjemahkan state architecture pada F3 menjadi mekanisme transition yang dapat:

\- dimulai;

\- dinilai;

\- diotorisasi;

\- dieksekusi;

\- diverifikasi;

\- direkam;

\- ditelusuri;

\- ditutup.

F4 tidak sekadar mendefinisikan perubahan status.

F4 memastikan bahwa material lifecycle transition memiliki:

\- source state;

\- target/resulting state;

\- rationale;

\- authority;

\- evidence;

\- dependency assessment;

\- execution;

\- verification;

\- canonical record.

Prinsip tersebut secara eksplisit ditetapkan oleh baseline PD-03. Performance Architecture Review.txt

\---

⸻

3\. Transition Definition

Lifecycle transition adalah perubahan yang terkendali dari satu lifecycle condition menuju resulting lifecycle condition.

Secara canonical:

Source State

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

Resulting State

Transition bukan sekadar:

• status update;

• administrative update;

• workflow completion;

• approval flag;

• task closure.

Transition harus merepresentasikan perubahan lifecycle condition yang dapat dibuktikan.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Transition Principle

No material lifecycle transition shall be recognized as complete until its source condition, authority, transition execution, verification, and resulting condition are identifiable and traceable.

Transition harus mempertahankan:

• lifecycle integrity;

• authority integrity;

• organizational ownership;

• evidence integrity;

• historical continuity;

• resulting-state correctness.

⸻

5\. Canonical Transition Flow

Baseline menetapkan transition flow berikut:

Current State

↓

Transition Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

Performance Architecture Review.txt

F4 memperlakukan flow tersebut sebagai minimum lifecycle transition pattern.

⸻

6\. Transition Stages

6.1 Current State Identification

Identifikasi kondisi lifecycle sebelum transition.

Minimum:

• lifecycle subject;

• current state;

• effective state;

• current owner;

• current authority context;

• relevant record.

Current state tidak boleh diasumsikan.

⸻

6.2 Transition Assessment

Transition harus dinilai sebelum authorization.

Assessment sekurang-kurangnya mempertimbangkan:

• reason;

• target condition;

• materiality;

• impact;

• dependencies;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• organizational effect;

• cross-platform effect;

• evidence requirement.

⸻

6.3 Authorization

Transition material membutuhkan applicable authority.

Model:

Transition Matter

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Decision / Authorization

↓

Authorized Transition

Lifecycle architecture tidak menciptakan authority baru.

Authority berasal dari applicable Governance Architecture, governance charter, mandate, delegation, atau higher-level constitutional authority.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

6.4 Transition Execution

Execution adalah pelaksanaan authorized change.

Execution harus:

• mengikuti authorized scope;

• menjaga boundary;

• menghasilkan evidence;

• mempertahankan traceability;

• tidak mengubah target secara informal.

Execution authority dibedakan dari decision authority:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

6.5 Verification

Verification menentukan apakah transition benar-benar menghasilkan kondisi yang diotorisasi.

Verification harus memeriksa:

• execution completeness;

• resulting condition;

• evidence;

• authority consistency;

• dependency outcome;

• organizational impact;

• canonical record.

⸻

6.6 Resulting State

Transition dianggap selesai hanya ketika resulting state telah ditentukan dan diverifikasi.

Execution Complete

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State Confirmed

Resulting state tidak boleh hanya disimpulkan dari:

• execution completion;

• approval timestamp;

• task closure.

⸻

7\. Transition Identity

Setiap material transition harus dapat diidentifikasi secara canonical.

Minimum:

Field Requirement

Transition ID Required

Lifecycle Subject Required

Source State Required

Target State Required

Change Type Required

Rationale Required

Authority Reference Required

Accountability Owner Required

Evidence Reference Required

Dependency Assessment Required when applicable

Execution Record Required

Verification Result Required

Resulting State Required

Effective Date Required

Historical Reference Required

⸻

8\. Source State Integrity

Source state harus:

1\. identifiable;

2\. current;

3\. valid;

4\. recorded;

5\. owned;

6\. verifiable.

Transition tidak boleh dimulai berdasarkan state yang ambiguous tanpa explicit exception handling.

⸻

9\. Target State Integrity

Target state harus:

• identifiable;

• semantically valid;

• within lifecycle model;

• compatible with applicable architecture;

• within authority scope;

• capable of verification.

Target state tidak boleh diciptakan hanya karena workflow membutuhkan status baru.

⸻

10\. Transition Eligibility

Tidak setiap source state dapat berpindah ke setiap target state.

Secara canonical:

Source State

↓

Eligibility Assessment

↓

Allowed / Not Allowed

Eligibility harus mempertimbangkan:

• state semantics;

• applicable lifecycle rule;

• authority;

• transition type;

• materiality;

• dependencies;

• constraints.

Final literal transition matrix belum source-backed dalam baseline yang tersedia.

Karena itu F4 menetapkan prinsip eligibility, bukan mengklaim seluruh allowed edges sebagai historical canonical fact.

⸻

11\. Transition Matrix Boundary

F4 menggunakan conceptual transition matrix:

Source Target Status

Pre-Established Established Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Established Transitional / Change Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Transitional / Change Established Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Established Retired Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Transitional / Change Retired Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Established Dissolved Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Transitional / Change Dissolved Candidate / requires source reconciliation

Retired Established Not canonical without explicit authority/source

Retired Dissolved Requires applicable lifecycle mechanism

Dissolved Any active state Not canonical by default

Tabel tersebut bukan klaim source literal.

Ia merupakan working architecture derived from F3 state semantics dan F1/F2 lifecycle principles.

Claude Code wajib mengganti atau mengkonfirmasi edge tersebut berdasarkan canonical corpus sebelum final implementation.

⸻

12\. Transition Type

F4 membedakan transition dari change category.

Change category:

Creation

Restructuring

Transition

Retirement

Dissolution

Transition mechanism:

Source State

↓

Transition

↓

Target State

Dengan demikian:

Change Type

≠

Transition State Movement

Creation dapat mengikuti pola:

Pre-Established

↓

Creation Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Creation Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Established

Creation transition harus menghasilkan:

• identity;

• scope;

• owner;

• authority context;

• canonical record.

⸻

14\. Restructuring Transition

Restructuring tidak otomatis berarti dissolution.

Default pattern:

Established

↓

Restructuring Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Established — Resulting Condition

Resulting condition dapat memiliki organizational configuration yang berbeda.

⸻

15\. Standard Lifecycle Transition

Untuk material change yang tidak mengubah lifecycle category:

Established

↓

Transition Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transitional / Change

↓

Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Established — Resulting Condition

⸻

16\. Retirement Transition

Retirement dapat mengikuti:

Established

↓

Retirement Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Retired

Retirement tidak menghapus historical continuity.

⸻

17\. Dissolution Transition

Dissolution dapat mengikuti:

Established

↓

Dissolution Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Transition Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Dissolved

Dissolution harus memiliki authority yang sesuai.

⸻

18\. Re-entry / Re-establishment

Re-entry dari Retired atau Dissolved tidak boleh diasumsikan.

Default rule:

Retired

≠

Automatically Re-established

Dissolved

≠

Automatically Re-established

Jika re-establishment diperlukan, mechanism tersebut harus:

• explicit;

• authorized;

• recorded;

• identity-safe;

• historically traceable.

⸻

19\. Transition Materiality

Transition intensity harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Materiality dapat dipengaruhi oleh:

• organizational impact;

• authority impact;

• dependency impact;

• compliance impact;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• cross-platform effect;

• scope.

Model:

Low Materiality

↓

Lightweight Transition Control

High Materiality

↓

Enhanced Assessment

\+ Review

\+ Evidence

\+ Authorization

\+ Verification

⸻

20\. Transition Evidence

Evidence harus proporsional terhadap transition.

Possible evidence:

• current state evidence;

• target state definition;

• rationale;

• dependency analysis;

• organizational assessment;

• governance assessment;

• performance evidence;

• implementation readiness;

• execution evidence;

• verification evidence.

Baseline secara eksplisit menyatakan bahwa evidence harus proporsional terhadap materiality, impact, organizational scope, dependency, risk, reversibility, dan cross-platform effect.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

21\. Transition Dependency Assessment

Material transition harus menilai dependency yang terdampak.

Dependency dapat meliputi:

• organizational dependency;

• governance dependency;

• architecture dependency;

• operational dependency;

• performance dependency;

• cross-platform dependency.

Dependency assessment tidak memindahkan ownership.

⸻

22\. Transition Authority

Authority harus menjawab:

Who may authorize this transition?

Authority tidak boleh disimpulkan hanya dari:

• organizational seniority;

• execution role;

• technical role;

• ownership of implementation.

Authority mengikuti applicable authority model.

⸻

23\. Transition Accountability

Accountable owner bertanggung jawab terhadap outcome transition.

Accountability tidak sama dengan:

• authorizing authority;

• execution role;

• verification role.

Jika segregation diperlukan, transition harus menjaga separation of duties.

⸻

24\. Transition Execution Boundary

Execution harus tetap berada pada applicable owner.

PD-03 tidak memperoleh operational execution ownership atas Platform lain hanya karena transition memiliki governance/compliance impact.

Pattern:

Governance Decision

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

Applicable Domain Owner

↓

Execution

↓

Evidence

↓

PD-03 Governance / Compliance Assessment

⸻

25\. Verification Independence

Jika materiality atau risk memerlukannya, verification harus memiliki independence yang memadai dari execution.

Minimum principle:

Execution

≠

Verification

Independence level harus proporsional terhadap risk dan materiality.

⸻

26\. Transition Failure

Transition dapat gagal karena:

• authorization invalid;

• evidence insufficient;

• execution incomplete;

• dependency failure;

• resulting condition invalid;

• verification failure;

• boundary violation.

Failure tidak boleh ditutupi dengan memaksa resulting state.

⸻

27\. Failed Transition

Jika transition gagal:

Transition Execution

↓

Verification Failure

↓

Transition = NOT COMPLETE

System harus mempertahankan:

• last verified state;

• transition record;

• failure evidence;

• owner;

• disposition.

⸻

28\. Partial Transition

Jika transition hanya selesai sebagian:

Partial Execution

≠

Completed Transition

Current verified state harus tetap dapat diketahui.

Partial state tidak boleh dipromosikan menjadi canonical resulting state tanpa verification.

⸻

29\. Transition Exception

Jika normal transition mechanism tidak dapat digunakan:

1\. exception harus diidentifikasi;

2\. rationale harus dicatat;

3\. authority harus ditentukan;

4\. mitigation harus tersedia;

5\. owner harus ditetapkan;

6\. disposition harus ditentukan;

7\. material exception harus direview.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi shortcut untuk menghindari authorization.

⸻

30\. Transition Reversibility

Jika transition reversible:

Transition

↓

Reversal Mechanism

Jika irreversible:

• irreversibility harus diketahui sebelum authorization;

• consequence harus dinilai;

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• verification harus lebih ketat apabila material.

Reversibility tidak selalu required.

⸻

31\. Transition Traceability

Material transition harus dapat direconstruct:

Lifecycle Matter

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Execution

↓

Evidence

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Canonical Record

Baseline menetapkan traceability chain tersebut sebagai lifecycle requirement.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

32\. Transition Historical Continuity

Transition tidak boleh menghapus:

• previous state;

• previous decision;

• previous authority;

• previous evidence;

• previous accountability;

• previous lifecycle record.

Historical continuity harus dipertahankan sesuai applicable retention and governance requirements.

⸻

Part D bertanggung jawab terhadap operation setelah resulting state berlaku.

F4 bertanggung jawab terhadap controlled movement menuju resulting state.

F4

Transition

36\. Transition and Performance

Performance evidence dapat menjadi input transition assessment.

Performance Evidence

↓

Transition Assessment

↓

Governance Decision

Namun:

Performance evidence does not automatically authorize lifecycle transition.

Authority tetap berada pada applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

37\. Cross-Platform Transition

Jika transition berdampak lintas Platform:

1\. impact harus diidentifikasi;

2\. affected domains harus diketahui;

3\. applicable owners harus dilibatkan;

4\. authority boundary harus dipertahankan;

5\. evidence harus tersedia;

6\. resulting state harus diverifikasi.

Cross-platform impact tidak menciptakan cross-platform ownership.

⸻

Minimum canonical transition record:

⸻

39\. Transition Closure

Transition hanya boleh ditutup apabila:

• authorized scope selesai;

• execution selesai;

• verification selesai;

• resulting state confirmed;

• record lengkap;

• unresolved exception ditangani atau explicitly accepted.

Closure:

Execution Complete

↓

Verification Complete

↓

Resulting State Confirmed

↓

Record Complete

↓

Transition Closed

⸻

40\. Transition Status

F4 menggunakan status konseptual berikut:

Proposed

↓

Assessed

↓

Authorized

↓

In Progress

↓

Verification

↓

Completed

Exception states:

Blocked

Rejected

Cancelled

Failed

Escalated

Catatan source-fidelity: vocabulary status di atas merupakan bounded synthesis untuk transition record/workflow dan bukan klaim bahwa baseline PD-03 menetapkan literal status taxonomy tersebut.

Status lifecycle subject tetap dibedakan dari transition process status.

⸻

41\. Transition vs Lifecycle State

Canonical distinction:

Lifecycle State

↓

Condition of Subject

Transition Status

↓

Condition of Transition Process

Contoh:

Subject:

Established

Transition:

In Progress

Subject tidak otomatis menjadi:

Lifecycle State = In Progress

In Progress adalah transition-process condition.

⸻

42\. Transition vs Workflow

Workflow dapat membantu execution.

Tetapi:

Workflow Completion

≠

Lifecycle Transition Completion

Lifecycle transition completion memerlukan verification dan resulting-state confirmation.

⸻

43\. Transition Integrity Rules

F4 menetapkan aturan:

1\. Jangan mengubah state tanpa valid transition basis.

2\. Jangan authorize transition tanpa applicable authority.

3\. Jangan menutup transition tanpa verification.

4\. Jangan menyatakan resulting state tanpa evidence yang memadai.

5\. Jangan menghapus previous state.

6\. Jangan mengubah authority melalui transition.

7\. Jangan menggunakan transition untuk memperluas domain.

8\. Jangan mengambil ownership Platform lain melalui transition.

9\. Jangan menggunakan workflow status sebagai lifecycle state.

10\. Jangan menyamakan execution completion dengan transition completion.

⸻

44\. Transition Governance Escalation

Transition harus dieskalasikan apabila:

• authority ambiguous;

• material boundary conflict;

• cross-platform ownership conflict;

• resulting state disputed;

• evidence insufficient untuk material transition;

• constitutional impact detected;

• transition irreversibility memiliki unresolved risk;

• delegated authority tidak cukup.

Escalation mengikuti Part C dan applicable governance.

⸻

45\. Transition Boundary

F4 defines:

• transition concept;

• transition lifecycle;

• transition assessment;

• authorization;

• execution boundary;

• verification;

• resulting state;

• eligibility principle;

• transition integrity;

• failure handling;

• exception handling;

• traceability;

• transition record;

• transition closure.

F4 does not define:

• new authority;

• organizational ownership;

• technical implementation mechanism;

• universal AIOS lifecycle engine;

• final historical transition matrix unless source-backed;

• Platform-independent lifecycle vocabulary.

⸻

46\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline secara eksplisit mendukung:

• material transition harus memiliki current state;

• resulting state harus dapat diidentifikasi;

• transition membutuhkan assessment;

• applicable authority harus ada;

• evidence harus proporsional;

• dependency harus dipertimbangkan;

• transition harus dieksekusi secara terkendali;

• resulting state harus diverifikasi;

• transition harus direkam;

• lifecycle traceability harus dipertahankan.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menyatakan bahwa detailed eligibility dan transition semantics berada pada F2–F4.

Namun:

Definitive literal transition matrix PD-03 tidak ditemukan dalam available baseline.

Karena itu transition edges dan process status vocabulary yang lebih detail pada F4 diperlakukan sebagai Bounded Canonical Synthesis, bukan historical source fact.

⸻

47\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Sebelum menganggap transition matrix F4 immutable, Claude Code wajib:

1\. search seluruh canonical corpus untuk lifecycle transition terminology;

2\. search PD-03 baseline;

3\. search F1, F2, dan F3 final versions;

4\. search Architecture Decision Records terkait lifecycle;

5\. search native AIOS lifecycle/entity transition patterns;

6\. membedakan native entity lifecycle dari organizational lifecycle PD-03;

7\. reconcile seluruh candidate source/target edges;

8\. mempertahankan source-backed terminology apabila ditemukan;

9\. menghapus atau mengganti bounded synthesis yang bertentangan dengan higher-authority source;

10\. menetapkan final transition matrix;

11\. mendokumentasikan material semantic choice;

12\. membuat ADR apabila transition taxonomy berubah secara material.

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• membuat transition edge hanya karena workflow memerlukannya;

• menganggap semua state dapat berpindah ke semua state;

• menganggap Retired otomatis dapat kembali Established;

• menganggap Dissolved dapat otomatis kembali active;

• menghapus historical state;

• membuat authority baru melalui transition;

• mengubah ownership Platform lain;

• mengubah constitutional boundary tanpa applicable authority.

Jika ambiguity berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh Constitution, Founder Reserved Authority, Canonical Architecture supremacy, atau fundamental governance boundary, Claude Code wajib melakukan escalation.

⸻

48\. Success Criteria

F4 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. transition memiliki definisi canonical;

2\. source state dapat diidentifikasi;

3\. target/resulting state dapat diidentifikasi;

4\. transition assessment tersedia;

5\. applicable authority dapat diidentifikasi;

6\. rationale dapat ditelusuri;

7\. evidence requirement dapat ditentukan;

8\. dependency impact dapat dinilai;

9\. execution dapat dibedakan dari authorization;

10\. verification dapat dibedakan dari execution;

11\. resulting state tidak ditetapkan sebelum verification;

12\. material transition memiliki canonical record;

13\. transition dapat direconstruct;

14\. historical continuity dipertahankan;

15\. transition failure dapat ditangani;

16\. partial transition tidak dipromosikan sebagai complete;

17\. exception memiliki governance;

18\. irreversible transition mendapatkan appropriate scrutiny;

19\. creation transition tercakup;

20\. restructuring transition tercakup;

21\. retirement transition tercakup;

22\. dissolution transition tercakup;

23\. cross-platform transition menjaga ownership boundary;

24\. performance evidence tidak otomatis menjadi authority;

25\. workflow status tidak disamakan dengan lifecycle state;

26\. transition closure membutuhkan verification;

27\. tidak ada unsupported authority;

28\. tidak ada domain expansion;

29\. unresolved source gap ditandai;

30\. final transition matrix dapat direconciled oleh Claude Code.

50\. Canonical Transition Statement

F4 — Lifecycle Transition establishes the controlled mechanism through which a PD-03 lifecycle subject may move from an identifiable current condition to an authorized and verified resulting condition through assessment, applicable authorization, controlled execution, evidence, verification, and canonical recording while preserving governance authority, organizational ownership, lifecycle integrity, historical continuity, and cross-platform boundaries.

A lifecycle transition is not complete merely because execution has finished. Completion requires verification of the resulting condition and canonical recording of the transition outcome.

F4 does not create lifecycle authority or organizational ownership. Authority remains governed by Part C and applicable higher-order governance. Detailed transition eligibility and final transition matrix remain subject to source reconciliation where the available baseline does not provide literal canonical edges.
