\# 1. Purpose

F5 menetapkan governance mechanism untuk mengendalikan lifecycle change pada PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

Jika:

\`\`\`text

F3

Lifecycle States

menetapkan kondisi lifecycle,

dan:

F4

Lifecycle Transition

menetapkan mekanisme perpindahan antar kondisi,

maka:

F5

Lifecycle Change Governance

menetapkan bagaimana perubahan lifecycle:

• diidentifikasi;

• diklasifikasikan;

• dinilai;

• diauthorisasi;

• direview;

• dikendalikan;

• dieksekusi melalui applicable mechanism;

• diverifikasi;

• dicatat;

• ditutup;

• dan, bila diperlukan, dieskalasikan.

F5 memastikan lifecycle change tidak menjadi uncontrolled organizational change.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

F5 berada dalam chain:

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

F5 mewarisi constitutional boundary dari F1.

F5 tidak boleh:

• menciptakan lifecycle authority baru;

• mengubah Platform identity;

• mengambil ownership domain Platform lain;

• menggantikan Governance Architecture pada Part C;

• menggantikan operational model pada Part D;

• menggantikan performance architecture pada Part E.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Principle

No material lifecycle change shall be authorized or recognized as complete without sufficient governance context, applicable authority, proportional evidence, controlled transition, verification, and traceable outcome.

Lifecycle change harus dapat dijelaskan:

Why

↓

What

↓

Who

↓

Authority

↓

Evidence

↓

Change

↓

Verification

↓

Result

⸻

4\. Lifecycle Change Definition

Lifecycle change adalah perubahan material terhadap lifecycle condition, organizational configuration, atau structural condition dari lifecycle subject yang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Lifecycle change dapat mencakup:

• creation;

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• dissolution.

Kelima concern tersebut merupakan lifecycle concern yang ditempatkan baseline pada Part F.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. Lifecycle Change vs Operational Change

F5 harus menjaga distinction:

Operational Change

↓

Mengubah cara pekerjaan dilakukan

↓

Part D

sedangkan:

Lifecycle Change

↓

Mengubah lifecycle condition

atau structural/organizational condition

↓

Part F

Operational improvement tidak otomatis menjadi lifecycle change.

Sebaliknya, lifecycle change dapat menghasilkan operational consequence.

⸻

6\. Change Governance Lifecycle

Canonical governance flow:

Change Need

↓

Change Identification

↓

Change Classification

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Governance Review

↓

Authority Determination

↓

Decision / Authorization

↓

Controlled Transition

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Canonical Recording

↓

Closure

F5 mengatur governance flow.

F4 mengatur transition mechanics.

⸻

7\. Change Initiation

Lifecycle change dapat diinisiasi oleh:

• lifecycle condition;

• organizational need;

• governance finding;

• compliance finding;

• performance signal;

• operational constraint;

• architecture dependency;

• structural requirement;

• strategic direction;

• approved change request.

Initiation tidak sama dengan authorization.

Initiation

≠

Authorization

⸻

8\. Change Identification

Setiap material lifecycle change harus memiliki identifiable change subject.

Minimum:

￼

Change yang tidak dapat diidentifikasi tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai canonical lifecycle change.

⸻

9\. Change Classification

Lifecycle change harus diklasifikasikan sebelum authorization.

Minimum categories:

Creation

Restructuring

Transition

Retirement

Dissolution

Classification menentukan:

• applicable authority;

• evidence intensity;

• review intensity;

• dependency assessment;

• transition mechanism;

• verification requirement.

⸻

10\. Change Materiality

Materiality menentukan tingkat governance yang diperlukan.

Materiality harus mempertimbangkan:

• scope;

• organizational impact;

• authority impact;

• dependency;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• cross-platform effect;

• compliance consequence;

• operational consequence.

Model:

Low Materiality

↓

Proportionate Governance

High Materiality

↓

Enhanced Governance

F5 tidak menetapkan numerical materiality score apabila belum source-backed.

⸻

11\. Change Impact Assessment

Material lifecycle change harus memiliki impact assessment.

Minimum assessment:

Organization

Governance

Authority

Accountability

Operations

Performance

Dependencies

Compliance

Canonical Artifacts

Cross-Platform Interfaces

Impact assessment harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

12\. Change Dependency Assessment

Change harus mengidentifikasi dependency yang terdampak.

Dependency dapat mencakup:

• organizational;

• governance;

• architecture;

• operational;

• performance;

• compliance;

• cross-platform.

Dependency assessment tidak memindahkan ownership.

⸻

13\. Authority Determination

Sebelum lifecycle change diotorisasi, applicable authority harus ditentukan.

Model:

Lifecycle Change

↓

Authority Assessment

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Authorization

F5 tidak menciptakan authority.

Authority tetap berasal dari:

• Part C;

• authority and mandate;

• governance charter;

• delegation;

• higher-order constitutional authority.

Baseline menegaskan bahwa lifecycle authority berasal dari applicable governance and authority architecture.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

14\. Authority vs Accountability

F5 harus mempertahankan:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Authority menentukan siapa yang berhak memutuskan atau mengotorisasi.

Accountability menentukan siapa yang bertanggung jawab atas outcome.

Execution dilakukan oleh applicable implementation owner.

⸻

15\. Delegated Lifecycle Change

Lifecycle authority dapat digunakan melalui valid delegation apabila delegation tersebut memang berlaku.

Delegation tidak boleh:

• menghapus accountability;

• memperluas scope tanpa basis;

• memindahkan ownership;

• menciptakan authority baru;

• menghindari higher-level approval requirement.

Jika delegated authority tidak cukup, change harus dieskalasikan.

⸻

16\. Governance Review

Material lifecycle change harus melalui governance review yang proporsional.

Review harus memeriksa:

• change purpose;

• classification;

• current state;

• target/resulting state;

• authority;

• evidence;

• impact;

• dependencies;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• implementation readiness;

• verification method.

Review bukan otomatis authorization.

Review

≠

Authorization

⸻

17\. Decision Requirement

Material lifecycle change harus memiliki decision yang dapat ditelusuri.

Decision minimum harus menjawab:

0\. apa yang diubah;

0\. mengapa;

0\. dari kondisi apa;

0\. menuju kondisi apa;

0\. authority apa yang digunakan;

0\. evidence apa yang mendukung;

0\. impact apa yang diterima;

0\. bagaimana transition akan diverifikasi.

⸻

18\. Decision Outcomes

Governance decision dapat menghasilkan:

Approved

Rejected

Deferred

Conditionally Approved

Escalated

Cancelled

Catatan source-fidelity: taxonomy literal di atas adalah bounded synthesis untuk workflow F5, bukan klaim bahwa seluruh vocabulary tersebut telah ditetapkan secara eksplisit oleh baseline.

Claude Code harus mereconcile vocabulary tersebut dengan canonical decision taxonomy sebelum implementation final.

⸻

19\. Conditional Approval

Conditional approval hanya berlaku apabila:

• condition dapat diidentifikasi;

• owner ditentukan;

• deadline/effective condition dapat ditentukan jika relevan;

• verification mechanism tersedia.

Conditional approval tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai unrestricted authorization.

⸻

20\. Rejection

Rejected lifecycle change:

• tidak boleh dieksekusi;

• harus memiliki rationale apabila material;

• harus tetap tercatat;

• dapat diajukan kembali hanya melalui applicable mechanism.

Rejection tidak menghapus change history.

⸻

21\. Deferral

Deferred change berarti:

change belum memperoleh authorization untuk execution.

Deferral bukan approval parsial.

Deferred change harus memiliki:

• reason;

• owner;

• next review condition apabila diperlukan.

⸻

22\. Change Evidence

Evidence harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Evidence dapat meliputi:

• current-state evidence;

• proposed-state definition;

• rationale;

• governance assessment;

• performance evidence;

• compliance evidence;

• dependency analysis;

• organizational impact;

• implementation readiness;

• transition evidence.

Baseline menetapkan bahwa evidence harus proporsional terhadap materiality, impact, organizational scope, dependency, risk, reversibility, dan cross-platform effect.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

23\. Evidence Sufficiency

Governance review harus menentukan apakah evidence cukup untuk decision.

Evidence

↓

Sufficiency Assessment

↓

Sufficient / Insufficient

Jika evidence insufficient untuk material change:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

No Final Authorization

kecuali applicable emergency mechanism secara eksplisit mengizinkannya.

⸻

24\. Change Risk

Material lifecycle change harus mempertimbangkan risk.

Risk dimensions dapat mencakup:

• governance risk;

• organizational risk;

• operational risk;

• compliance risk;

• dependency risk;

• cross-platform risk;

• irreversibility risk.

Risk assessment harus proporsional terhadap change.

⸻

25\. Reversibility

Lifecycle change harus mempertimbangkan apakah perubahan dapat dibalik.

Reversible

↓

Reversal Path Identified

atau:

Irreversible

↓

Enhanced Consequence Assessment

Irreversible change membutuhkan scrutiny yang sesuai.

⸻

26\. Cross-Platform Change Governance

Jika lifecycle change berdampak terhadap Platform lain:

0\. affected Platform harus diidentifikasi;

0\. dependency harus dianalisis;

0\. applicable owner harus diketahui;

0\. applicable authority harus dihormati;

0\. cross-platform coordination harus dilakukan;

0\. ownership tidak boleh berpindah secara informal.

Cross-platform impact tidak menjadikan PD-03 owner atas Platform lain.

⸻

27\. Governance Boundary Preservation

F5 tidak boleh digunakan untuk:

• mengambil alih Architecture;

• mengambil alih Security;

• mengambil alih Quality;

• mengambil alih Runtime;

• mengambil alih AI Engineering;

• mengambil alih Infrastructure;

• mengambil alih Developer Experience.

PD-03 tetap berada dalam:

Governance & Compliance

↓

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Boundary tersebut harus tetap dipertahankan sepanjang lifecycle change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

28\. Change and Architecture

Lifecycle change yang memiliki architectural consequence harus menggunakan applicable Architecture Governance mechanism.

Model:

Lifecycle Change

↓

Architecture Impact

↓

Architecture Governance

↓

Applicable Architecture Decision

F5 tidak menggantikan Architecture Governance.

⸻

29\. Change and Organization Architecture

Jika lifecycle change mengubah organizational structure:

Lifecycle Decision

↓

Authorized Organizational Change

↓

Part B

↓

Resulting Organization

F5 mengatur governance lifecycle change.

Part B menentukan resulting organizational structure.

⸻

30\. Change and Operations

Lifecycle change harus mempertimbangkan operational readiness.

Namun:

Lifecycle Governance

≠

Operational Execution

Setelah resulting state berlaku, operating model mengambil alih operationalization.

⸻

31\. Change and Performance

Performance evidence dapat menjadi trigger atau input lifecycle change.

Namun:

Performance Signal

≠

Lifecycle Decision

Performance evidence harus masuk ke governance assessment sebelum decision.

Baseline menegaskan hubungan:

Performance Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Assessment

↓

Change Decision

↓

Lifecycle Transition

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

32\. Change and Compliance

Compliance finding dapat menjadi trigger lifecycle change.

Model:

Compliance Finding

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Lifecycle Change Assessment

↓

Governance Decision

Compliance finding tidak otomatis mengubah lifecycle state.

⸻

33\. Change Execution Boundary

Setelah authorization:

Authorized Change

↓

Applicable Owner

↓

Transition Execution

PD-03 governance authority tidak otomatis berarti PD-03 execution authority.

Execution ownership tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

34\. Change Verification

Setiap material lifecycle change harus memiliki verification mechanism.

Verification harus menentukan apakah:

• authorized scope tercapai;

• resulting state benar;

• evidence cukup;

• dependencies tertangani;

• conditions terpenuhi;

• canonical record lengkap.

Verification bukan sekadar:

"implementation finished."

⸻

35\. Change Completion

Lifecycle change hanya dapat dianggap complete apabila:

Authorization

↓

Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State Confirmed

↓

Canonical Record

↓

Closure

Execution completion tanpa verification bukan lifecycle completion.

⸻

36\. Failed Change

Jika verification gagal:

Change

↓

Verification Failure

↓

Not Complete

Record harus mempertahankan:

• last verified state;

• attempted change;

• failure reason;

• evidence;

• owner;

• disposition.

⸻

37\. Partial Change

Partial execution tidak boleh dipromosikan menjadi completed lifecycle change.

Partial Execution

≠

Completed Transition

Current verified state harus tetap diketahui.

⸻

38\. Change Exception

Exception terhadap normal lifecycle change mechanism harus:

0\. diidentifikasi;

0\. memiliki rationale;

0\. memiliki authority;

0\. memiliki owner;

0\. memiliki mitigation;

0\. memiliki disposition;

0\. dicatat;

0\. direview jika material.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi permanent bypass.

⸻

39\. Emergency Lifecycle Change

Jika AIOS memiliki emergency mechanism yang applicable, emergency lifecycle change tetap harus:

• memiliki context;

• memiliki scope;

• memiliki authority basis;

• dicatat;

• diverifikasi;

• direview setelah emergency;

• dipulihkan ke normal governance mechanism.

F5 tidak menciptakan emergency authority baru.

Jika baseline canonical corpus tidak menetapkan emergency lifecycle authority, Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation sebelum mengaktifkan mechanism tersebut.

⸻

40\. Change Traceability

Material lifecycle change harus dapat direconstruct:

Initiation

↓

Assessment

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Verification

↓

Result

↓

Closure

Baseline secara eksplisit menempatkan lifecycle traceability sebagai:

Lifecycle Matter

→ Assessment

→ Authority

→ Decision

→ Transition

→ Verification

→ Result

→ Canonical Record

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Minimum record:

￼

⸻

42\. Change Record Integrity

Canonical change record harus:

• attributable;

• versioned;

• traceable;

• reviewable;

• historically preserved.

Correction terhadap record tidak boleh menghapus historical state.

Correction harus menghasilkan traceable amendment.

⸻

43\. Change Closure

Closure hanya dapat dilakukan apabila:

• authorized scope selesai;

• applicable transition selesai;

• verification selesai;

• resulting state confirmed;

• required evidence tersedia;

• record lengkap;

• unresolved exception memiliki disposition.

No Verified Result

↓

No Final Closure

⸻

44\. Governance Escalation

Lifecycle change harus dieskalasikan apabila:

• authority ambiguous;

• material boundary conflict;

• cross-platform ownership conflict;

• constitutional impact terdeteksi;

• delegation tidak mencukupi;

• evidence material tidak memadai;

• resulting state disputed;

• irreversible consequence belum terselesaikan.

Escalation mengikuti Part C dan applicable authority.

⸻

45\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Lifecycle Review

F5 dan F6 harus dibedakan:

F5

Governance of Change

↓

Mengatur bagaimana change dikendalikan

F6

Lifecycle Review

↓

Mengatur bagaimana lifecycle condition/change dievaluasi

F5 tidak menduplikasi review methodology F6.

⸻

46\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Lifecycle Control

F5 dan F7 harus dibedakan:

F5

Governance

↓

Authority

Decision

Accountability

Change Governance

F7

Control

↓

Control Mechanism

Monitoring

Exception Detection

Corrective Control

F5 menentukan governance context.

F7 menentukan control architecture secara detail.

⸻

47\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Implementation

F5 dan F8 harus dibedakan:

F5

Authorize and Govern

F8

Implement

F5 tidak mengambil execution ownership dari F8 atau applicable domain owner.

⸻

48\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Improvement

Improvement dapat menghasilkan lifecycle change proposal.

Namun:

Improvement Recommendation

≠

Authorized Lifecycle Change

Lifecycle change tetap harus melalui F5 governance mechanism.

⸻

49\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Maturity

Maturity assessment berada pada F9.

F5 dapat menghasilkan evidence yang digunakan untuk maturity assessment.

F5 tidak menetapkan maturity score.

⸻

50\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Success

F5 memastikan change governed.

F10 menentukan lifecycle success.

Change yang berhasil dieksekusi belum tentu menghasilkan lifecycle success apabila:

• authority violated;

• boundary breached;

• resulting state invalid;

• traceability incomplete;

• operational readiness insufficient.

⸻

51\. Change Governance Principles

F5 menetapkan prinsip:

0\. Govern before material change.

0\. Match authority to materiality.

0\. Match evidence to risk and impact.

0\. Preserve accountability.

0\. Separate authorization from execution where required.

0\. Preserve lifecycle state integrity.

0\. Verify resulting state.

0\. Preserve historical continuity.

0\. Escalate unresolved authority ambiguity.

0\. Preserve cross-platform ownership.

0\. Do not use lifecycle change to bypass governance.

0\. Do not convert operational convenience into constitutional change.

52\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline secara eksplisit mendukung:

• Part F sebagai lifecycle/change boundary;

• lifecycle categories creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, dissolution;

• applicable authority;

• proportional evidence;

• lifecycle traceability;

• controlled transition;

• verification;

Namun baseline yang tersedia belum memberikan definitive literal taxonomy untuk seluruh lifecycle-change decision outcomes, materiality levels, atau emergency lifecycle governance states.

53\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation sebelum menganggap detail berikut immutable:

0\. lifecycle change classification taxonomy;

0\. materiality thresholds;

0\. decision outcome vocabulary;

0\. emergency lifecycle mechanism;

0\. detailed approval thresholds;

0\. mandatory review thresholds;

0\. final transition governance matrix;

0\. lifecycle change authority matrix.

Claude Code harus:

• search seluruh canonical corpus;

• search PD-03 baseline;

• search Part C authority governance;

• search F1–F4;

• search existing AIOS lifecycle/change mechanisms;

• membedakan native entity lifecycle dengan organizational lifecycle PD-03;

• mempertahankan source-backed terminology;

• membuat ADR apabila terdapat material semantic choice;

• menandai unresolved ambiguity secara eksplisit.

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• menciptakan authority baru;

• menganggap operational urgency sebagai lifecycle authority;

• menganggap performance result sebagai automatic authorization;

• mengambil ownership Platform lain;

• membuat transition edge tanpa source/architecture basis;

• menggunakan emergency mechanism yang belum memiliki applicable authority.

Jika ambiguity berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh:

• Founder Reserved Authority;

• Constitution;

• Canonical Architecture supremacy;

• fundamental governance boundary;

maka harus dilakukan escalation.

54\. Success Criteria

F5 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. lifecycle change memiliki governance mechanism yang jelas;

0\. initiation dibedakan dari authorization;

0\. classification dilakukan sebelum authorization;

0\. materiality diperhitungkan;

0\. impact assessment tersedia;

0\. dependency assessment tersedia;

0\. authority dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. accountability dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. governance review tersedia;

0\. decision dapat ditelusuri;

0\. conditional approval dapat dikontrol;

0\. rejection tetap tercatat;

0\. deferral tidak dianggap approval;

0\. cross-platform impact dapat ditangani;

0\. execution ownership tetap terpisah;

0\. verification diperlukan untuk material change;

0\. failed change dapat ditangani;

0\. partial change tidak dianggap complete;

0\. exception memiliki governance;

0\. irreversible change mendapat scrutiny;

0\. canonical change record tersedia;

0\. historical continuity dipertahankan;

0\. closure membutuhkan verification;

0\. escalation tersedia untuk authority ambiguity;

0\. F5 tidak mengambil alih F6 review;

0\. F5 tidak mengambil alih F7 control;

0\. F5 tidak mengambil alih F8 implementation;

0\. F5 tidak menciptakan authority baru;

0\. F5 tidak memperluas PD-03 domain boundary;

0\. unresolved source gaps ditandai;

0\. Claude Code memiliki explicit reconciliation directive.

⸻

56\. Canonical Governance Statement

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance establishes the governance mechanism through which material lifecycle changes of PD-03 Governance & Compliance are identified, classified, assessed, reviewed, authorized, transitioned, verified, recorded, escalated when necessary, and formally closed while preserving authority integrity, accountability, evidence sufficiency, lifecycle continuity, organizational ownership, and cross-platform boundaries.

Lifecycle change governance does not create new authority, organizational ownership, or execution responsibility. It governs the use of authority established elsewhere and ensures that material lifecycle change remains controlled, evidence-based, traceable, and verifiable.

F5 must remain distinct from F4 transition mechanics, F6 lifecycle review, F7 lifecycle control, and F8 lifecycle implementation.

F5 tidak boleh:

• menciptakan lifecycle authority baru;

• mengubah Platform identity;

• mengambil ownership domain Platform lain;

• menggantikan Governance Architecture pada Part C;

• menggantikan operational model pada Part D;

• menggantikan performance architecture pada Part E.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Principle

No material lifecycle change shall be authorized or recognized as complete without sufficient governance context, applicable authority, proportional evidence, controlled transition, verification, and traceable outcome.

Lifecycle change harus dapat dijelaskan:

Why

↓

What

↓

Who

↓

Authority

↓

Evidence

↓

Change

↓

Verification

↓

Result

⸻

4\. Lifecycle Change Definition

Lifecycle change adalah perubahan material terhadap lifecycle condition, organizational configuration, atau structural condition dari lifecycle subject yang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Lifecycle change dapat mencakup:

• creation;

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• dissolution.

Kelima concern tersebut merupakan lifecycle concern yang ditempatkan baseline pada Part F.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. Lifecycle Change vs Operational Change

F5 harus menjaga distinction:

Operational Change

↓

Mengubah cara pekerjaan dilakukan

↓

Part D

sedangkan:

Lifecycle Change

↓

Mengubah lifecycle condition

atau structural/organizational condition

↓

Part F

Operational improvement tidak otomatis menjadi lifecycle change.

Sebaliknya, lifecycle change dapat menghasilkan operational consequence.

⸻

6\. Change Governance Lifecycle

Canonical governance flow:

Change Need

↓

Change Identification

↓

Change Classification

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Governance Review

↓

Authority Determination

↓

Decision / Authorization

↓

Controlled Transition

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Canonical Recording

↓

Closure

F5 mengatur governance flow.

F4 mengatur transition mechanics.

⸻

7\. Change Initiation

Lifecycle change dapat diinisiasi oleh:

• lifecycle condition;

• organizational need;

• governance finding;

• compliance finding;

• performance signal;

• operational constraint;

• architecture dependency;

• structural requirement;

• strategic direction;

• approved change request.

Initiation tidak sama dengan authorization.

Initiation

≠

Authorization

⸻

8\. Change Identification

Setiap material lifecycle change harus memiliki identifiable change subject.

Minimum:

Field Requirement

Change ID Required

Lifecycle Subject Required

Current State Required

Proposed Resulting State Required when applicable

Change Type Required

Initiator Required

Rationale Required

Date Required

Change yang tidak dapat diidentifikasi tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai canonical lifecycle change.

⸻

9\. Change Classification

Lifecycle change harus diklasifikasikan sebelum authorization.

Minimum categories:

Creation

Restructuring

Transition

Retirement

Dissolution

Classification menentukan:

• applicable authority;

• evidence intensity;

• review intensity;

• dependency assessment;

• transition mechanism;

• verification requirement.

⸻

10\. Change Materiality

Materiality menentukan tingkat governance yang diperlukan.

Materiality harus mempertimbangkan:

• scope;

• organizational impact;

• authority impact;

• dependency;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• cross-platform effect;

• compliance consequence;

• operational consequence.

Model:

Low Materiality

↓

Proportionate Governance

High Materiality

↓

Enhanced Governance

F5 tidak menetapkan numerical materiality score apabila belum source-backed.

⸻

11\. Change Impact Assessment

Material lifecycle change harus memiliki impact assessment.

Minimum assessment:

Organization

Governance

Authority

Accountability

Operations

Performance

Dependencies

Compliance

Canonical Artifacts

Cross-Platform Interfaces

Impact assessment harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

12\. Change Dependency Assessment

Change harus mengidentifikasi dependency yang terdampak.

Dependency dapat mencakup:

• organizational;

• governance;

• architecture;

• operational;

• performance;

• compliance;

• cross-platform.

Dependency assessment tidak memindahkan ownership.

⸻

13\. Authority Determination

Sebelum lifecycle change diotorisasi, applicable authority harus ditentukan.

Model:

Lifecycle Change

↓

Authority Assessment

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Authorization

F5 tidak menciptakan authority.

Authority tetap berasal dari:

• Part C;

• authority and mandate;

• governance charter;

• delegation;

• higher-order constitutional authority.

Baseline menegaskan bahwa lifecycle authority berasal dari applicable governance and authority architecture.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

14\. Authority vs Accountability

F5 harus mempertahankan:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Authority menentukan siapa yang berhak memutuskan atau mengotorisasi.

Accountability menentukan siapa yang bertanggung jawab atas outcome.

Execution dilakukan oleh applicable implementation owner.

⸻

15\. Delegated Lifecycle Change

Lifecycle authority dapat digunakan melalui valid delegation apabila delegation tersebut memang berlaku.

Delegation tidak boleh:

• menghapus accountability;

• memperluas scope tanpa basis;

• memindahkan ownership;

• menciptakan authority baru;

• menghindari higher-level approval requirement.

Jika delegated authority tidak cukup, change harus dieskalasikan.

⸻

16\. Governance Review

Material lifecycle change harus melalui governance review yang proporsional.

Review harus memeriksa:

• change purpose;

• classification;

• current state;

• target/resulting state;

• authority;

• evidence;

• impact;

• dependencies;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• implementation readiness;

• verification method.

Review bukan otomatis authorization.

Review

≠

Authorization

⸻

17\. Decision Requirement

Material lifecycle change harus memiliki decision yang dapat ditelusuri.

Decision minimum harus menjawab:

1\. apa yang diubah;

2\. mengapa;

3\. dari kondisi apa;

4\. menuju kondisi apa;

5\. authority apa yang digunakan;

6\. evidence apa yang mendukung;

7\. impact apa yang diterima;

8\. bagaimana transition akan diverifikasi.

⸻

18\. Decision Outcomes

Governance decision dapat menghasilkan:

Approved

Rejected

Deferred

Conditionally Approved

Escalated

Cancelled

Catatan source-fidelity: taxonomy literal di atas adalah bounded synthesis untuk workflow F5, bukan klaim bahwa seluruh vocabulary tersebut telah ditetapkan secara eksplisit oleh baseline.

Claude Code harus mereconcile vocabulary tersebut dengan canonical decision taxonomy sebelum implementation final.

⸻

19\. Conditional Approval

Conditional approval hanya berlaku apabila:

• condition dapat diidentifikasi;

• owner ditentukan;

• deadline/effective condition dapat ditentukan jika relevan;

• verification mechanism tersedia.

Conditional approval tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai unrestricted authorization.

⸻

20\. Rejection

Rejected lifecycle change:

• tidak boleh dieksekusi;

• harus memiliki rationale apabila material;

• harus tetap tercatat;

• dapat diajukan kembali hanya melalui applicable mechanism.

Rejection tidak menghapus change history.

⸻

21\. Deferral

Deferred change berarti:

change belum memperoleh authorization untuk execution.

Deferral bukan approval parsial.

Deferred change harus memiliki:

• reason;

• owner;

• next review condition apabila diperlukan.

⸻

22\. Change Evidence

Evidence harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Evidence dapat meliputi:

• current-state evidence;

• proposed-state definition;

• rationale;

• governance assessment;

• performance evidence;

• compliance evidence;

• dependency analysis;

• organizational impact;

• implementation readiness;

• transition evidence.

Baseline menetapkan bahwa evidence harus proporsional terhadap materiality, impact, organizational scope, dependency, risk, reversibility, dan cross-platform effect.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

23\. Evidence Sufficiency

Governance review harus menentukan apakah evidence cukup untuk decision.

Evidence

↓

Sufficiency Assessment

↓

Sufficient / Insufficient

Jika evidence insufficient untuk material change:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

No Final Authorization

kecuali applicable emergency mechanism secara eksplisit mengizinkannya.

⸻

24\. Change Risk

Material lifecycle change harus mempertimbangkan risk.

Risk dimensions dapat mencakup:

• governance risk;

• organizational risk;

• operational risk;

• compliance risk;

• dependency risk;

• cross-platform risk;

• irreversibility risk.

Risk assessment harus proporsional terhadap change.

⸻

25\. Reversibility

Lifecycle change harus mempertimbangkan apakah perubahan dapat dibalik.

Reversible

↓

Reversal Path Identified

atau:

Irreversible

↓

Enhanced Consequence Assessment

Irreversible change membutuhkan scrutiny yang sesuai.

⸻

26\. Cross-Platform Change Governance

Jika lifecycle change berdampak terhadap Platform lain:

1\. affected Platform harus diidentifikasi;

2\. dependency harus dianalisis;

3\. applicable owner harus diketahui;

4\. applicable authority harus dihormati;

5\. cross-platform coordination harus dilakukan;

6\. ownership tidak boleh berpindah secara informal.

Cross-platform impact tidak menjadikan PD-03 owner atas Platform lain.

⸻

27\. Governance Boundary Preservation

F5 tidak boleh digunakan untuk:

• mengambil alih Architecture;

• mengambil alih Security;

• mengambil alih Quality;

• mengambil alih Runtime;

• mengambil alih AI Engineering;

• mengambil alih Infrastructure;

• mengambil alih Developer Experience.

PD-03 tetap berada dalam:

Governance & Compliance

↓

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Boundary tersebut harus tetap dipertahankan sepanjang lifecycle change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

28\. Change and Architecture

Lifecycle change yang memiliki architectural consequence harus menggunakan applicable Architecture Governance mechanism.

Model:

Lifecycle Change

↓

Architecture Impact

↓

Architecture Governance

↓

Applicable Architecture Decision

F5 tidak menggantikan Architecture Governance.

⸻

29\. Change and Organization Architecture

Jika lifecycle change mengubah organizational structure:

Lifecycle Decision

↓

Authorized Organizational Change

↓

Part B

↓

Resulting Organization

F5 mengatur governance lifecycle change.

Part B menentukan resulting organizational structure.

⸻

30\. Change and Operations

Lifecycle change harus mempertimbangkan operational readiness.

Namun:

Lifecycle Governance

≠

Operational Execution

Setelah resulting state berlaku, operating model mengambil alih operationalization.

⸻

31\. Change and Performance

Performance evidence dapat menjadi trigger atau input lifecycle change.

Namun:

Performance Signal

≠

Lifecycle Decision

Performance evidence harus masuk ke governance assessment sebelum decision.

Baseline menegaskan hubungan:

Performance Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Assessment

↓

Change Decision

↓

Lifecycle Transition

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

32\. Change and Compliance

Compliance finding dapat menjadi trigger lifecycle change.

Model:

Compliance Finding

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Lifecycle Change Assessment

↓

Governance Decision

Compliance finding tidak otomatis mengubah lifecycle state.

⸻

33\. Change Execution Boundary

Setelah authorization:

Authorized Change

↓

Applicable Owner

↓

Transition Execution

PD-03 governance authority tidak otomatis berarti PD-03 execution authority.

Execution ownership tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

34\. Change Verification

Setiap material lifecycle change harus memiliki verification mechanism.

Verification harus menentukan apakah:

• authorized scope tercapai;

• resulting state benar;

• evidence cukup;

• dependencies tertangani;

• conditions terpenuhi;

• canonical record lengkap.

Verification bukan sekadar:

"implementation finished."

⸻

35\. Change Completion

Lifecycle change hanya dapat dianggap complete apabila:

Authorization

↓

Execution

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State Confirmed

↓

Canonical Record

↓

Closure

Execution completion tanpa verification bukan lifecycle completion.

⸻

36\. Failed Change

Jika verification gagal:

Change

↓

Verification Failure

↓

Not Complete

Record harus mempertahankan:

• last verified state;

• attempted change;

• failure reason;

• evidence;

• owner;

• disposition.

⸻

37\. Partial Change

Partial execution tidak boleh dipromosikan menjadi completed lifecycle change.

Partial Execution

≠

Completed Transition

Current verified state harus tetap diketahui.

⸻

38\. Change Exception

Exception terhadap normal lifecycle change mechanism harus:

1\. diidentifikasi;

2\. memiliki rationale;

3\. memiliki authority;

4\. memiliki owner;

5\. memiliki mitigation;

6\. memiliki disposition;

7\. dicatat;

8\. direview jika material.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi permanent bypass.

⸻

39\. Emergency Lifecycle Change

Jika AIOS memiliki emergency mechanism yang applicable, emergency lifecycle change tetap harus:

• memiliki context;

• memiliki scope;

• memiliki authority basis;

• dicatat;

• diverifikasi;

• direview setelah emergency;

• dipulihkan ke normal governance mechanism.

F5 tidak menciptakan emergency authority baru.

Jika baseline canonical corpus tidak menetapkan emergency lifecycle authority, Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation sebelum mengaktifkan mechanism tersebut.

⸻

40\. Change Traceability

Material lifecycle change harus dapat direconstruct:

Initiation

↓

Assessment

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Verification

↓

Result

↓

Closure

Baseline secara eksplisit menempatkan lifecycle traceability sebagai:

Lifecycle Matter

→ Assessment

→ Authority

→ Decision

→ Transition

→ Verification

→ Result

→ Canonical Record

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

41\. Canonical Change Record

Minimum record:

Field Requirement

Change ID Required

Lifecycle Subject Required

Change Type Required

Current State Required

Proposed State Required when applicable

Rationale Required

Materiality Required

Impact Assessment Required

Dependency Assessment Required when applicable

Authority Required

Accountability Required

Evidence Required

Review Result Required

Decision Required

Decision Reference Required

Transition Reference Required when applicable

Verification Result Required

Resulting State Required

Effective Date Required

Exception Reference Required when applicable

Historical Reference Required

Closure Status Required

⸻

42\. Change Record Integrity

Canonical change record harus:

• attributable;

• versioned;

• traceable;

• reviewable;

• historically preserved.

Correction terhadap record tidak boleh menghapus historical state.

Correction harus menghasilkan traceable amendment.

⸻

43\. Change Closure

Closure hanya dapat dilakukan apabila:

• authorized scope selesai;

• applicable transition selesai;

• verification selesai;

• resulting state confirmed;

• required evidence tersedia;

• record lengkap;

• unresolved exception memiliki disposition.

No Verified Result

↓

No Final Closure

⸻

44\. Governance Escalation

Lifecycle change harus dieskalasikan apabila:

• authority ambiguous;

• material boundary conflict;

• cross-platform ownership conflict;

• constitutional impact terdeteksi;

• delegation tidak mencukupi;

• evidence material tidak memadai;

• resulting state disputed;

• irreversible consequence belum terselesaikan.

Escalation mengikuti Part C dan applicable authority.

⸻

45\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Lifecycle Review

F5 dan F6 harus dibedakan:

F5

Governance of Change

↓

Mengatur bagaimana change dikendalikan

F6

Lifecycle Review

↓

Mengatur bagaimana lifecycle condition/change dievaluasi

F5 tidak menduplikasi review methodology F6.

⸻

46\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Lifecycle Control

F5 dan F7 harus dibedakan:

F5

Governance

↓

Authority

Decision

Accountability

Change Governance

F7

Control

↓

Control Mechanism

Monitoring

Exception Detection

Corrective Control

F5 menentukan governance context.

F7 menentukan control architecture secara detail.

⸻

47\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Implementation

F5 dan F8 harus dibedakan:

F5

Authorize and Govern

F8

Implement

F5 tidak mengambil execution ownership dari F8 atau applicable domain owner.

⸻

48\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Improvement

Improvement dapat menghasilkan lifecycle change proposal.

Namun:

Improvement Recommendation

≠

Authorized Lifecycle Change

Lifecycle change tetap harus melalui F5 governance mechanism.

⸻

49\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Maturity

Maturity assessment berada pada F9.

F5 dapat menghasilkan evidence yang digunakan untuk maturity assessment.

F5 tidak menetapkan maturity score.

⸻

50\. Lifecycle Change Governance vs Success

F5 memastikan change governed.

F10 menentukan lifecycle success.

Change yang berhasil dieksekusi belum tentu menghasilkan lifecycle success apabila:

• authority violated;

• boundary breached;

• resulting state invalid;

• traceability incomplete;

• operational readiness insufficient.

⸻

51\. Change Governance Principles

F5 menetapkan prinsip:

1\. Govern before material change.

2\. Match authority to materiality.

3\. Match evidence to risk and impact.

4\. Preserve accountability.

5\. Separate authorization from execution where required.

6\. Preserve lifecycle state integrity.

7\. Verify resulting state.

8\. Preserve historical continuity.

9\. Escalate unresolved authority ambiguity.

10\. Preserve cross-platform ownership.

11\. Do not use lifecycle change to bypass governance.

12\. Do not convert operational convenience into constitutional change.

⸻

52\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline secara eksplisit mendukung:

• Part F sebagai lifecycle/change boundary;

• lifecycle categories creation, restructuring, transition, retirement, dissolution;

• applicable authority;

• proportional evidence;

• lifecycle traceability;

• controlled transition;

• verification;

• canonical recording;

• exception handling;

• cross-platform ownership preservation.

Namun baseline yang tersedia belum memberikan definitive literal taxonomy untuk seluruh lifecycle-change decision outcomes, materiality levels, atau emergency lifecycle governance states.

Karena itu vocabulary tersebut pada F5 diklasifikasikan sebagai:

Bounded Canonical Synthesis

dan tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai historical source fact tanpa reconciliation.

⸻

53\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation sebelum menganggap detail berikut immutable:

1\. lifecycle change classification taxonomy;

2\. materiality thresholds;

3\. decision outcome vocabulary;

4\. emergency lifecycle mechanism;

5\. detailed approval thresholds;

6\. mandatory review thresholds;

7\. final transition governance matrix;

8\. lifecycle change authority matrix.

Claude Code harus:

• search seluruh canonical corpus;

• search PD-03 baseline;

• search Part C authority governance;

• search F1–F4;

• search existing AIOS lifecycle/change mechanisms;

• membedakan native entity lifecycle dengan organizational lifecycle PD-03;

• mempertahankan source-backed terminology;

• membuat ADR apabila terdapat material semantic choice;

• menandai unresolved ambiguity secara eksplisit.

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• menciptakan authority baru;

• menganggap operational urgency sebagai lifecycle authority;

• menganggap performance result sebagai automatic authorization;

• mengambil ownership Platform lain;

• membuat transition edge tanpa source/architecture basis;

• menggunakan emergency mechanism yang belum memiliki applicable authority.

Jika ambiguity berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh:

• Founder Reserved Authority;

• Constitution;

• Canonical Architecture supremacy;

• fundamental governance boundary;

maka harus dilakukan escalation.

⸻

54\. Success Criteria

F5 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. lifecycle change memiliki governance mechanism yang jelas;

2\. initiation dibedakan dari authorization;

3\. classification dilakukan sebelum authorization;

4\. materiality diperhitungkan;

5\. impact assessment tersedia;

6\. dependency assessment tersedia;

7\. authority dapat diidentifikasi;

8\. accountability dapat diidentifikasi;

9\. evidence requirement proporsional;

10\. governance review tersedia;

11\. decision dapat ditelusuri;

12\. conditional approval dapat dikontrol;

13\. rejection tetap tercatat;

14\. deferral tidak dianggap approval;

15\. cross-platform impact dapat ditangani;

16\. execution ownership tetap terpisah;

17\. verification diperlukan untuk material change;

18\. failed change dapat ditangani;

19\. partial change tidak dianggap complete;

20\. exception memiliki governance;

21\. irreversible change mendapat scrutiny;

22\. canonical change record tersedia;

23\. historical continuity dipertahankan;

24\. closure membutuhkan verification;

25\. escalation tersedia untuk authority ambiguity;

26\. F5 tidak mengambil alih F6 review;

27\. F5 tidak mengambil alih F7 control;

28\. F5 tidak mengambil alih F8 implementation;

29\. F5 tidak menciptakan authority baru;

30\. F5 tidak memperluas PD-03 domain boundary;

31\. unresolved source gaps ditandai;

32\. Claude Code memiliki explicit reconciliation directive.

⸻

56\. Canonical Governance Statement

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance establishes the governance mechanism through which material lifecycle changes of PD-03 Governance & Compliance are identified, classified, assessed, reviewed, authorized, transitioned, verified, recorded, escalated when necessary, and formally closed while preserving authority integrity, accountability, evidence sufficiency, lifecycle continuity, organizational ownership, and cross-platform boundaries.

Lifecycle change governance does not create new authority, organizational ownership, or execution responsibility. It governs the use of authority established elsewhere and ensures that material lifecycle change remains controlled, evidence-based, traceable, and verifiable.

F5 must remain distinct from F4 transition mechanics, F6 lifecycle review, F7 lifecycle control, and F8 lifecycle implementation.

⸻

\# 1. Purpose

F6 menetapkan architecture untuk melakukan review terhadap lifecycle condition, lifecycle integrity, lifecycle change, dan resulting lifecycle state PD-03.

Jika:

\`\`\`text

F4

Lifecycle Transition

menentukan bagaimana lifecycle subject berpindah antar kondisi,

dan:

F5

Lifecycle Change Governance

menentukan bagaimana lifecycle change dikendalikan,

maka:

F6

Lifecycle Review

menentukan bagaimana lifecycle condition dan lifecycle change dievaluasi secara terstruktur untuk memastikan bahwa lifecycle tetap:

• valid;

• governed;

• evidence-based;

• traceable;

• within authority;

• within organizational boundary;

• consistent with resulting decision;

• suitable untuk continued operation atau lifecycle action berikutnya.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

F6 merupakan child architecture dari F1.

Relationship:

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

F6 menggunakan output dari F1–F5.

F6 tidak boleh mengubah constitutional boundary secara informal.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Review Principle

Lifecycle Review shall determine whether the lifecycle condition, lifecycle transition, lifecycle decision, and resulting state remain valid, governed, evidenced, traceable, and consistent with applicable authority and architecture.

Lifecycle review bukan sekadar status check.

Review harus mampu menjawab:

What is the current lifecycle condition?

↓

Is it still valid?

↓

Is the lifecycle record complete?

↓

Is authority still applicable?

↓

Is the resulting condition consistent?

↓

Is further lifecycle action required?

⸻

4\. Lifecycle Review Boundary

F6 berada pada boundary:

Lifecycle Governance

↓

Lifecycle Review

↓

Lifecycle Finding

↓

Possible Change / Control / Improvement

F6 tidak otomatis melakukan change.

F6 menghasilkan:

• assessment;

• finding;

• review conclusion;

• recommendation;

• escalation trigger;

• lifecycle review record.

Decision tetap mengikuti applicable authority.

Review

≠

8\. Review Subject

Lifecycle Review dapat mengevaluasi:

• lifecycle subject;

• lifecycle state;

• lifecycle transition;

• lifecycle change;

• lifecycle decision;

• lifecycle authority;

• lifecycle accountability;

• lifecycle evidence;

• resulting state;

• lifecycle record;

• lifecycle exception;

• lifecycle dependency;

• lifecycle continuity.

Scope review harus dinyatakan secara eksplisit.

⸻

9\. Review Trigger

Lifecycle Review dapat dipicu oleh:

9.1 Scheduled Review

Review berdasarkan review interval yang telah ditentukan.

9.2 Event-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh material lifecycle event.

Contoh:

• creation;

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• dissolution;

• material organizational change.

9.3 Risk-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh material lifecycle risk.

9.4 Exception-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh lifecycle exception atau unresolved finding.

9.5 Evidence-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh evidence yang menunjukkan bahwa lifecycle condition mungkin tidak lagi valid.

9.6 Governance-Directed Review

Review yang diminta oleh applicable governance authority.

Catatan source-fidelity: taxonomy trigger di atas adalah bounded synthesis. Baseline mendukung keberadaan review sebagai bagian lifecycle governance, tetapi tidak memberikan taxonomy literal seluruh trigger tersebut.

⸻

10\. Review Frequency

Review frequency harus proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• lifecycle risk;

• organizational impact;

• dependency;

• reversibility;

• cross-platform impact;

• authority sensitivity;

• historical findings.

Tidak semua lifecycle subject membutuhkan frequency yang sama.

Jika review expiry atau review date berlaku, tanggal tersebut harus dapat ditelusuri dalam canonical record.

• decision berada dalam authority boundary.

Review tidak boleh memperluas authority.

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

27\. Review Conclusion

Review conclusion harus membedakan:

Non-Compliant

Unable to Conclude

Review conclusion tidak boleh melebihi evidence.

Lifecycle Review harus dieskalasikan apabila:

• authority ambiguous;

• constitutional boundary terancam;

• lifecycle state tidak dapat ditentukan;

• resulting state disputed;

• material cross-platform conflict terjadi;

• evidence material tidak tersedia;

• accountability tidak jelas;

• lifecycle record integrity terganggu;

• irreversible consequence belum terselesaikan.

Escalation mengikuti Part C.

47\. Review Integrity Rules

F6 menetapkan:

0\. Jangan review tanpa defined scope.

0\. Jangan conclude tanpa sufficient evidence.

0\. Jangan mengubah finding menjadi decision tanpa authority.

0\. Jangan menghapus historical review.

0\. Jangan menganggap absence of evidence sebagai evidence of compliance.

0\. Jangan menciptakan lifecycle state melalui review.

0\. Jangan mengambil ownership implementation.

0\. Jangan menggunakan review untuk memperluas authority.

0\. Jangan menyamakan finding dengan non-compliance tanpa applicable criterion.

0\. Jangan menutup material review tanpa disposition.

0\. Jangan mengabaikan unresolved material finding.

0\. Jangan mengubah historical conclusion tanpa traceable correction.

• reviewer tidak memiliki authority yang applicable;

• evidence tidak memadai;

• finding tidak traceable;

• conclusion tidak supported;

• material conflict tidak ditangani;

• required escalation tidak dilakukan;

• record tidak lengkap.

Review failure harus menghasilkan explicit review exception atau re-review requirement.

50\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline yang tersedia mendukung secara kuat bahwa:

• F6 merupakan bagian dari Part F;

• lifecycle governance mencakup review;

• lifecycle record dapat memiliki review/expiry;

• material lifecycle activity membutuhkan evidence, authority, decision, transition, verification, result, dan canonical record;

• lifecycle review harus berada dalam boundary lifecycle architecture;

• lifecycle change tetap harus dibedakan dari operating model.

Namun baseline tidak menyediakan definisi literal lengkap F6, termasuk:

• definitive review taxonomy;

• definitive review cadence;

• definitive finding severity;

• definitive review outcome vocabulary;

• definitive independence thresholds;

• definitive review authority matrix.

Karena itu detail tersebut ditandai:

BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS

dan tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai historical canonical fact tanpa source reconciliation.

54\. Canonical Lifecycle Review Statement

F6 — Lifecycle Review establishes the mechanism through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance evaluates lifecycle conditions, lifecycle changes, transitions, authority, evidence, accountability, resulting states, and lifecycle records to determine whether they remain valid, governed, traceable, and consistent with applicable architecture and authority.

Lifecycle Review produces assessment, findings, conclusions, recommendations, and follow-up requirements. It does not independently create lifecycle authority, authorize lifecycle change, create lifecycle states, or assume implementation ownership.

Material lifecycle review shall remain evidence-based, proportionate to materiality, traceable to its criteria and source evidence, and capable of preserving historical review continuity.

\# 1. Purpose

F6 menetapkan architecture untuk melakukan review terhadap lifecycle condition, lifecycle integrity, lifecycle change, dan resulting lifecycle state PD-03.

Jika:

\`\`\`text

F4

Lifecycle Transition

menentukan bagaimana lifecycle subject berpindah antar kondisi,

dan:

F5

Lifecycle Change Governance

menentukan bagaimana lifecycle change dikendalikan,

maka:

F6

Lifecycle Review

menentukan bagaimana lifecycle condition dan lifecycle change dievaluasi secara terstruktur untuk memastikan bahwa lifecycle tetap:

• valid;

• governed;

• evidence-based;

• traceable;

• within authority;

• within organizational boundary;

• consistent with resulting decision;

• suitable untuk continued operation atau lifecycle action berikutnya.

F6 tidak boleh mengubah constitutional boundary secara informal.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Review Principle

Lifecycle Review shall determine whether the lifecycle condition, lifecycle transition, lifecycle decision, and resulting state remain valid, governed, evidenced, traceable, and consistent with applicable authority and architecture.

Lifecycle review bukan sekadar status check.

Review harus mampu menjawab:

What is the current lifecycle condition?

↓

Is it still valid?

↓

Is the lifecycle record complete?

↓

Is authority still applicable?

↓

Is the resulting condition consistent?

↓

Is further lifecycle action required?

Possible Change / Control / Improvement

F6 tidak otomatis melakukan change.

F6 menghasilkan:

• assessment;

• finding;

• review conclusion;

• recommendation;

• escalation trigger;

• lifecycle review record.

Decision tetap mengikuti applicable authority.

Evaluate lifecycle condition

and transition outcome

F6 dapat memeriksa apakah transition yang telah dilakukan benar-benar menghasilkan resulting state yang sesuai.

⸻

7\. Lifecycle Review vs Lifecycle Control

F6 dan F7 harus tetap dibedakan.

F6

Lifecycle Review

↓

Evaluate

Assess

Find

Recommend

F7

Lifecycle Control

↓

Control

Monitor

Prevent

Detect

Correct

Review menghasilkan judgment.

Control menghasilkan atau menjalankan control mechanism.

⸻

8\. Review Subject

Lifecycle Review dapat mengevaluasi:

• lifecycle subject;

• lifecycle state;

• lifecycle transition;

• lifecycle change;

• lifecycle decision;

• lifecycle authority;

• lifecycle accountability;

• lifecycle evidence;

• resulting state;

• lifecycle record;

• lifecycle exception;

• lifecycle dependency;

• lifecycle continuity.

Scope review harus dinyatakan secara eksplisit.

⸻

9\. Review Trigger

Lifecycle Review dapat dipicu oleh:

9.1 Scheduled Review

Review berdasarkan review interval yang telah ditentukan.

9.2 Event-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh material lifecycle event.

Contoh:

• creation;

• restructuring;

• transition;

• retirement;

• dissolution;

• material organizational change.

9.3 Risk-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh material lifecycle risk.

9.4 Exception-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh lifecycle exception atau unresolved finding.

9.5 Evidence-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh evidence yang menunjukkan bahwa lifecycle condition mungkin tidak lagi valid.

9.6 Governance-Directed Review

Review yang diminta oleh applicable governance authority.

Catatan source-fidelity: taxonomy trigger di atas adalah bounded synthesis. Baseline mendukung keberadaan review sebagai bagian lifecycle governance, tetapi tidak memberikan taxonomy literal seluruh trigger tersebut.

⸻

10\. Review Frequency

Review frequency harus proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• lifecycle risk;

• organizational impact;

• dependency;

• reversibility;

• cross-platform impact;

• authority sensitivity;

• historical findings.

Tidak semua lifecycle subject membutuhkan frequency yang sama.

Jika review expiry atau review date berlaku, tanggal tersebut harus dapat ditelusuri dalam canonical record.

⸻

11\. Review Planning

Material Lifecycle Review harus memiliki review scope sebelum assessment dimulai.

Minimum:

Field Requirement

Review ID Required

Review Subject Required

Review Scope Required

Review Trigger Required

Review Owner Required

Review Authority Required when applicable

Review Date Required

Evidence Scope Required

Applicable Criteria Required

Expected Outcome Required

⸻

12\. Review Criteria

Lifecycle Review harus menggunakan criteria yang relevan terhadap subject.

Criteria dapat berasal dari:

• Lifecycle Constitution;

• Lifecycle Model;

• Lifecycle States;

• Lifecycle Transition;

• Lifecycle Change Governance;

• Governance Architecture;

• applicable authority;

• organizational architecture;

• compliance requirement;

• performance evidence;

• approved lifecycle decision.

Review criteria tidak boleh dibuat untuk menghasilkan predetermined conclusion.

⸻

13\. Review Evidence

Review harus menggunakan evidence yang relevan.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• lifecycle record;

• current state evidence;

• transition evidence;

• authority record;

• decision record;

• accountability record;

• implementation evidence;

• verification result;

• compliance evidence;

• performance evidence;

• dependency evidence;

• exception record.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri ke review conclusion.

⸻

14\. Evidence Sufficiency

Review harus menentukan apakah evidence cukup untuk menghasilkan conclusion.

Model:

Evidence

↓

Evidence Sufficiency

↓

Sufficient / Insufficient

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Review Limitation

↓

Additional Evidence / Escalation

Review tidak boleh mengubah evidence gap menjadi false certainty.

⸻

15\. Review Analysis

Lifecycle Review harus membandingkan:

Expected Lifecycle Condition

-

Actual Lifecycle Condition

-

Evidence

↓

Review Assessment

Analysis harus mempertimbangkan:

• state correctness;

• authority validity;

• accountability;

• transition correctness;

• resulting state;

• dependency;

• compliance;

• continuity;

• boundary integrity.

⸻

16\. Lifecycle State Review

Review terhadap lifecycle state harus memastikan bahwa:

• state dapat diidentifikasi;

• state sesuai dengan lifecycle model;

• state memiliki evidence;

• state konsisten dengan transition history;

• owner dapat diidentifikasi;

• state tidak bertentangan dengan canonical record.

Jika state ambiguity ditemukan:

State Ambiguity

↓

Review Finding

↓

Resolution / Escalation

F6 tidak menciptakan state baru secara informal.

⸻

17\. Transition Review

Material transition harus dapat direview terhadap:

1\. source state;

2\. target state;

3\. rationale;

4\. authority;

5\. evidence;

6\. dependency;

7\. execution;

8\. verification;

9\. resulting state;

10\. canonical record.

Canonical review chain:

Source State

↓

Transition

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

Review memastikan chain tersebut konsisten.

⸻

18\. Change Review

Lifecycle change review harus menilai apakah:

• change classification benar;

• applicable authority valid;

• evidence cukup;

• impact assessment tersedia;

• dependencies ditangani;

• transition mechanism sesuai;

• resulting state valid;

• canonical record lengkap.

F6 tidak menggantikan F5 change governance.

⸻

19\. Authority Review

Lifecycle Review harus memeriksa authority apabila authority merupakan bagian material dari review scope.

Minimum:

• authority exists;

• authority is applicable;

• authority scope is sufficient;

• delegation valid jika digunakan;

• decision berada dalam authority boundary.

Review tidak boleh memperluas authority.

⸻

20\. Accountability Review

Review harus memeriksa apakah:

• accountable owner identifiable;

• execution owner identifiable;

• authority owner identifiable apabila diperlukan;

• responsibility tidak ambiguous;

• resulting outcome memiliki accountable owner.

Model:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Review tidak boleh menggabungkan ketiganya hanya demi simplifikasi.

⸻

21\. Organizational Boundary Review

F6 harus memastikan lifecycle change tidak menyebabkan organizational drift.

Review dapat memeriksa:

• organizational boundary;

• responsibility allocation;

• capability arrangement;

• reporting relationship;

• Platform ownership;

• cross-platform dependency.

Lifecycle Review tidak boleh menjadikan PD-03 owner atas domain Platform lain.

⸻

22\. Cross-Platform Review

Jika lifecycle subject memiliki cross-platform impact, review harus memeriksa:

• affected Platform;

• dependency;

• authority relationship;

• coordination;

• ownership;

• execution responsibility;

• resulting impact.

Model:

PD-03 Lifecycle Review

↓

Cross-Platform Impact

↓

Applicable Platform Owners

↓

Coordination / Governance

Cross-platform review tidak memindahkan ownership.

⸻

23\. Performance Evidence in Lifecycle Review

Performance evidence dapat menjadi input Lifecycle Review.

Performance Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Review

↓

Finding

↓

Possible Lifecycle Action

Performance evidence tidak otomatis menentukan lifecycle outcome.

Performance signal harus diinterpretasikan dalam lifecycle context.

⸻

24\. Compliance Evidence in Lifecycle Review

Compliance evidence dapat menjadi review input.

Compliance Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Review

↓

Compliance Finding

↓

Lifecycle Implication

Compliance finding tidak otomatis mengubah lifecycle state.

Applicable authority tetap diperlukan.

⸻

25\. Review Finding

Material finding harus memiliki:

Field Requirement

Finding ID Required

Review ID Required

Subject Required

Condition Required

Criterion Required

Evidence Required

Impact Required when material

Severity / Materiality Required when applicable

Owner Required

Recommendation Required when applicable

Disposition Required

Status Required

⸻

26\. Finding Classification

Finding dapat diklasifikasikan sebagai:

Compliant

Observation

Minor Gap

Material Gap

Critical Gap

Unresolved

Source-fidelity qualification: taxonomy di atas adalah bounded synthesis, bukan literal source taxonomy.

Claude Code harus mereconcile dengan canonical finding vocabulary jika tersedia.

⸻

27\. Review Conclusion

Review conclusion harus membedakan:

Confirmed

Evidence cukup dan condition sesuai criteria.

Partially Confirmed

Sebagian evidence atau condition belum lengkap.

Not Confirmed

Evidence tidak cukup untuk mengkonfirmasi condition.

Non-Compliant

Condition tidak memenuhi applicable requirement.

Unable to Conclude

Evidence atau authority context tidak cukup.

Review conclusion tidak boleh melebihi evidence.

⸻

28\. Recommendation

Recommendation harus dibedakan dari decision.

Review Finding

↓

Recommendation

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Decision

F6 dapat memberikan recommendation.

F6 tidak otomatis menjadi decision authority.

⸻

29\. Review Escalation

Lifecycle Review harus dieskalasikan apabila:

• authority ambiguous;

• constitutional boundary terancam;

• lifecycle state tidak dapat ditentukan;

• resulting state disputed;

• material cross-platform conflict terjadi;

• evidence material tidak tersedia;

• accountability tidak jelas;

• lifecycle record integrity terganggu;

• irreversible consequence belum terselesaikan.

Escalation mengikuti Part C.

⸻

30\. Review Exception

Jika normal review mechanism tidak dapat dilakukan:

1\. exception harus dicatat;

2\. limitation harus dijelaskan;

3\. authority harus diketahui;

4\. owner harus ditetapkan;

5\. residual risk harus diketahui jika applicable;

6\. follow-up harus ditentukan.

Review exception tidak boleh dihilangkan dari history.

⸻

31\. Review Closure

Lifecycle Review dapat ditutup apabila:

• review scope selesai;

• evidence telah dinilai;

• findings telah dicatat;

• conclusion telah ditetapkan;

• recommendation telah dicatat jika applicable;

• escalation telah diselesaikan atau disposition ditentukan;

• review record lengkap.

Review Scope

↓

Evidence

↓

Analysis

↓

Finding

↓

Conclusion

↓

Disposition

↓

Closure

⸻

32\. Review Follow-Up

Material findings harus memiliki follow-up mechanism.

Follow-up dapat menghasilkan:

Corrective Action

↓

Control Change

↓

Lifecycle Change

↓

Improvement

↓

Re-review

F6 tidak mengambil ownership atas implementation follow-up apabila implementation berada pada domain lain.

⸻

33\. Re-Review

Re-review diperlukan apabila:

• material finding belum resolved;

• corrective action selesai;

• lifecycle transition baru terjadi;

• evidence berubah;

• review conclusion menjadi obsolete;

• authority berubah;

• lifecycle condition berubah secara material.

Re-review harus mempertahankan reference terhadap original review.

⸻

34\. Review History

Lifecycle Review harus mempertahankan historical continuity.

Record tidak boleh menghapus:

• previous review;

• previous finding;

• previous conclusion;

• previous recommendation;

• previous disposition.

Correction harus traceable.

⸻

35\. Review Independence

Review independence harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Jika review bersifat material atau memiliki conflict of interest:

Review Subject

↓

Independent / Appropriate Reviewer

↓

Review Conclusion

Reviewer tidak boleh memvalidasi hasilnya sendiri apabila applicable governance mensyaratkan independence.

⸻

36\. Review Materiality

Review depth harus proporsional terhadap:

• lifecycle materiality;

• organizational impact;

• authority sensitivity;

• risk;

• reversibility;

• dependency;

• cross-platform impact;

• compliance consequence.

Model:

Low Materiality

↓

Proportionate Review

High Materiality

↓

Enhanced Review

F6 tidak menetapkan numerical thresholds tanpa source-backed basis.

⸻

47\. Review Integrity Rules

F6 menetapkan:

1\. Jangan review tanpa defined scope.

2\. Jangan conclude tanpa sufficient evidence.

3\. Jangan mengubah finding menjadi decision tanpa authority.

4\. Jangan menghapus historical review.

5\. Jangan menganggap absence of evidence sebagai evidence of compliance.

6\. Jangan menciptakan lifecycle state melalui review.

7\. Jangan mengambil ownership implementation.

8\. Jangan menggunakan review untuk memperluas authority.

9\. Jangan menyamakan finding dengan non-compliance tanpa applicable criterion.

10\. Jangan menutup material review tanpa disposition.

11\. Jangan mengabaikan unresolved material finding.

12\. Jangan mengubah historical conclusion tanpa traceable correction.

⸻

48\. Lifecycle Review Failure

Review dianggap gagal secara governance apabila:

• scope tidak jelas;

• reviewer tidak memiliki authority yang applicable;

• evidence tidak memadai;

• finding tidak traceable;

• conclusion tidak supported;

• material conflict tidak ditangani;

• required escalation tidak dilakukan;

• record tidak lengkap.

Review failure harus menghasilkan explicit review exception atau re-review requirement.

⸻

49\. Review Quality

Review quality ditentukan oleh:

• evidence quality;

• criteria clarity;

• analytical rigor;

• reviewer suitability;

• traceability;

• independence where applicable;

• conclusion integrity;

• follow-up completeness.

Review quality bukan sekadar completion rate.

⸻

50\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline yang tersedia mendukung secara kuat bahwa:

• F6 merupakan bagian dari Part F;

• lifecycle governance mencakup review;

• lifecycle record dapat memiliki review/expiry;

• material lifecycle activity membutuhkan evidence, authority, decision, transition, verification, result, dan canonical record;

• lifecycle review harus berada dalam boundary lifecycle architecture;

• lifecycle change tetap harus dibedakan dari operating model.

Namun baseline tidak menyediakan definisi literal lengkap F6, termasuk:

• definitive review taxonomy;

• definitive review cadence;

• definitive finding severity;

• definitive review outcome vocabulary;

• definitive independence thresholds;

• definitive review authority matrix.

Karena itu detail tersebut ditandai:

BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS

dan tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai historical canonical fact tanpa source reconciliation.

⸻

51\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation untuk:

1\. definitive F6 review semantics;

2\. lifecycle review cadence;

3\. review trigger taxonomy;

4\. review authority;

5\. review independence;

6\. finding classification;

7\. review conclusion taxonomy;

8\. lifecycle review record schema;

9\. relationship antara F6 dan native AIOS review mechanisms;

10\. relationship antara F6 dan Governance Review pada Part C.

Claude Code harus:

• search seluruh canonical corpus;

• search PD-03 baseline;

• search F1–F5;

• search Governance Review mechanisms;

• search Architecture Review Log;

• search existing lifecycle review artifacts;

• preserve source-backed terminology;

• document any bounded synthesis;

• create ADR untuk material semantic decision;

• maintain historical traceability.

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• menciptakan authority baru melalui F6;

• menjadikan review sebagai approval;

• menjadikan finding sebagai automatic lifecycle change;

• membuat lifecycle state baru tanpa applicable authority;

• mengambil ownership implementation;

• menghapus historical review;

• menggunakan review untuk memperluas PD-03 domain boundary.

Jika ambiguity berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh Founder Reserved Authority, Constitution, Canonical Architecture supremacy, atau fundamental governance boundary, Claude Code wajib melakukan escalation.

⸻

52\. Success Criteria

F6 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. lifecycle review memiliki purpose yang jelas;

2\. review dibedakan dari change governance;

3\. review dibedakan dari transition;

4\. review dibedakan dari control;

5\. review scope dapat ditentukan;

6\. review trigger dapat ditentukan;

7\. review criteria dapat ditentukan;

8\. evidence requirement jelas;

9\. evidence sufficiency dapat dinilai;

10\. lifecycle state dapat direview;

11\. transition dapat direview;

12\. authority dapat direview;

13\. accountability dapat direview;

14\. organizational boundary dapat direview;

15\. cross-platform impact dapat direview;

16\. performance evidence dapat digunakan sebagai input;

17\. compliance evidence dapat digunakan sebagai input;

18\. findings dapat direkam;

19\. conclusions tidak melebihi evidence;

20\. recommendation dibedakan dari decision;

21\. escalation mechanism tersedia;

22\. review closure memiliki explicit requirements;

23\. material findings memiliki follow-up;

24\. re-review mechanism tersedia;

25\. historical review continuity dipertahankan;

26\. review independence dapat diterapkan bila diperlukan;

27\. review materiality dapat menentukan depth;

28\. canonical review record tersedia;

29\. F6 tidak menciptakan authority;

30\. F6 tidak mengambil ownership implementation;

31\. F6 tidak memperluas PD-03 domain;

32\. unresolved source gaps ditandai;

33\. Claude Code memiliki explicit source-reconciliation directive.

54\. Canonical Lifecycle Review Statement

F6 — Lifecycle Review establishes the mechanism through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance evaluates lifecycle conditions, lifecycle changes, transitions, authority, evidence, accountability, resulting states, and lifecycle records to determine whether they remain valid, governed, traceable, and consistent with applicable architecture and authority.

Lifecycle Review produces assessment, findings, conclusions, recommendations, and follow-up requirements. It does not independently create lifecycle authority, authorize lifecycle change, create lifecycle states, or assume implementation ownership.

Material lifecycle review shall remain evidence-based, proportionate to materiality, traceable to its criteria and source evidence, and capable of preserving historical review continuity.

F7

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part F — Lifecycle Architecture

\# F7 — Lifecycle Control

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* F7

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Lifecycle Control

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-03

\> \*\*Official Name:\*\* Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Volume:\*\* Volume 3

\> \*\*Part:\*\* Part F — Lifecycle Architecture
