\> \*\*Primary Dependencies:\*\* F1 — Lifecycle Constitution; F2 — Lifecycle Model; F3 — Lifecycle States; F4 — Lifecycle Transition; F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance; F6 — Lifecycle Review; Part C — Governance Architecture; Part D — Governance Operations; Part E — Performance Architecture

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Classification:\*\* Lifecycle Control Architecture

\> \*\*Source-Fidelity Mode:\*\* Bounded Canonical Synthesis

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION

1\. Purpose

F7 menetapkan architecture untuk mengendalikan material lifecycle activity agar lifecycle change berlangsung dalam boundary yang sah, terotorisasi, tervalidasi, terdokumentasi, dan dapat ditelusuri.

F7 menjawab pertanyaan:

Bagaimana lifecycle change dikendalikan agar tidak keluar dari authority, state, transition, evidence, dan organizational boundary yang berlaku?

F7 bukan lifecycle decision authority.

F7 bukan lifecycle implementation.

F7 bukan lifecycle review.

Relationship:

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

↓

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

F7 merupakan child architecture dari F1.

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

F7 mewarisi constitutional constraints dari F1–F6.

F7 tidak boleh:

• menciptakan lifecycle authority baru;

• menciptakan lifecycle state baru;

• menggantikan lifecycle decision;

• mengambil ownership implementation;

• memperluas organizational boundary PD-03.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Lifecycle Control Principle

Material lifecycle change shall be controlled through authorization, state validation, transition validation, evidence, traceability, review, exception handling, and canonical recording.

Control harus memastikan bahwa lifecycle change tidak bergerak dari:

Intent

langsung menjadi:

Result

tanpa governance control chain.

Canonical chain:

Lifecycle Intent

↓

Authorization

↓

State Validation

↓

Transition Validation

↓

Evidence

↓

Controlled Action

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Canonical Record

Prinsip ini secara eksplisit didukung oleh baseline.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

4\. Control Boundary

F7 mengendalikan lifecycle integrity, bukan seluruh operational activity.

Boundary:

Lifecycle Governance

↓

Lifecycle Control

↓

Lifecycle Integrity

sedangkan:

Operational Execution

↓

Applicable Domain Owner

Governance control tidak otomatis menjadi operational execution.

⸻

5\. Control vs Review

F6:

Review

↓

Assess

Find

Conclude

Recommend

F7:

Control

↓

Prevent

Validate

Constrain

Detect

Record

Escalate

Dengan demikian:

Review ≠ Control

F6 dapat menemukan control weakness.

F7 menyediakan control mechanism untuk menjaga lifecycle integrity.

⸻

6\. Control vs Change Governance

F5 menentukan bagaimana lifecycle change governed.

F7 memastikan bahwa applicable governance requirement tersebut terwujud sebagai control.

F5

Change Governance

↓

What must be governed

↓

F7

Lifecycle Control

↓

How lifecycle integrity is controlled

F7 tidak menggantikan F5.

⸻

7\. Control vs Implementation

F7 menetapkan control requirement.

F8 menjalankan lifecycle implementation.

F7

Control Requirement

↓

F8

Authorized Implementation

↓

Result

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih execution domain hanya karena lifecycle control berlaku terhadap domain tersebut.

⸻

8\. Control Scope

Lifecycle Control dapat mencakup:

• authorization control;

• state control;

• transition control;

• evidence control;

• traceability control;

• review control;

• exception control;

• record control;

• boundary control;

• accountability control;

• verification control;

• escalation control.

Source-fidelity qualification: daftar taxonomy tersebut merupakan bounded synthesis dari control principles yang tersedia, bukan taxonomy literal lengkap dari baseline.

⸻

9\. Authorization Control

Material lifecycle activity harus memiliki applicable authority.

Control harus memastikan:

• authority dapat diidentifikasi;

• scope authority sesuai;

• authority masih berlaku;

• delegation valid apabila digunakan;

• action berada dalam authority boundary.

Model:

Lifecycle Action

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Scope Validation

↓

Authorized / Not Authorized

F7 tidak menciptakan authority.

⸻

10\. State Validation Control

Lifecycle action harus diverifikasi terhadap current state.

Control harus mencegah:

Wrong Current State

↓

Invalid Transition

Minimum validation:

• current state identifiable;

• current state valid;

• current state sesuai canonical record;

• lifecycle subject benar;

• transition berasal dari state yang applicable.

⸻

11\. Transition Validation Control

Sebelum material transition dilakukan, control harus memastikan:

0\. target state valid;

0\. transition diperbolehkan;

0\. authority tersedia;

0\. prerequisites terpenuhi;

0\. evidence tersedia;

0\. dependencies diketahui;

0\. resulting state dapat diverifikasi.

Model:

Current State

↓

Transition Rules

↓

Prerequisites

↓

Authorization

↓

Valid Transition

Detail transition matrix tetap berada pada F4.

F7 hanya mengendalikan compliance terhadap matrix tersebut.

⸻

12\. Evidence Control

Lifecycle control harus memastikan evidence tersedia sesuai materiality.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• authorization;

• assessment;

• current state;

• target state;

• rationale;

• impact assessment;

• transition evidence;

• verification;

• resulting state;

• exception;

• disposition.

Evidence tidak boleh dibuat setelah fakta hanya untuk memenuhi record requirement.

⸻

13\. Evidence Sufficiency Control

Control harus dapat membedakan:

Evidence Available

dengan:

Evidence Sufficient

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Control Hold / Exception / Escalation

Control tidak boleh menganggap absence of evidence sebagai compliance.

⸻

14\. Traceability Control

Material lifecycle action harus memiliki traceability.

Minimum chain:

Lifecycle Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Result

Setiap link material harus dapat direconstruct.

Baseline secara eksplisit mensyaratkan lifecycle traceability.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

15\. Decision Reference Control

Lifecycle action harus memiliki reference terhadap applicable decision apabila decision diperlukan.

Control harus mencegah:

Implementation

↓

without

↓

Required Decision

Decision reference harus dapat ditelusuri ke:

• decision ID;

• authority;

• scope;

• date;

• applicable lifecycle subject.

⸻

16\. Accountability Control

Control harus memastikan accountable owner dapat diidentifikasi.

Distinction:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Jika accountability tidak dapat ditentukan untuk material lifecycle action:

Ambiguous Accountability

↓

Control Exception

↓

Resolution / Escalation

⸻

17\. Boundary Control

F7 harus menjaga organizational dan Platform boundary.

Control harus mendeteksi apabila lifecycle change:

• memperluas domain PD-03 secara informal;

• memindahkan ownership Platform lain;

• mengubah authority tanpa applicable decision;

• mengambil alih execution domain;

• mengubah architecture ownership tanpa authority.

Dependency tidak boleh berubah menjadi ownership.

Dependency ≠ Ownership

⸻

18\. Cross-Platform Control

Material lifecycle change yang berdampak pada Platform lain harus menggunakan applicable coordination mechanism.

Control harus memeriksa:

• affected Platform;

• dependency;

• impact;

• authority;

• coordination;

• ownership;

• evidence;

• resulting state.

Model:

PD-03 Lifecycle Change

↓

Cross-Platform Impact

↓

Coordination

↓

Applicable Platform Owners

↓

Controlled Change

PD-03 tidak memperoleh ownership atas Platform yang terdampak.

⸻

19\. Control of Irreversible Transition

Baseline menyatakan bahwa reversibility tidak selalu diwajibkan.

Jika transition irreversible:

• irreversibility harus diketahui sebelum authorization;

• consequence harus dinilai;

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• resulting state harus dapat diverifikasi.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian:

Irreversible Transition

↓

Enhanced Control Attention

F7 tidak boleh memaksakan reversibility ketika source atau technical reality tidak memungkinkan.

⸻

20\. Lifecycle Exception Control

Exception dapat terjadi apabila normal transition mechanism tidak dapat digunakan.

Exception harus:

0\. diidentifikasi;

0\. diklasifikasikan;

0\. memiliki rationale;

0\. memiliki owner;

0\. memiliki applicable authority;

0\. memiliki mitigation;

0\. memiliki disposition;

0\. dicatat;

0\. direview apabila material.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Exception tidak boleh menjadi shortcut untuk menghindari governance.

⸻

21\. Exception Control Lifecycle

Canonical exception lifecycle:

Exception Detected

↓

Classification

↓

Rationale

↓

Authority

↓

Mitigation

↓

Disposition

↓

Record

↓

Review if Material

↓

Closure

Unresolved material exception tidak boleh diam-diam dianggap closed.

⸻

22\. Control Hold

Apabila mandatory control condition tidak terpenuhi, lifecycle action dapat ditempatkan pada control hold apabila applicable.

Contoh:

Missing Authority

↓

CONTROL HOLD

Invalid State

↓

CONTROL HOLD

Insufficient Evidence

↓

CONTROL HOLD

Control hold bukan rejection permanen.

Control hold berarti lifecycle action belum memenuhi required condition untuk melanjutkan.

⸻

23\. Control Override

Control override hanya dapat dilakukan apabila applicable governance mengizinkannya.

Override harus memiliki:

• authority;

• rationale;

• scope;

• duration;

• owner;

• risk consideration;

• record;

• review apabila material.

F7 tidak boleh membuat override mechanism baru tanpa source-backed authority.

⸻

24\. Control Failure

Control failure terjadi apabila control yang seharusnya mencegah, mendeteksi, atau membatasi lifecycle risk tidak bekerja sebagaimana dimaksud.

Control failure harus menghasilkan:

Control Failure

↓

Finding

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Corrective Action / Escalation

↓

Re-control / Re-review

Control failure tidak boleh dihapus hanya karena lifecycle action akhirnya berhasil.

⸻

25\. Control Effectiveness

Control effectiveness harus dievaluasi terhadap intended control objective.

Pertanyaan minimum:

• apakah control exists?

• apakah control applicable?

• apakah control operated?

• apakah control evidence tersedia?

• apakah control prevented/detected the intended condition?

• apakah exception ditangani?

• apakah control failure terjadi?

Source-fidelity qualification: model effectiveness di atas adalah bounded synthesis. Baseline mendukung control dan lifecycle integrity, tetapi belum menyediakan definitive control effectiveness taxonomy.

⸻

26\. Preventive Control

Preventive control berfungsi mencegah lifecycle action yang invalid sebelum terjadi.

Contoh:

Invalid Authority

↓

BLOCK

Invalid State

↓

BLOCK

Missing Prerequisite

↓

BLOCK

Preventive control harus digunakan jika feasible dan proporsional.

⸻

27\. Detective Control

Detective control mengidentifikasi lifecycle integrity issue setelah atau selama lifecycle activity.

Contoh:

Transition Executed

↓

State Verification

↓

Mismatch Detected

Detection harus menghasilkan finding atau exception apabila material.

⸻

28\. Corrective Control

Corrective control digunakan untuk menangani control failure atau lifecycle integrity issue.

Finding

↓

Corrective Action

↓

Verification

↓

Closure

Corrective action tidak boleh dianggap selesai hanya karena action telah dilakukan.

Result harus diverifikasi.

⸻

29\. Control Materiality

Control harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Materiality dapat mempertimbangkan:

• organizational impact;

• authority sensitivity;

• lifecycle consequence;

• reversibility;

• compliance impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• dependency;

• operational impact;

• historical significance.

Baseline secara eksplisit menyatakan bahwa lifecycle control harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

F7 tidak menetapkan numerical threshold tanpa source-backed basis.

⸻

30\. Control Segregation

Jika control objective membutuhkan independence, control responsibility harus dipisahkan dari incompatible execution responsibility.

Contoh:

Control Definition

≠

Control Self-Validation

apabila independence material.

Namun F7 tidak secara otomatis mewajibkan physical organizational separation untuk setiap control.

Segregation harus proporsional terhadap risk dan governance requirement.

⸻

31\. Control Ownership

Control ownership harus dapat diidentifikasi.

Minimum distinction:

Control Owner

↓

Accountable for Control Design / Integrity

Control Operator

↓

Executes Control

Control Reviewer

↓

Assesses Control when required

Satu person/unit dapat memiliki lebih dari satu role hanya jika tidak menciptakan incompatible conflict.

Source-fidelity qualification: role taxonomy ini merupakan bounded synthesis dan harus direconcile dengan canonical governance vocabulary oleh Claude Code.

⸻

32\. Control Evidence

Setiap material control operation harus menghasilkan evidence yang cukup untuk membuktikan:

• control operated;

• applicable condition;

• control result;

• exception jika ada;

• reviewer jika required;

• timestamp/effective context jika material.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri ke lifecycle action.

43\. Control Escalation

Escalation diperlukan apabila:

• mandatory control condition gagal;

• authority conflict terjadi;

• material exception unresolved;

• lifecycle state tidak valid;

• transition integrity disputed;

• cross-platform boundary terancam;

• control failure memiliki material consequence;

• evidence material hilang;

• control override membutuhkan authority yang tidak tersedia.

Escalation mengikuti applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

44\. Control Closure

Lifecycle control issue dapat ditutup apabila:

0\. issue telah diidentifikasi;

0\. owner telah ditetapkan;

0\. required action telah dilakukan;

0\. evidence tersedia;

0\. verification selesai;

0\. residual issue diketahui;

0\. applicable reviewer menyetujui closure apabila diperlukan;

0\. canonical record diperbarui.

Issue

↓

Action

↓

Evidence

↓

Verification

↓

Closure

⸻

45\. Control Continuity

Lifecycle transition tidak boleh menghapus control history secara otomatis.

Retirement, restructuring, atau dissolution harus mempertahankan historical control evidence sesuai applicable retention requirements.

Historical continuity merupakan bagian dari lifecycle integrity.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

46\. Control Integrity Rules

F7 menetapkan:

0\. Jangan mengizinkan material lifecycle action tanpa applicable authority.

0\. Jangan mengizinkan invalid state transition.

0\. Jangan menganggap evidence yang tidak cukup sebagai evidence yang valid.

0\. Jangan menghapus lifecycle control history.

0\. Jangan mengubah exception menjadi normal path secara informal.

0\. Jangan menggunakan control untuk menciptakan authority baru.

0\. Jangan menggunakan governance control untuk mengambil alih execution.

0\. Jangan melewati mandatory control hanya karena operational urgency.

0\. Jangan menutup control issue tanpa verification yang memadai.

0\. Jangan mengubah dependency menjadi ownership.

0\. Jangan menganggap control success sebagai lifecycle success.

0\. Jangan mengorbankan lifecycle integrity demi kecepatan perubahan.

↓

Automation dapat digunakan untuk:

• state validation;

• record validation;

• evidence collection;

• traceability;

• control execution;

• detection;

• notification;

• escalation.

Automation tidak otomatis memperoleh authority.

Automation

≠

Authority

Jika automated control menghasilkan material decision, applicable human/governance authority requirement tetap berlaku.

AI agent dapat membantu:

• evidence collection;

• consistency checking;

• control monitoring;

• anomaly detection;

AI agent tidak boleh memperoleh lifecycle authority hanya karena menjalankan control.

Authority harus berasal dari applicable governance model.

Material lifecycle control harus observable apabila practical.

Observability dapat mencakup:

• control execution status;

• control failure;

• exception;

• overdue control;

• unresolved finding;

• missing evidence;

• state mismatch.

Control observability membantu lifecycle review dan governance assurance.

57\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation terhadap:

0\. PD-03 Governance Baseline;

0\. F1–F6;

0\. Governance Control architecture pada Part C;

0\. Governance Operations pada Part D;

0\. Performance measurement architecture pada Part E;

0\. canonical AIOS control vocabulary;

0\. existing exception mechanisms;

0\. existing authority/delegation mechanisms;

0\. existing lifecycle implementation mechanisms;

Claude Code harus menentukan:

• apakah control taxonomy sudah ada;

• apakah control ownership sudah canonical;

• apakah emergency change mechanism sudah ada;

• apakah automated control memiliki defined authority boundary;

• apakah lifecycle control metrics telah didefinisikan di Performance Architecture.

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• membuat authority baru;

• membuat control ownership yang bertentangan dengan organizational architecture;

• mengubah control menjadi implementation ownership;

• menjadikan automation sebagai autonomous authority;

• mengadopsi control taxonomy eksternal tanpa reconciliation;

• menghapus source gap;

• menyamarkan bounded synthesis sebagai historical canonical fact.

Jika ambiguity dapat diselesaikan melalui delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh constitutional authority, Founder Reserved Authority, Canonical Architecture supremacy, atau fundamental governance boundary, Claude Code wajib melakukan escalation.

⸻

60\. Canonical Lifecycle Control Statement

F7 — Lifecycle Control establishes the control architecture through which material PD-03 lifecycle activities are constrained, validated, evidenced, traced, reviewed, exception-handled, and canonically recorded so that lifecycle integrity is preserved throughout authorization, state transition, implementation, verification, and resulting-state establishment.

Lifecycle Control does not create authority, replace lifecycle review, authorize lifecycle change independently, or assume implementation ownership. It provides the control boundary necessary to ensure that authorized lifecycle change remains within applicable state, transition, evidence, accountability, organizational, and cross-platform constraints.

Lifecycle Control shall remain proportionate to lifecycle materiality and shall preserve historical traceability and canonical lifecycle records.

1\. Purpose

F7 menetapkan architecture untuk mengendalikan material lifecycle activity agar lifecycle change berlangsung dalam boundary yang sah, terotorisasi, tervalidasi, terdokumentasi, dan dapat ditelusuri.

F7 menjawab pertanyaan:

Bagaimana lifecycle change dikendalikan agar tidak keluar dari authority, state, transition, evidence, dan organizational boundary yang berlaku?

F7 bukan lifecycle decision authority.

F7 bukan lifecycle implementation.

F7 bukan lifecycle review.

Relationship:

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

↓

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

F7 merupakan child architecture dari F1.

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

F7 mewarisi constitutional constraints dari F1–F6.

F7 tidak boleh:

• menciptakan lifecycle authority baru;

• menciptakan lifecycle state baru;

• menggantikan lifecycle decision;

• mengambil ownership implementation;

• memperluas organizational boundary PD-03.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Lifecycle Control Principle

Material lifecycle change shall be controlled through authorization, state validation, transition validation, evidence, traceability, review, exception handling, and canonical recording.

Control harus memastikan bahwa lifecycle change tidak bergerak dari:

Intent

langsung menjadi:

Result

tanpa governance control chain.

Canonical chain:

Lifecycle Intent

↓

Authorization

↓

State Validation

↓

Transition Validation

↓

Evidence

↓

Controlled Action

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Canonical Record

Prinsip ini secara eksplisit didukung oleh baseline.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

4\. Control Boundary

F7 mengendalikan lifecycle integrity, bukan seluruh operational activity.

Boundary:

Lifecycle Governance

↓

Lifecycle Control

↓

Lifecycle Integrity

sedangkan:

Operational Execution

↓

Applicable Domain Owner

Governance control tidak otomatis menjadi operational execution.

⸻

5\. Control vs Review

F6:

Review

↓

Assess

Find

Conclude

Recommend

F7:

Control

↓

Prevent

Validate

Constrain

Detect

Record

Escalate

Dengan demikian:

Review ≠ Control

F6 dapat menemukan control weakness.

F7 menyediakan control mechanism untuk menjaga lifecycle integrity.

⸻

6\. Control vs Change Governance

F5 menentukan bagaimana lifecycle change governed.

F7 memastikan bahwa applicable governance requirement tersebut terwujud sebagai control.

F5

Change Governance

↓

What must be governed

↓

F7

Lifecycle Control

↓

How lifecycle integrity is controlled

F7 tidak menggantikan F5.

⸻

7\. Control vs Implementation

F7 menetapkan control requirement.

F8 menjalankan lifecycle implementation.

F7

Control Requirement

↓

F8

Authorized Implementation

↓

Result

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih execution domain hanya karena lifecycle control berlaku terhadap domain tersebut.

⸻

8\. Control Scope

Lifecycle Control dapat mencakup:

• authorization control;

• state control;

• transition control;

• evidence control;

• traceability control;

• review control;

• exception control;

• record control;

• boundary control;

• accountability control;

• verification control;

• escalation control.

Source-fidelity qualification: daftar taxonomy tersebut merupakan bounded synthesis dari control principles yang tersedia, bukan taxonomy literal lengkap dari baseline.

⸻

9\. Authorization Control

Material lifecycle activity harus memiliki applicable authority.

Control harus memastikan:

• authority dapat diidentifikasi;

• scope authority sesuai;

• authority masih berlaku;

• delegation valid apabila digunakan;

• action berada dalam authority boundary.

Model:

Lifecycle Action

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Scope Validation

↓

Authorized / Not Authorized

F7 tidak menciptakan authority.

⸻

10\. State Validation Control

Lifecycle action harus diverifikasi terhadap current state.

Control harus mencegah:

Wrong Current State

↓

Invalid Transition

Minimum validation:

• current state identifiable;

• current state valid;

• current state sesuai canonical record;

• lifecycle subject benar;

• transition berasal dari state yang applicable.

⸻

11\. Transition Validation Control

Sebelum material transition dilakukan, control harus memastikan:

1\. target state valid;

2\. transition diperbolehkan;

3\. authority tersedia;

4\. prerequisites terpenuhi;

5\. evidence tersedia;

6\. dependencies diketahui;

7\. resulting state dapat diverifikasi.

Model:

Current State

↓

Transition Rules

↓

Prerequisites

↓

Authorization

↓

Valid Transition

Detail transition matrix tetap berada pada F4.

F7 hanya mengendalikan compliance terhadap matrix tersebut.

⸻

12\. Evidence Control

Lifecycle control harus memastikan evidence tersedia sesuai materiality.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• authorization;

• assessment;

• current state;

• target state;

• rationale;

• impact assessment;

• transition evidence;

• verification;

• resulting state;

• exception;

• disposition.

Evidence tidak boleh dibuat setelah fakta hanya untuk memenuhi record requirement.

⸻

13\. Evidence Sufficiency Control

Control harus dapat membedakan:

Evidence Available

dengan:

Evidence Sufficient

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Control Hold / Exception / Escalation

Control tidak boleh menganggap absence of evidence sebagai compliance.

⸻

14\. Traceability Control

Material lifecycle action harus memiliki traceability.

Minimum chain:

Lifecycle Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Result

Setiap link material harus dapat direconstruct.

Baseline secara eksplisit mensyaratkan lifecycle traceability.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

15\. Decision Reference Control

Lifecycle action harus memiliki reference terhadap applicable decision apabila decision diperlukan.

Control harus mencegah:

Implementation

↓

without

↓

Required Decision

Decision reference harus dapat ditelusuri ke:

• decision ID;

• authority;

• scope;

• date;

• applicable lifecycle subject.

⸻

16\. Accountability Control

Control harus memastikan accountable owner dapat diidentifikasi.

Distinction:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Jika accountability tidak dapat ditentukan untuk material lifecycle action:

Ambiguous Accountability

↓

Control Exception

↓

Resolution / Escalation

⸻

17\. Boundary Control

F7 harus menjaga organizational dan Platform boundary.

Control harus mendeteksi apabila lifecycle change:

• memperluas domain PD-03 secara informal;

• memindahkan ownership Platform lain;

• mengubah authority tanpa applicable decision;

• mengambil alih execution domain;

• mengubah architecture ownership tanpa authority.

Dependency tidak boleh berubah menjadi ownership.

Dependency ≠ Ownership

⸻

18\. Cross-Platform Control

Material lifecycle change yang berdampak pada Platform lain harus menggunakan applicable coordination mechanism.

Control harus memeriksa:

• affected Platform;

• dependency;

• impact;

• authority;

• coordination;

• ownership;

• evidence;

• resulting state.

Model:

PD-03 Lifecycle Change

↓

Cross-Platform Impact

↓

Coordination

↓

Applicable Platform Owners

↓

Controlled Change

PD-03 tidak memperoleh ownership atas Platform yang terdampak.

⸻

19\. Control of Irreversible Transition

Baseline menyatakan bahwa reversibility tidak selalu diwajibkan.

Jika transition irreversible:

• irreversibility harus diketahui sebelum authorization;

• consequence harus dinilai;

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• resulting state harus dapat diverifikasi.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian:

Irreversible Transition

↓

Enhanced Control Attention

F7 tidak boleh memaksakan reversibility ketika source atau technical reality tidak memungkinkan.

⸻

20\. Lifecycle Exception Control

Exception dapat terjadi apabila normal transition mechanism tidak dapat digunakan.

Exception harus:

1\. diidentifikasi;

2\. diklasifikasikan;

3\. memiliki rationale;

4\. memiliki owner;

5\. memiliki applicable authority;

6\. memiliki mitigation;

7\. memiliki disposition;

8\. dicatat;

9\. direview apabila material.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Exception tidak boleh menjadi shortcut untuk menghindari governance.

⸻

21\. Exception Control Lifecycle

Canonical exception lifecycle:

Exception Detected

↓

Classification

↓

Rationale

↓

Authority

↓

Mitigation

↓

Disposition

↓

Record

↓

Review if Material

↓

Closure

Unresolved material exception tidak boleh diam-diam dianggap closed.

⸻

22\. Control Hold

Apabila mandatory control condition tidak terpenuhi, lifecycle action dapat ditempatkan pada control hold apabila applicable.

Contoh:

Missing Authority

↓

CONTROL HOLD

Invalid State

↓

CONTROL HOLD

Insufficient Evidence

↓

CONTROL HOLD

Control hold bukan rejection permanen.

Control hold berarti lifecycle action belum memenuhi required condition untuk melanjutkan.

⸻

23\. Control Override

Control override hanya dapat dilakukan apabila applicable governance mengizinkannya.

Override harus memiliki:

• authority;

• rationale;

• scope;

• duration;

• owner;

• risk consideration;

• record;

• review apabila material.

F7 tidak boleh membuat override mechanism baru tanpa source-backed authority.

⸻

24\. Control Failure

Control failure terjadi apabila control yang seharusnya mencegah, mendeteksi, atau membatasi lifecycle risk tidak bekerja sebagaimana dimaksud.

Control failure harus menghasilkan:

Control Failure

↓

Finding

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Corrective Action / Escalation

↓

Re-control / Re-review

Control failure tidak boleh dihapus hanya karena lifecycle action akhirnya berhasil.

⸻

25\. Control Effectiveness

Control effectiveness harus dievaluasi terhadap intended control objective.

Pertanyaan minimum:

• apakah control exists?

• apakah control applicable?

• apakah control operated?

• apakah control evidence tersedia?

• apakah control prevented/detected the intended condition?

• apakah exception ditangani?

• apakah control failure terjadi?

Source-fidelity qualification: model effectiveness di atas adalah bounded synthesis. Baseline mendukung control dan lifecycle integrity, tetapi belum menyediakan definitive control effectiveness taxonomy.

⸻

26\. Preventive Control

Preventive control berfungsi mencegah lifecycle action yang invalid sebelum terjadi.

Contoh:

Invalid Authority

↓

BLOCK

Invalid State

↓

BLOCK

Missing Prerequisite

↓

BLOCK

Preventive control harus digunakan jika feasible dan proporsional.

⸻

27\. Detective Control

Detective control mengidentifikasi lifecycle integrity issue setelah atau selama lifecycle activity.

Contoh:

Transition Executed

↓

State Verification

↓

Mismatch Detected

Detection harus menghasilkan finding atau exception apabila material.

⸻

28\. Corrective Control

Corrective control digunakan untuk menangani control failure atau lifecycle integrity issue.

Finding

↓

Corrective Action

↓

Verification

↓

Closure

Corrective action tidak boleh dianggap selesai hanya karena action telah dilakukan.

Result harus diverifikasi.

⸻

29\. Control Materiality

Control harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Materiality dapat mempertimbangkan:

• organizational impact;

• authority sensitivity;

• lifecycle consequence;

• reversibility;

• compliance impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• dependency;

• operational impact;

• historical significance.

Baseline secara eksplisit menyatakan bahwa lifecycle control harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

F7 tidak menetapkan numerical threshold tanpa source-backed basis.

⸻

30\. Control Segregation

Jika control objective membutuhkan independence, control responsibility harus dipisahkan dari incompatible execution responsibility.

Contoh:

Control Definition

≠

Control Self-Validation

apabila independence material.

Namun F7 tidak secara otomatis mewajibkan physical organizational separation untuk setiap control.

Segregation harus proporsional terhadap risk dan governance requirement.

⸻

31\. Control Ownership

Control ownership harus dapat diidentifikasi.

Minimum distinction:

Control Owner

↓

Accountable for Control Design / Integrity

Control Operator

↓

Executes Control

Control Reviewer

↓

Assesses Control when required

Satu person/unit dapat memiliki lebih dari satu role hanya jika tidak menciptakan incompatible conflict.

Source-fidelity qualification: role taxonomy ini merupakan bounded synthesis dan harus direconcile dengan canonical governance vocabulary oleh Claude Code.

⸻

32\. Control Evidence

Setiap material control operation harus menghasilkan evidence yang cukup untuk membuktikan:

• control operated;

• applicable condition;

• control result;

• exception jika ada;

• reviewer jika required;

• timestamp/effective context jika material.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri ke lifecycle action.

⸻

33\. Control Record

Minimum lifecycle control record:

Field Requirement

Control ID Required

Lifecycle Subject Required

Control Objective Required

Control Type Required

Applicable State Required when applicable

Applicable Transition Required when applicable

Authority Reference Required

Control Owner Required

Control Evidence Required

Control Result Required

Exception Required when applicable

Finding Required when applicable

Verification Required when applicable

Effective Date Required

Review Date Required when applicable

Historical Reference Required

⸻

42\. Performance Architecture Relationship

Performance evidence dapat menjadi input control assessment.

Part E

Performance Evidence

Performance result tidak otomatis menghasilkan lifecycle control failure.

Applicable criteria tetap diperlukan.

⸻

43\. Control Escalation

Escalation diperlukan apabila:

• mandatory control condition gagal;

• authority conflict terjadi;

• material exception unresolved;

• lifecycle state tidak valid;

• transition integrity disputed;

• cross-platform boundary terancam;

• control failure memiliki material consequence;

• evidence material hilang;

• control override membutuhkan authority yang tidak tersedia.

Escalation mengikuti applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

44\. Control Closure

Lifecycle control issue dapat ditutup apabila:

1\. issue telah diidentifikasi;

2\. owner telah ditetapkan;

3\. required action telah dilakukan;

4\. evidence tersedia;

5\. verification selesai;

6\. residual issue diketahui;

7\. applicable reviewer menyetujui closure apabila diperlukan;

8\. canonical record diperbarui.

Issue

↓

Action

↓

Evidence

↓

Verification

↓

Closure

⸻

45\. Control Continuity

Lifecycle transition tidak boleh menghapus control history secara otomatis.

Retirement, restructuring, atau dissolution harus mempertahankan historical control evidence sesuai applicable retention requirements.

Historical continuity merupakan bagian dari lifecycle integrity.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

46\. Control Integrity Rules

F7 menetapkan:

1\. Jangan mengizinkan material lifecycle action tanpa applicable authority.

2\. Jangan mengizinkan invalid state transition.

3\. Jangan menganggap evidence yang tidak cukup sebagai evidence yang valid.

4\. Jangan menghapus lifecycle control history.

5\. Jangan mengubah exception menjadi normal path secara informal.

6\. Jangan menggunakan control untuk menciptakan authority baru.

7\. Jangan menggunakan governance control untuk mengambil alih execution.

8\. Jangan melewati mandatory control hanya karena operational urgency.

9\. Jangan menutup control issue tanpa verification yang memadai.

10\. Jangan mengubah dependency menjadi ownership.

11\. Jangan menganggap control success sebagai lifecycle success.

12\. Jangan mengorbankan lifecycle integrity demi kecepatan perubahan.

⸻

47\. Control Failure Handling

Jika lifecycle control gagal:

Control Failure

↓

Contain

↓

Assess Impact

↓

Record

↓

Correct

↓

Verify

↓

Re-control

↓

Re-review if Material

Material control failure harus dapat dieskalasikan.

⸻

48\. Lifecycle Control and Urgent Change

Urgency tidak menghapus governance requirement.

Jika urgent lifecycle action diperlukan:

Urgent Need

↓

Applicable Emergency / Exception Mechanism

↓

Authority

↓

Controlled Action

↓

Evidence

↓

Post-Action Review

Source-fidelity qualification: emergency control path tidak ditetapkan secara literal dalam baseline yang tersedia. Claude Code wajib mereconcile apakah AIOS memiliki emergency-change mechanism sebelum menjadikannya canonical.

⸻

49\. Lifecycle Control and Automation

Automation dapat digunakan untuk:

• state validation;

• record validation;

• evidence collection;

• traceability;

• control execution;

• detection;

• notification;

• escalation.

Automation tidak otomatis memperoleh authority.

Automation

≠

Authority

Jika automated control menghasilkan material decision, applicable human/governance authority requirement tetap berlaku.

⸻

50\. Lifecycle Control and AI Agents

AI agent dapat membantu:

• evidence collection;

• consistency checking;

• control monitoring;

• anomaly detection;

• traceability;

• review preparation;

• escalation preparation.

AI agent tidak boleh memperoleh lifecycle authority hanya karena menjalankan control.

Authority harus berasal dari applicable governance model.

⸻

51\. Control Observability

Material lifecycle control harus observable apabila practical.

Observability dapat mencakup:

• control execution status;

• control failure;

• exception;

• overdue control;

• unresolved finding;

• missing evidence;

• state mismatch.

Control observability membantu lifecycle review dan governance assurance.

⸻

52\. Control Metrics

Potential control indicators:

• control compliance rate;

• control failure rate;

• unresolved exception count;

• evidence completeness;

• transition validation failure;

• authorization failure;

• mean remediation time;

• re-review frequency.

Source-fidelity qualification: metric taxonomy di atas belum diberikan secara literal oleh baseline. Claude Code harus menentukan apakah metric tersebut compatible dengan canonical Performance Architecture sebelum membekukannya sebagai standard.

⸻

53\. Control Improvement

Control harus dapat diperbaiki apabila:

• control failure berulang;

• control terlalu kompleks;

• evidence burden tidak proporsional;

• control tidak mendeteksi intended condition;

• lifecycle risk berubah;

• architecture berubah;

• dependency berubah.

Control improvement menjadi input F9.

⸻

54\. Control Maturity

Control capability dapat berkembang dari:

Ad Hoc

↓

Defined

↓

Managed

↓

Controlled

↓

Adaptive

Namun F7 tidak menetapkan final maturity score.

Maturity assessment tetap berada pada F9.

55\. Out of Scope

F7 tidak menetapkan:

• detailed lifecycle state catalogue;

• definitive transition matrix;

• technical implementation;

• operational control catalogue milik Platform lain;

• enterprise-wide control framework di luar PD-03;

• workforce assignment;

• individual control operators;

• compensation;

• technical security controls;

• runtime enforcement implementation;

• final lifecycle maturity score;

• final lifecycle success criteria.

Detail tersebut berada pada applicable child section atau domain owner.

⸻

56\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Source yang tersedia secara langsung mendukung:

• lifecycle control sebagai bagian Part F;

• authorization;

• state validation;

• transition validation;

• evidence;

• traceability;

• review;

• exception handling;

• canonical recording;

• proportionality terhadap materiality;

• exception governance;

• reversibility consideration;

• irreversible transition scrutiny;

• lifecycle impact assessment;

• canonical lifecycle records.

Namun baseline yang tersedia belum memberikan definitive canonical detail untuk:

• control taxonomy;

• control effectiveness scale;

• automation authority model.

Karena itu F7 menggunakan:

BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS

Detail tersebut tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai immutable canonical fact sebelum source reconciliation.

⸻

57\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation terhadap:

1\. PD-03 Governance Baseline;

2\. F1–F6;

3\. Governance Control architecture pada Part C;

4\. Governance Operations pada Part D;

5\. Performance measurement architecture pada Part E;

6\. canonical AIOS control vocabulary;

7\. existing exception mechanisms;

8\. existing authority/delegation mechanisms;

9\. existing lifecycle implementation mechanisms;

10\. existing lifecycle evidence and record schemas.

Claude Code harus menentukan:

• apakah control taxonomy sudah ada;

• apakah control ownership sudah canonical;

• apakah emergency change mechanism sudah ada;

• apakah automated control memiliki defined authority boundary;

• apakah lifecycle control metrics telah didefinisikan di Performance Architecture.

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• membuat authority baru;

• membuat control ownership yang bertentangan dengan organizational architecture;

• mengubah control menjadi implementation ownership;

• menjadikan automation sebagai autonomous authority;

• mengadopsi control taxonomy eksternal tanpa reconciliation;

• menghapus source gap;

• menyamarkan bounded synthesis sebagai historical canonical fact.

Jika ambiguity dapat diselesaikan melalui delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai Delegation Charter.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh constitutional authority, Founder Reserved Authority, Canonical Architecture supremacy, atau fundamental governance boundary, Claude Code wajib melakukan escalation.

⸻

58\. Success Criteria

F7 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. lifecycle control memiliki purpose yang jelas;

2\. lifecycle control boundary jelas;

3\. control dibedakan dari review;

4\. control dibedakan dari change governance;

5\. control dibedakan dari implementation;

6\. authorization control tersedia;

7\. state validation control tersedia;

8\. transition validation control tersedia;

9\. evidence control tersedia;

10\. evidence sufficiency dapat dinilai;

11\. traceability control tersedia;

12\. decision reference dapat diverifikasi;

13\. accountability dapat diverifikasi;

14\. organizational boundary terlindungi;

15\. cross-platform boundary terlindungi;

16\. irreversible transition mendapatkan additional scrutiny;

17\. exception lifecycle memiliki governance;

18\. control hold dapat digunakan apabila applicable;

19\. control failure memiliki handling mechanism;

20\. preventive control dapat diterapkan;

21\. detective control dapat diterapkan;

22\. corrective control dapat diterapkan;

23\. control materiality bersifat proporsional;

24\. control ownership dapat ditelusuri;

25\. control evidence dapat direcord;

26\. control closure membutuhkan verification;

27\. historical control continuity dipertahankan;

28\. escalation mechanism tersedia;

29\. F7 tidak menciptakan authority;

60\. Canonical Lifecycle Control Statement

F7 — Lifecycle Control establishes the control architecture through which material PD-03 lifecycle activities are constrained, validated, evidenced, traced, reviewed, exception-handled, and canonically recorded so that lifecycle integrity is preserved throughout authorization, state transition, implementation, verification, and resulting-state establishment.

Lifecycle Control does not create authority, replace lifecycle review, authorize lifecycle change independently, or assume implementation ownership. It provides the control boundary necessary to ensure that authorized lifecycle change remains within applicable state, transition, evidence, accountability, organizational, and cross-platform constraints.

Lifecycle Control shall remain proportionate to lifecycle materiality and shall preserve historical traceability and canonical lifecycle records.

\# 1. Purpose

F8 menetapkan architecture untuk \*\*melaksanakan authorized lifecycle change\*\* setelah lifecycle change tersebut memenuhi applicable governance, review, control, authority, dan transition requirements.

F8 menjawab pertanyaan:

\> \*\*Bagaimana lifecycle change yang telah diotorisasi dan dikendalikan diwujudkan menjadi perubahan kondisi lifecycle yang nyata, dapat diverifikasi, dan dapat dicatat secara canonical?\*\*

F8 berada setelah:

4\. Fundamental Implementation Principle

No lifecycle implementation shall occur unless the applicable lifecycle change is authorized, sufficiently controlled, and associated with an identifiable transition context.

Implementation tidak boleh menjadi cara untuk memperoleh authority yang belum ada.

Decision

↓

Authorization

↓

Control

↓

Implementation

bukan:

Implementation

↓

Decision

⸻

5\. Implementation vs Authorization

F8 harus menjaga separation:

Authorization

≠

Implementation

Authority menentukan apakah lifecycle change boleh dilakukan.

Implementation mewujudkan lifecycle change yang telah diotorisasi.

Implementation tidak boleh memperluas:

• decision scope;

• authority scope;

• organizational boundary;

• lifecycle transition;

• resulting state.

Jika implementation membutuhkan perubahan terhadap authorized scope:

Scope Change

↓

Return to Applicable Governance

Conflict

↓

Control / Review / Escalation

bukan unilateral reinterpretation.

↓

8\. Implementation Boundary

F8 mencakup implementation yang diperlukan untuk menghasilkan resulting lifecycle condition.

11\. Implementation Readiness

Sebelum implementation dimulai, applicable readiness conditions harus dapat diverifikasi.

Minimum readiness considerations:

• authorized change exists;

• current state is known;

• target state is known when applicable;

• implementation scope is defined;

• accountable owner is known;

• applicable controls are identified;

• required evidence is understood;

• dependencies are identified;

• implementation prerequisites are satisfied;

• resulting state can be verified.

Source-fidelity qualification: daftar readiness ini merupakan bounded synthesis dari lifecycle authority, evidence, transition, control, dan resulting-state principles. Baseline tidak menyediakan readiness checklist literal yang lengkap.

⸻

12\. Implementation Preconditions

Implementation tidak boleh dimulai apabila mandatory preconditions belum terpenuhi.

↓

NO IMPLEMENTATION

↓

NO IMPLEMENTATION

Missing Mandatory Control

↓

NO IMPLEMENTATION

Unresolved Material Boundary Conflict

↓

NO IMPLEMENTATION

13\. Implementation Scope

Setiap material implementation harus memiliki identifiable scope.

Scope sekurang-kurangnya menjelaskan:

• lifecycle subject;

• current state;

• target state;

• change type;

• affected organizational configuration;

• affected responsibilities;

• affected artifacts;

• affected dependencies;

• applicable implementation boundary.

Implementation tidak boleh meluas melampaui approved scope.

⸻

14\. Implementation Plan

Untuk material lifecycle change, implementation harus memiliki plan yang cukup untuk memastikan controlled execution.

Implementation plan dapat mencakup:

0\. objective;

0\. scope;

0\. prerequisites;

0\. dependencies;

0\. sequence;

0\. control points;

0\. evidence requirements;

0\. verification method;

0\. rollback/recovery consideration apabila applicable;

0\. resulting-state confirmation.

Source-fidelity qualification: format plan di atas adalah bounded synthesis. Baseline mendukung controlled transition dan evidence, tetapi tidak menetapkan template implementation plan literal.

Material lifecycle implementation harus mengikuti sequence yang menjaga lifecycle integrity.

16\. Controlled Implementation

Implementation harus berjalan dalam approved boundary.

Control points dapat digunakan untuk:

• prerequisite verification;

• scope validation;

• authority confirmation;

• evidence capture;

• state validation;

• transition validation;

• intermediate verification;

• final verification.

F7 menentukan control principle.

F8 menerapkannya pada implementation lifecycle.

17\. Implementation Evidence

Implementation harus menghasilkan evidence yang cukup untuk menunjukkan bahwa authorized change benar-benar dilakukan.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• implementation record;

• execution evidence;

• affected artifact;

• configuration evidence;

• organizational change evidence;

• approval reference;

• control result;

• verification result;

• resulting-state evidence.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri ke lifecycle subject dan transition.

⸻

18\. Evidence Integrity

Implementation evidence harus:

• identifiable;

• attributable;

• traceable;

• sufficiently complete;

• consistent dengan authorized scope;

• dapat dikaitkan dengan resulting state.

Evidence tidak boleh:

• fabricated;

• detached dari implementation;

• dibuat tanpa lifecycle context;

• digunakan untuk melegitimasi unauthorized change.

• tidak melebihi delegated authority.

Delegated implementation authority tidak boleh dianggap sebagai unrestricted lifecycle authority.

26\. Implementation Hold

Implementation dapat ditempatkan pada hold apabila:

• authority tidak valid;

• prerequisite gagal;

• state mismatch;

• control failure;

• material dependency unavailable;

• required evidence tidak tersedia;

• scope conflict ditemukan;

• escalation required.

Hold bukan failure otomatis.

Hold berarti implementation tidak boleh melanjutkan sampai condition yang diperlukan terpenuhi.

31\. Irreversible Implementation

Jika implementation menghasilkan irreversible lifecycle change:

• irreversibility harus diketahui;

• applicable authority harus valid;

• evidence harus memadai;

• implementation scope harus jelas;

• verification harus tersedia;

• resulting state harus dapat dicatat.

Baseline menetapkan bahwa reversibility tidak universal; jika transition irreversible, consequence dan authority harus dinilai sebelum action.

32\. Recovery and Reversibility

Jika recovery atau rollback secara teknis dan governance memungkinkan, implementation planning dapat memasukkannya.

Namun:

Rollback is not a universal lifecycle requirement.

Jangan menganggap setiap lifecycle implementation harus reversible apabila source atau technical condition tidak memungkinkan.

Irreversible Action

Enhanced Pre-Implementation Control

↓

Execution

Verification

Automation tidak memperoleh lifecycle authority hanya karena melakukan implementation.

Automation

≠

Authority

57\. AI-Assisted Implementation

AI agents dapat membantu:

• implementation planning;

• documentation;

• evidence collection;

• consistency checking;

• state validation;

• verification preparation;

• dependency analysis;

• reporting.

Namun AI assistance tidak boleh mengubah authorized scope secara autonomous tanpa applicable authority.

⸻

58\. Implementation Observability

Material implementation harus observable apabila practical.

Observability dapat mencakup:

• implementation status;

• control status;

• evidence status;

• verification status;

• dependency status;

• exception status;

• resulting-state status.

Observability mendukung F6, F7, F9, dan F10.

• implementation completion rate;

• implementation failure rate;

• exception rate;

• resulting-state mismatch rate.

Source-fidelity qualification: metric taxonomy tersebut belum ditemukan sebagai canonical literal source. Jangan membekukannya sebagai official KPI sebelum reconciliation dengan Part E.

60\. Implementation Improvement

67\. Canonical Lifecycle Implementation Statement

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation establishes the controlled implementation boundary through which an authorized lifecycle change is prepared, executed, evidenced, verified, and converted into a valid resulting lifecycle state without creating new authority, expanding organizational ownership, violating lifecycle controls, or compromising historical traceability.

Lifecycle Implementation shall remain subordinate to applicable lifecycle authority, governance, transition, review, and control requirements. Execution of a lifecycle change does not itself constitute authorization to alter the approved scope, authority, resulting state, or ownership boundary.

Where implementation affects another Platform or domain, implementation ownership remains with the applicable domain owner while PD-03 retains its Governance & Compliance responsibility.

\# 1. Purpose

F8 menetapkan architecture untuk \*\*melaksanakan authorized lifecycle change\*\* setelah lifecycle change tersebut memenuhi applicable governance, review, control, authority, dan transition requirements.

F8 menjawab pertanyaan:

\> \*\*Bagaimana lifecycle change yang telah diotorisasi dan dikendalikan diwujudkan menjadi perubahan kondisi lifecycle yang nyata, dapat diverifikasi, dan dapat dicatat secara canonical?\*\*

F8 berada setelah:

\`\`\`text

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

↓

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

F8 kemudian menghasilkan evidence untuk:

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity

↓

F10 — Lifecycle Success

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

F8 merupakan child architecture dari F1 — Lifecycle Constitution.

F8 tidak berdiri sebagai independent authority.

F1

Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2

Lifecycle Model

↓

F3

Lifecycle States

↓

F4

Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5

Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6

Lifecycle Review

↓

F7

Lifecycle Control

↓

F8

Lifecycle Implementation

F8 menggunakan hasil dari F1–F7 sebagai constraint.

F8 tidak boleh mengubah constitutional lifecycle model secara informal.

⸻

3\. Source Boundary

Baseline secara eksplisit menempatkan F8 sebagai salah satu child section Part F.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menunjukkan lifecycle loop:

Authority Decision

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

Dengan demikian F8 secara constitutional berada pada implementation boundary setelah authorization dan control, bukan pada decision boundary.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Implementation Principle

No lifecycle implementation shall occur unless the applicable lifecycle change is authorized, sufficiently controlled, and associated with an identifiable transition context.

Implementation tidak boleh menjadi cara untuk memperoleh authority yang belum ada.

Decision

↓

Authorization

↓

Control

↓

Implementation

bukan:

Implementation

↓

Decision

⸻

5\. Implementation vs Authorization

F8 harus menjaga separation:

Authorization

≠

Implementation

Authority menentukan apakah lifecycle change boleh dilakukan.

Implementation mewujudkan lifecycle change yang telah diotorisasi.

Implementation tidak boleh memperluas:

• decision scope;

• authority scope;

• organizational boundary;

• lifecycle transition;

• resulting state.

Jika implementation membutuhkan perubahan terhadap authorized scope:

Scope Change

↓

Return to Applicable Governance

⸻

6\. Implementation vs Lifecycle Decision

F8 tidak membuat lifecycle decision.

F5

Governance

↓

F6

Review

↓

F7

Control

↓

F8

Implementation

Implementation team atau implementation mechanism tidak boleh mengubah decision secara informal.

Jika terdapat conflict antara decision dan implementation condition:

Conflict

↓

Control / Review / Escalation

bukan unilateral reinterpretation.

⸻

7\. Implementation vs Lifecycle Control

F7 menetapkan control requirements.

F8 melaksanakan authorized change dalam control boundary tersebut.

F7

Control Requirement

↓

F8

Controlled Implementation

↓

Evidence

↓

Verification

F8 tidak boleh menghapus mandatory control hanya karena implementation membutuhkan kecepatan.

⸻

8\. Implementation Boundary

F8 mencakup implementation yang diperlukan untuk menghasilkan resulting lifecycle condition.

F8 tidak otomatis mencakup seluruh technical implementation dari Platform lain.

Boundary:

Lifecycle Implementation

↓

Authorized Lifecycle Outcome

bukan:

Lifecycle Implementation

↓

All Technical Execution

Jika lifecycle implementation membutuhkan technical work pada Platform lain, execution ownership tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

9\. Organizational Implementation Boundary

Lifecycle implementation dapat menyebabkan perubahan pada:

• organizational configuration;

• responsibility allocation;

• capability arrangement;

• team arrangement;

• reporting arrangement;

• operating configuration.

Namun resulting organizational structure tetap harus mengikuti Organization Architecture.

Lifecycle Decision

↓

Authorized Organizational Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Updated Organization State

F8 tidak menggantikan Part B.

⸻

10\. Cross-Platform Implementation

Lifecycle implementation dapat memiliki cross-platform impact.

Jika demikian:

PD-03 Lifecycle Change

↓

Cross-Platform Impact

↓

Coordination

↓

Applicable Domain Owners

↓

Implementation

Cross-platform implementation tidak memindahkan ownership.

PD-03 tetap owner Governance & Compliance.

Platform lain tetap owner domain masing-masing.

⸻

11\. Implementation Readiness

Sebelum implementation dimulai, applicable readiness conditions harus dapat diverifikasi.

Minimum readiness considerations:

• authorized change exists;

• current state is known;

• target state is known when applicable;

• implementation scope is defined;

• accountable owner is known;

• applicable controls are identified;

• required evidence is understood;

• dependencies are identified;

• implementation prerequisites are satisfied;

• resulting state can be verified.

Source-fidelity qualification: daftar readiness ini merupakan bounded synthesis dari lifecycle authority, evidence, transition, control, dan resulting-state principles. Baseline tidak menyediakan readiness checklist literal yang lengkap.

⸻

12\. Implementation Preconditions

Implementation tidak boleh dimulai apabila mandatory preconditions belum terpenuhi.

Contoh:

Missing Authority

↓

NO IMPLEMENTATION

Invalid Current State

↓

NO IMPLEMENTATION

Missing Mandatory Control

↓

NO IMPLEMENTATION

Unresolved Material Boundary Conflict

↓

NO IMPLEMENTATION

Control hold tetap mengikuti F7.

⸻

13\. Implementation Scope

Setiap material implementation harus memiliki identifiable scope.

Scope sekurang-kurangnya menjelaskan:

• lifecycle subject;

• current state;

• target state;

• change type;

• affected organizational configuration;

• affected responsibilities;

• affected artifacts;

• affected dependencies;

• applicable implementation boundary.

Implementation tidak boleh meluas melampaui approved scope.

⸻

14\. Implementation Plan

Untuk material lifecycle change, implementation harus memiliki plan yang cukup untuk memastikan controlled execution.

Implementation plan dapat mencakup:

1\. objective;

2\. scope;

3\. prerequisites;

4\. dependencies;

5\. sequence;

6\. control points;

7\. evidence requirements;

8\. verification method;

9\. rollback/recovery consideration apabila applicable;

10\. resulting-state confirmation.

Source-fidelity qualification: format plan di atas adalah bounded synthesis. Baseline mendukung controlled transition dan evidence, tetapi tidak menetapkan template implementation plan literal.

⸻

15\. Implementation Sequence

Material lifecycle implementation harus mengikuti sequence yang menjaga lifecycle integrity.

Canonical pattern:

Authorized Change

↓

Readiness

↓

Preparation

↓

Controlled Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Canonical Record

Implementation tidak boleh langsung mengubah canonical state sebelum verification apabila verification memang merupakan prerequisite.

⸻

16\. Controlled Implementation

Implementation harus berjalan dalam approved boundary.

Control points dapat digunakan untuk:

• prerequisite verification;

• scope validation;

• authority confirmation;

• evidence capture;

• state validation;

• transition validation;

• intermediate verification;

• final verification.

F7 menentukan control principle.

F8 menerapkannya pada implementation lifecycle.

⸻

17\. Implementation Evidence

Implementation harus menghasilkan evidence yang cukup untuk menunjukkan bahwa authorized change benar-benar dilakukan.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• implementation record;

• execution evidence;

• affected artifact;

• configuration evidence;

• organizational change evidence;

• approval reference;

• control result;

• verification result;

• resulting-state evidence.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri ke lifecycle subject dan transition.

⸻

18\. Evidence Integrity

Implementation evidence harus:

• identifiable;

• attributable;

• traceable;

• sufficiently complete;

• consistent dengan authorized scope;

• dapat dikaitkan dengan resulting state.

Evidence tidak boleh:

• fabricated;

• detached dari implementation;

• dibuat tanpa lifecycle context;

• digunakan untuk melegitimasi unauthorized change.

⸻

19\. Implementation Traceability

Implementation harus mempertahankan traceability:

Lifecycle Matter

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Implementation

↓

Evidence

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Canonical Record

Traceability merupakan continuation dari F7.

Baseline secara eksplisit mensyaratkan hubungan antara evidence, authority, decision, implementation, dan resulting state.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

20\. Implementation Accountability

Implementation harus memiliki accountable owner.

Distinction:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Implementation

Authority menentukan right to act.

Accountability menentukan responsibility terhadap outcome.

Implementation melakukan authorized work.

Tidak boleh ada material implementation tanpa accountable ownership.

⸻

21\. Implementation Ownership

Implementation ownership harus mengikuti applicable domain ownership.

Untuk organizational lifecycle change:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

↓

Lifecycle Governance / Authorization

Tetapi apabila implementation menyentuh:

• Architecture;

• Runtime;

• Security;

• Quality;

• Infrastructure;

• AI Engineering;

• Developer Experience;

maka implementation ownership tetap berada pada applicable Platform/domain owner.

Lifecycle governance tidak otomatis menjadi implementation ownership.

⸻

22\. Implementation and Delegation

Implementation authority dapat didelegasikan apabila applicable governance mengizinkannya.

Delegation harus:

• memiliki scope;

• memiliki authority basis;

• memiliki accountability;

• memiliki effective period apabila applicable;

• dapat ditelusuri;

• tidak melebihi delegated authority.

Delegated implementation authority tidak boleh dianggap sebagai unrestricted lifecycle authority.

⸻

23\. Implementation and Exceptions

Jika implementation tidak dapat mengikuti normal path:

Implementation Exception

↓

F7 Exception Control

↓

Authority / Disposition

↓

Controlled Continuation

Implementation team tidak boleh membuat permanent exception secara informal.

Exception harus mengikuti F7.

⸻

24\. Implementation Failure

Jika implementation gagal:

Implementation Failure

↓

Contain

↓

Assess

↓

Record

↓

Control / Review

↓

Recovery / Corrective Action

↓

Verification

Implementation failure tidak boleh langsung dianggap sebagai lifecycle transition failure.

Impact harus dinilai terlebih dahulu.

⸻

25\. Partial Implementation

Partial implementation harus dapat dibedakan dari completed implementation.

Status tidak boleh ambiguous.

Contoh conceptual states:

NOT STARTED

IN PROGRESS

PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED

IMPLEMENTED — PENDING VERIFICATION

VERIFIED

FAILED

BLOCKED

Source-fidelity qualification: taxonomy status di atas belum ditemukan sebagai canonical literal taxonomy dalam baseline. Claude Code harus melakukan source reconciliation sebelum membekukannya sebagai formal status vocabulary.

⸻

26\. Implementation Hold

Implementation dapat ditempatkan pada hold apabila:

• authority tidak valid;

• prerequisite gagal;

• state mismatch;

• control failure;

• material dependency unavailable;

• required evidence tidak tersedia;

• scope conflict ditemukan;

• escalation required.

Hold bukan failure otomatis.

Hold berarti implementation tidak boleh melanjutkan sampai condition yang diperlukan terpenuhi.

⸻

27\. Implementation Completion

Implementation tidak boleh dinyatakan complete hanya karena execution activity selesai.

Minimum completion condition:

Implementation Executed

↓

Evidence Captured

↓

Verification Completed

↓

Resulting State Confirmed

↓

Canonical Record Updated

Dengan demikian:

Executed ≠ Completed

⸻

28\. Verification Boundary

Verification merupakan boundary antara implementation dan resulting lifecycle state.

F8

Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

Verification harus menentukan apakah implementation menghasilkan condition yang diharapkan.

F8 tidak boleh menetapkan verification criteria yang bertentangan dengan F4, F7, atau applicable lifecycle decision.

⸻

29\. Resulting State

Implementation dianggap berhasil menghasilkan lifecycle transition apabila resulting state:

• identifiable;

• valid;

• consistent with authorized target;

• supported by evidence;

• recorded;

• within applicable boundary.

Baseline secara eksplisit menempatkan resulting state dan verification sebagai bagian dari lifecycle integrity.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

30\. Resulting-State Mismatch

Jika resulting state berbeda dari authorized target:

Resulting State Mismatch

↓

Verification Failure

↓

Control / Review

↓

Correction / Reassessment / Escalation

Mismatch tidak boleh disembunyikan dengan mengubah target state setelah implementation tanpa applicable authority.

⸻

31\. Irreversible Implementation

Jika implementation menghasilkan irreversible lifecycle change:

• irreversibility harus diketahui;

• applicable authority harus valid;

• evidence harus memadai;

• implementation scope harus jelas;

• verification harus tersedia;

• resulting state harus dapat dicatat.

Baseline menetapkan bahwa reversibility tidak universal; jika transition irreversible, consequence dan authority harus dinilai sebelum action.

⸻

32\. Recovery and Reversibility

Jika recovery atau rollback secara teknis dan governance memungkinkan, implementation planning dapat memasukkannya.

Namun:

Rollback is not a universal lifecycle requirement.

Jangan menganggap setiap lifecycle implementation harus reversible apabila source atau technical condition tidak memungkinkan.

Jika tidak reversible:

Irreversible Action

↓

Enhanced Pre-Implementation Control

↓

Execution

↓

Verification

⸻

33\. Implementation and Historical Continuity

Lifecycle implementation tidak boleh menghapus historical continuity.

Perubahan terhadap:

• organizational structure;

• responsibility;

• governance state;

• lifecycle state;

• canonical artifacts;

harus mempertahankan historical reference sesuai applicable requirements.

Retirement atau restructuring tidak otomatis menghapus historical records.

⸻

34\. Implementation and Canonical Records

Setelah implementation berhasil diverifikasi, canonical lifecycle record harus diperbarui.

Minimum record relationship:

Field Requirement

Lifecycle Subject Required

Previous State Required

Target State Required when applicable

Change Type Required

Authority Reference Required

Decision Reference Required

Implementation Record Required

Evidence Required

Verification Result Required

Resulting State Required

Effective Date Required

Historical Reference Required

Field tambahan dapat ditetapkan oleh F9/F10 atau applicable lifecycle record schema.

Baseline telah menetapkan canonical lifecycle record dengan field-field inti tersebut.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

35\. Implementation and Governance Operations

Setelah lifecycle implementation selesai:

F8

Implementation

↓

Resulting State

↓

Part D

Operate Resulting State

F8 tidak mengambil alih operating model.

Part D menjadi operational owner terhadap kondisi organisasi yang telah menjadi resulting state.

⸻

36\. Implementation and Performance

Implementation menghasilkan evidence yang dapat digunakan oleh Part E.

Implementation

↓

Implementation Evidence

↓

Performance Observation

↓

Performance Review

Performance architecture tetap memiliki responsibility terhadap measurement dan performance assessment.

F8 tidak menetapkan KPI baru.

⸻

37\. Implementation and Lifecycle Improvement

Implementation findings dapat menjadi input F9.

Implementation Evidence

↓

Implementation Finding

↓

Improvement Opportunity

↓

F9

Recurring implementation friction dapat menjadi evidence untuk lifecycle improvement.

⸻

38\. Implementation and Lifecycle Success

F8 menyediakan evidence bagi F10.

Namun:

Implementation Success

≠

Lifecycle Success

Implementation success berarti authorized change berhasil diwujudkan.

Lifecycle success harus mempertimbangkan broader lifecycle outcome.

⸻

39\. Cross-Platform Implementation Coordination

Jika implementation berdampak lintas Platform:

1\. affected Platform harus diidentifikasi;

2\. dependency harus diketahui;

3\. applicable authority harus diperiksa;

4\. coordination harus dilakukan;

5\. ownership harus tetap jelas;

6\. evidence harus dikumpulkan;

7\. resulting state harus diverifikasi.

Cross-platform implementation tidak boleh menggunakan PD-03 authority untuk mengambil alih domain Platform lain.

⸻

40\. Platform Autonomy

F8 wajib menjaga:

Dependency

≠

Ownership

dan:

Coordination

≠

Subordination

PD-03 dapat mengoordinasikan lifecycle governance.

PD-03 tidak otomatis menjadi executor seluruh perubahan lintas Platform.

41\. Implementation Control Points

42\. Implementation Transparency

Material implementation status harus dapat diketahui oleh legitimate stakeholders yang membutuhkan informasi untuk:

• governance;

• review;

• accountability;

• coordination;

• verification;

• operational readiness.

Transparency tidak berarti seluruh implementation detail harus unrestricted.

Applicable confidentiality dan access constraints tetap berlaku.

⸻

43\. Implementation Risk

Material lifecycle implementation harus mempertimbangkan risk terhadap:

• lifecycle integrity;

• organizational continuity;

• governance integrity;

• authority;

• accountability;

• dependency;

• cross-platform impact;

• resulting state;

• historical continuity.

Implementation risk tidak boleh dihilangkan hanya karena transition telah memperoleh approval.

atau:

7\. canonical record diperbarui;

8\. applicable operational owner dapat menerima resulting state.

Closure

Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation terhadap:

1\. PD-03 Governance Baseline;

2\. F1 — Lifecycle Constitution;

3\. F2 — Lifecycle Model;

16\. existing canonical lifecycle record schema.

Claude Code harus secara khusus memverifikasi:

• exception implementation mechanism;

• rollback/recovery semantics;

• automation authority;

Claude Code tidak boleh:

• menciptakan lifecycle authority baru;

• mengubah lifecycle decision melalui implementation;

• memperluas PD-03 ownership;

• mengambil alih implementation Platform lain;

• membuat implementation taxonomy eksternal menjadi canonical tanpa reconciliation;

• menyamarkan bounded synthesis sebagai source-backed fact.

Jika ambiguity berada dalam delegated architecture authority, Claude Code dapat menyelesaikannya sesuai delegation yang berlaku.

Jika ambiguity menyentuh constitutional authority, Founder Reserved Authority, canonical governance boundary, atau fundamental Platform ownership, Claude Code wajib melakukan escalation.

65\. Success Criteria

F8 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. implementation berada setelah authorization;

2\. implementation tidak menciptakan authority;

3\. implementation scope dapat diidentifikasi;

4\. implementation prerequisites dapat diverifikasi;

5\. implementation memiliki accountable owner;

6\. implementation mengikuti applicable control requirements;

7\. implementation menghasilkan evidence;

8\. evidence dapat ditelusuri;

9\. implementation tidak memperluas domain ownership;

10\. cross-platform implementation mempertahankan Platform autonomy;

11\. implementation failure dapat ditangani;

12\. partial implementation dapat dibedakan dari completion apabila taxonomy telah disahkan;

13\. completion membutuhkan verification;

14\. resulting state dapat diverifikasi;

15\. resulting-state mismatch dapat ditangani;

16\. canonical lifecycle record diperbarui;

17\. historical continuity dipertahankan;

18\. implementation dapat di-handoff ke operating model apabila applicable;

19\. retirement implementation mempertahankan historical record;

20\. dissolution implementation menyelesaikan applicable dependencies dan records;

21\. restructuring tidak otomatis dianggap dissolution;

22\. creation menghasilkan identifiable lifecycle subject;

23\. exception tetap berada dalam governance boundary;

24\. automation tidak memperoleh authority otomatis;

25\. AI assistance tidak dapat mengubah authorized scope secara informal;

26\. implementation evidence dapat menjadi input performance;

27\. implementation evidence dapat menjadi input lifecycle improvement;

28\. implementation success dibedakan dari lifecycle success;

29\. source gaps ditandai;

30\. Claude Code memiliki explicit source-reconciliation directive.

67\. Canonical Lifecycle Implementation Statement

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation establishes the controlled implementation boundary through which an authorized lifecycle change is prepared, executed, evidenced, verified, and converted into a valid resulting lifecycle state without creating new authority, expanding organizational ownership, violating lifecycle controls, or compromising historical traceability.

Lifecycle Implementation shall remain subordinate to applicable lifecycle authority, governance, transition, review, and control requirements. Execution of a lifecycle change does not itself constitute authorization to alter the approved scope, authority, resulting state, or ownership boundary.

Where implementation affects another Platform or domain, implementation ownership remains with the applicable domain owner while PD-03 retains its Governance & Compliance responsibility.

\# 1. Purpose

F9 menetapkan architecture untuk:

\- mengevaluasi kualitas lifecycle system;

\- mengidentifikasi lifecycle improvement opportunity;

\- menangkap lifecycle learning;

\- meningkatkan lifecycle capability;

\- menilai lifecycle maturity;

\- mengidentifikasi maturity gap;

\- mengarahkan maturity improvement;

\- menjaga lifecycle system tetap effective, controlled, traceable, dan adaptive.

F9 berada setelah F8:

\`\`\`text

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

↓

Implementation Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Review / Findings

↓

F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity

↓

Improvement / Maturity Action

↓

Future Lifecycle Cycle

F9 tidak menggantikan F10.

F9 menjawab:

Seberapa baik dan seberapa matang lifecycle system PD-03, dan bagaimana lifecycle capability tersebut ditingkatkan?

F10 menjawab:

Kapan lifecycle architecture dianggap berhasil?

Source secara eksplisit memisahkan maturity assessment pada F9 dari lifecycle success pada F10.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

F9 merupakan child architecture dari F1 — Lifecycle Constitution.

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

↓

↓

F9 tidak boleh mengubah constitutional lifecycle boundary secara informal.

Jika improvement membutuhkan perubahan terhadap:

• authority;

• governance constitution;

• organizational ownership;

• lifecycle model;

• canonical state model;

• transition authority;

maka improvement harus kembali ke applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Principle

Lifecycle improvement shall be evidence-based, controlled, traceable, proportionate to lifecycle risk and materiality, and must preserve the authority and ownership boundaries established by the lifecycle architecture.

Lifecycle improvement bukan sekadar:

• menambah proses;

• menambah dokumen;

• menambah review;

• menambah approval;

• menaikkan maturity label.

Improvement harus menghasilkan peningkatan capability yang dapat dibuktikan.

⸻

4\. Improvement vs Maturity

F9 memiliki dua concern yang berhubungan tetapi berbeda:

Lifecycle Improvement

↓

What should become better?

dan:

Lifecycle Maturity

↓

How capable is the lifecycle system today?

Dengan demikian:

Improvement ≠ Maturity

Improvement adalah mekanisme perubahan capability.

Maturity adalah assessment terhadap capability tersebut.

⸻

5\. Improvement Boundary

F9 mencakup improvement terhadap:

• lifecycle model;

• lifecycle governance;

• lifecycle review;

• lifecycle control;

• lifecycle implementation;

• lifecycle evidence;

• lifecycle traceability;

• lifecycle decision quality;

• lifecycle transition quality;

• lifecycle learning;

• lifecycle capability.

F9 tidak otomatis mencakup:

• strategic objective redesign;

• organizational restructuring;

• technical implementation;

• performance KPI ownership;

• enterprise transformation;

• workforce policy.

Jika improvement tersebut membutuhkan perubahan domain lain, applicable domain architecture harus dilibatkan.

• transition inefficiency;

• evidence quality issue;

• unclear accountability;

• maturity gap;

• emerging lifecycle risk;

• cross-platform coordination issue;

• repeated exception.

Opportunity tidak otomatis berarti change harus dilakukan.

• approvals;

• controls;

• tools;

• meetings.

Maturity harus berdasarkan demonstrated capability.
