• stable;

• adaptable ketika context berubah.

⸻

24\. Lifecycle Model Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle model:

• defined;

• coherent;

• reusable;

• internally consistent;

• aligned dengan organization architecture;

• memiliki clear boundaries.

⸻

25\. State & Transition Maturity

Menilai apakah:

• current state dapat diidentifikasi;

• resulting state dapat diverifikasi;

• transition semantics jelas;

• transition authority jelas;

• invalid transition dapat dideteksi;

• historical state dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

26\. Governance & Authority Maturity

Menilai apakah:

• authority jelas;

• delegation dapat ditelusuri;

• accountability jelas;

• review mechanism berfungsi;

• escalation tersedia;

• authority boundaries dipatuhi.

F9 tidak memiliki authority lebih tinggi daripada Governance Architecture.

⸻

27\. Control & Evidence Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle memiliki:

• control;

• evidence;

• traceability;

• canonical records;

• exception handling;

• verification.

Evidence harus cukup untuk mendukung lifecycle claims.

⸻

28\. Implementation & Verification Maturity

Menilai apakah:

• authorized changes dapat diimplementasikan;

• implementation dapat dikendalikan;

• evidence dapat dikumpulkan;

• resulting state dapat diverifikasi;

• failures dapat ditangani;

• closure dapat dibuktikan.

⸻

29\. Learning & Improvement Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle system:

• menangkap lessons;

• mengidentifikasi recurring problems;

• melakukan improvement;

• memvalidasi improvement;

• mempertahankan learning;

• mengintegrasikan learning ke future lifecycle cycles.

⸻

30\. Adaptability Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle system mampu:

• mendeteksi changing context;

• mengidentifikasi emerging lifecycle needs;

• menyesuaikan mechanism;

• mempertahankan governance integrity;

• mengadaptasi tanpa architectural drift;

• merespons perubahan secara intentional.

Adaptive tidak berarti:

"system berubah terus-menerus."

Adaptive berarti:

system mampu berubah secara intentional, evidence-based, controlled, dan tepat waktu ketika context berubah.

⸻

31\. Proposed Maturity Levels

Baseline tidak menyediakan lifecycle maturity level taxonomy literal.

Untuk construction sementara, digunakan pola berikut:

Initial

↓

Defined

↓

Managed

↓

Optimized

↓

Adaptive

Status: BOUNDED SYNTHESIS — REQUIRES SOURCE RECONCILIATION

Pola tersebut mengikuti maturity architecture yang telah digunakan dalam Performance Architecture, tetapi tidak boleh dianggap otomatis canonical untuk lifecycle tanpa reconciliation.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

32\. Initial

Lifecycle capability berada dalam kondisi:

• reactive;

• inconsistent;

• fragmented;

• person-dependent;

• weakly documented;

• weakly traceable.

Typical condition:

Lifecycle Need

↓

Ad Hoc Response

↓

Manual Decision

↓

Limited Record

⸻

33\. Defined

Lifecycle mechanisms telah:

• documented;

• structured;

• assigned;

• repeatable in principle.

Namun consistency dan effectiveness belum sepenuhnya terbukti.

Primary risk:

Defined on paper, inconsistent in practice.

⸻

34\. Managed

Lifecycle system:

• berjalan consistently;

• memiliki evidence;

• memiliki ownership;

• memiliki controls;

• dapat direview;

• dapat ditelusuri;

• memiliki predictable transition mechanism.

⸻

35\. Optimized

Lifecycle system:

• systematically improved;

• friction reduced;

• control effectiveness improved;

• transition quality improved;

• evidence quality improved;

• learning incorporated.

Optimization harus tetap berada dalam authority boundary.

⸻

36\. Adaptive

Lifecycle system mampu:

• mendeteksi changing context;

• belajar dari lifecycle evidence;

• mengubah improvement priorities;

• menyesuaikan lifecycle mechanism;

• mempertahankan governance integrity;

• menjaga historical continuity;

• merespons material change secara controlled.

Adaptive tidak berarti uncontrolled flexibility.

⸻

37\. Maturity Evidence Principle

Maturity harus ditentukan berdasarkan:

Claim

↓

Criterion

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Conclusion

Tidak boleh:

Label

↓

Assumed Maturity

⸻

38\. Maturity Confidence

Setiap material maturity assessment harus mempertimbangkan confidence.

Confidence dipengaruhi oleh:

• evidence completeness;

• evidence quality;

• evidence recency;

• consistency;

• traceability;

• assessment coverage.

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Do not overclaim maturity.

⸻

39\. Maturity Ceiling

Overall maturity tidak boleh melampaui maturity capability paling critical apabila critical weakness menghambat lifecycle integrity.

Contoh:

Governance = Managed

Implementation = Managed

Evidence = Initial

Maturity keseluruhan tidak boleh sekadar menyatakan:

"Managed"

tanpa memperlihatkan critical evidence weakness.

⸻

40\. Maturity Profile

Maturity harus dapat ditampilkan sebagai profile:

￼

Catatan: tabel ini adalah contoh format, bukan assessment aktual PD-03.

⸻

41\. Maturity Gap

Maturity gap adalah perbedaan antara:

Current Capability

↓

Required / Desired Capability

Namun desired maturity harus memiliki rationale.

Tidak semua lifecycle capability harus mencapai Adaptive.

⸻

42\. Maturity Target

Target maturity harus mempertimbangkan:

• lifecycle criticality;

• risk;

• organizational complexity;

• regulatory/compliance need;

• cross-platform dependency;

• reversibility;

• cost;

• strategic importance.

Target maturity bukan ranking.

⸻

43\. Maturity Improvement Loop

Canonical loop:

Maturity Assessment

↓

Maturity Gap

↓

Improvement Opportunity

↓

Improvement Decision

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

↓

Re-assessment

↓

Updated Maturity Profile

Loop dapat berulang.

⸻

44\. Maturity Regression

Maturity dapat turun.

Possible causes:

• governance degradation;

• loss of capability;

• control failure;

• organizational change;

• evidence degradation;

• process fragmentation;

• dependency failure;

• context change.

Regression harus dicatat sebagai evidence, bukan disembunyikan.

⸻

45\. Maturity Sustainability

Maturity tidak dianggap durable hanya karena assessment sekali menghasilkan level tinggi.

Sustainability membutuhkan:

• repeated evidence;

• stable capability;

• consistent operation;

• continued control;

• continued learning.

High Maturity

↓

Sustained Evidence

↓

Sustainable Maturity

⸻

46\. Improvement Portfolio

Material improvement opportunities dapat dikelompokkan sebagai portfolio.

Potential categories:

• governance improvement;

• transition improvement;

• control improvement;

• evidence improvement;

• implementation improvement;

• learning improvement;

• adaptability improvement.

Portfolio harus mempertahankan prioritization dan ownership.

⸻

47\. Improvement Dependencies

Improvement dapat memiliki dependency:

Governance Improvement

↓

Control Improvement

↓

Implementation Improvement

↓

Maturity Improvement

Dependency harus terlihat.

Improvement tidak boleh dilakukan seolah-olah independent jika dependency material.

⸻

48\. Improvement Trade-Off

Tidak semua improvement harus dilakukan.

Assessment dapat menghasilkan:

Improve

Maintain

Defer

Reject

Monitor

Escalate

Keputusan harus memiliki rationale apabila material.

⸻

49\. Improvement and Lifecycle Stability

Improvement tidak boleh menciptakan unnecessary instability.

Prinsip:

Improve where evidence demonstrates meaningful benefit.

Jangan mengubah lifecycle mechanism hanya karena:

• novelty;

• preference;

• cosmetic simplification;

• trend;

• tool availability.

⸻

50\. Improvement and Governance Integrity

Improvement tidak boleh:

• bypass authority;

• remove mandatory controls;

• weaken traceability;

• obscure accountability;

• erase history;

• alter canonical state without authority.

Improvement harus meningkatkan lifecycle system tanpa merusak constitutional integrity.

⸻

51\. Improvement and Cross-Platform Coordination

Jika improvement memiliki cross-platform impact:

Lifecycle Improvement

↓

Affected Platform

↓

Coordination

↓

Applicable Domain Owner

↓

Implementation

PD-03 tetap mempertahankan Governance & Compliance ownership.

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih execution ownership Platform lain.

⸻

52\. Improvement and Performance Architecture

Part E dapat menyediakan evidence untuk F9.

Performance Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Finding

↓

Improvement Opportunity

Namun:

Performance Result

≠

Automatic Lifecycle Decision

Lifecycle authority tetap berada pada applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

53\. Improvement and Operations

Part D menghasilkan operational evidence.

F9 dapat menggunakan evidence tersebut sebagai improvement input.

Part D

Operating Evidence

↓

F6/F9

Lifecycle Review / Improvement

F9 tidak mengambil alih operating model.

Jika lifecycle improvement membutuhkan perubahan organisasi:

F9

Improvement Proposal

↓

Applicable Organization Architecture

Governance Decision

↓

F8 Implementation

F9 tidak memiliki automatic organizational restructuring authority.

57\. Maturity Review

Maturity review harus:

• menggunakan evidence;

• mempertimbangkan lifecycle history;

• mempertimbangkan recurring findings;

• mempertimbangkan improvement effectiveness;

• mempertimbangkan regression;

• mempertimbangkan sustainability.

Maturity review tidak boleh menjadi ranking exercise.

⸻

58\. Maturity Reporting

Maturity reporting harus memberikan visibility terhadap:

• current maturity;

• maturity profile;

• confidence;

• bottleneck;

• maturity gap;

• trend;

• improvement priorities;

• regression;

• unresolved evidence gaps.

Reporting dapat diintegrasikan dengan Part E reporting mechanisms apabila applicable.

⸻

59\. Maturity and Executive Decision

Maturity information dapat menjadi input executive decision.

Namun:

Maturity Assessment

↓

Decision Input

bukan:

Maturity Assessment

↓

Automatic Authority

Maturity tidak dapat secara otomatis:

• approve;

• reject;

• restructure;

• retire;

• dissolve;

• reassign ownership.

⸻

60\. Maturity and Compliance

Compliance maturity dapat menjadi bagian dari lifecycle assessment apabila applicable.

Namun F9 tidak menggantikan Compliance function.

PD-03 tetap memiliki governance/compliance responsibility.

Domain owner tetap bertanggung jawab atas execution dalam domainnya.

⸻

61\. Lifecycle Learning Repository

Material lessons learned harus dapat dipertahankan secara traceable.

Learning repository dapat berisi:

• event;

• evidence;

• finding;

• decision;

• improvement;

• validation;

• lesson.

Historical learning harus dapat digunakan untuk future lifecycle assessment.

⸻

62\. Anti-Gaming Principle

Lifecycle maturity tidak boleh dimanipulasi melalui:

• documentation inflation;

• metric inflation;

• artificial compliance;

• selective evidence;

• hiding exceptions;

• hiding failures;

• relabeling states;

• redefining criteria after assessment.

Maturity harus merefleksikan observed capability.

⸻

63\. Improvement Failure

Jika improvement gagal:

Improvement Failure

↓

Record

↓

Assess Cause

↓

Learning

↓

Re-prioritize

↓

Retry / Replace / Stop

Failure bukan alasan untuk menghapus historical evidence.

⸻

64\. Improvement Stop Condition

Improvement dapat dihentikan apabila:

• expected benefit tidak lagi valid;

• evidence menunjukkan approach tidak efektif;

• risk meningkat;

• dependency berubah;

• authority berubah;

• improvement tidak lagi material;

• target maturity telah tercapai;

• cost-benefit tidak justified.

Stop harus memiliki rationale apabila material.

⸻

65\. Improvement Completion

Improvement dianggap complete apabila:

0\. authorized improvement telah implemented;

0\. evidence tersedia;

0\. validation selesai;

0\. expected outcome dinilai;

0\. maturity impact dinilai apabila applicable;

0\. lessons captured;

0\. record diperbarui.

Implemented

↓

Validated

↓

Recorded

↓

Learned

⸻

66\. Improvement vs Lifecycle Success

Improvement success tidak sama dengan lifecycle success.

Improvement Success

≠

Lifecycle Success

F9 menilai apakah capability meningkat.

F10 menentukan apakah lifecycle architecture secara keseluruhan memenuhi success conditions.

⸻

67\. Maturity vs Lifecycle Success

Maturity tinggi tidak otomatis berarti lifecycle success.

Sebaliknya, lifecycle success pada satu event tidak otomatis berarti maturity tinggi.

Maturity

↓

Capability Quality

Success

↓

Outcome Quality

Keduanya harus dipertahankan sebagai separate concepts.

73\. Lifecycle Capability Improvement

F9 improvement target dapat mencakup:

Lifecycle Capability

├── Governance

├── Decision

├── Review

├── Control

├── Implementation

Target improvement harus dipilih berdasarkan evidence.

⸻

74\. Lifecycle Maturity Profile

PD-03 dapat memiliki maturity profile yang berbeda antar-dimension.

Contoh:

Intent → Managed

Model → Defined

State & Transition → Defined

Governance → Managed

Control & Evidence → Managed

Implementation → Defined

Learning → Initial

Adaptability → Initial

Contoh tersebut bukan assessment aktual.

⸻

75\. Maturity Bottleneck

Jika satu critical dimension sangat rendah:

Governance → Managed

Control → Managed

Evidence → Initial

overall maturity tidak boleh menyembunyikan evidence bottleneck.

Maturity report harus menunjukkan:

Critical Bottleneck

apabila applicable.

⸻

76\. Maturity Progression

Progression harus didasarkan pada demonstrated capability.

Initial

↓

Evidence of Definition

↓

Defined

↓

Evidence of Consistent Operation

↓

Managed

↓

Evidence of Systematic Optimization

↓

Optimized

↓

Evidence of Controlled Adaptation

↓

Adaptive

Label maturity tidak boleh naik hanya karena target waktu telah tercapai.

⸻

77\. Maturity Regression

Jika evidence menunjukkan capability menurun:

Managed

↓

Regression Evidence

↓

Defined

Regression harus:

• dicatat;

• dianalisis;

• communicated;

• menjadi improvement input.

Regression bukan kegagalan governance dengan sendirinya.

⸻

78\. Maturity Sustainability

Maturity tinggi harus dipertahankan melalui:

• repeated evidence;

• control;

• review;

• learning;

• improvement;

• continuity.

Maturity tidak boleh dianggap permanent.

⸻

79\. Lifecycle Improvement Principles

F9 mengikuti prinsip:

LI-01 — Evidence Before Improvement

Tidak ada material improvement tanpa evidence.

LI-02 — Improve the System, Not the Label

Improvement harus meningkatkan capability, bukan sekadar maturity score.

LI-03 — Preserve Governance Integrity

Improvement tidak boleh bypass authority.

LI-04 — Preserve Historical Continuity

Improvement tidak boleh menghapus lifecycle history.

LI-05 — Proportional Improvement

Effort harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

LI-06 — Validate Improvement

Improvement harus diuji terhadap expected outcome.

LI-07 — Learn From Failure

Failure menjadi learning input.

LI-08 — Preserve Platform Autonomy

Cross-platform improvement tidak mengambil ownership execution.

LI-09 — No Automatic Authority

Maturity assessment tidak menciptakan authority.

LI-10 — Controlled Adaptability

Adaptability harus intentional, evidence-based, dan controlled.

⸻

80\. Out of Scope

F9 tidak menetapkan:

• lifecycle authority;

• definitive lifecycle state taxonomy;

• definitive transition matrix;

• governance constitution;

• organizational restructuring authority;

• technical implementation architecture;

• Platform execution ownership;

• enterprise performance KPI;

• workforce maturity;

• compensation;

• automatic maturity sanctions;

• automatic organizational consequences.

0\. PD-03 Governance Baseline;

0\. F2 — Lifecycle Model;

0\. F3 — Lifecycle States;

0\. F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

83\. Success Criteria

F9 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. lifecycle improvement dibedakan dari lifecycle maturity;

0\. improvement memiliki evidence basis;

0\. improvement memiliki identifiable opportunity;

0\. material improvement memiliki applicable authority;

0\. improvement memiliki owner;

0\. improvement memiliki validation;

0\. lifecycle learning dapat ditangkap;

0\. recurring failure dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. improvement failure dapat dipelajari;

0\. improvement tidak menghapus historical evidence;

0\. improvement tidak menciptakan authority baru;

0\. improvement tidak memperluas PD-03 ownership;

0\. cross-platform improvement mempertahankan Platform autonomy;

0\. maturity assessment berada pada F9;

0\. maturity dibedakan dari lifecycle success;

0\. maturity assessment berbasis evidence;

0\. maturity confidence dapat dinilai;

0\. insufficient evidence dapat mencegah overclaim;

0\. maturity profile dapat memperlihatkan dimension-level weakness;

0\. maturity gap dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. maturity improvement loop tersedia;

0\. maturity regression dapat dicatat;

0\. maturity sustainability dapat dinilai;

0\. maturity target memiliki rationale;

0\. maturity tidak menjadi ranking exercise;

0\. maturity tidak menciptakan automatic sanction;

0\. lifecycle improvement tidak digunakan untuk bypass governance;

0\. lifecycle improvement tetap terhubung dengan F8 implementation;

0\. lifecycle improvement dapat menggunakan F6 findings;

0\. lifecycle improvement dapat menggunakan Part E evidence;

0\. F9 tidak mengambil alih F10 success determination;

0\. tidak terdapat material contradiction dengan F1–F8;

0\. F9 dapat menjadi reusable lifecycle improvement/maturity pattern setelah domain adaptation.

85\. Canonical Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity Statement

F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity establishes the architecture through which PD-03 continuously learns from lifecycle evidence, identifies and validates improvement opportunities, and assesses the maturity of its lifecycle capability while preserving governance authority, organizational ownership, Platform autonomy, historical continuity, evidence integrity, and lifecycle control.

Lifecycle improvement shall improve demonstrated capability rather than merely improve documentation or maturity labels. Lifecycle maturity shall be determined from observable evidence and shall not create authority, organizational ownership, or automatic consequence beyond the applicable governance architecture.

Where source does not define a definitive lifecycle maturity taxonomy, the taxonomy shall remain explicitly bounded until reconciled against the canonical AIOS architecture corpus.

\# 1. Purpose

F9 menetapkan architecture untuk:

\- mengevaluasi kualitas lifecycle system;

\- mengidentifikasi lifecycle improvement opportunity;

\- menangkap lifecycle learning;

\- meningkatkan lifecycle capability;

\- menilai lifecycle maturity;

\- mengidentifikasi maturity gap;

\- mengarahkan maturity improvement;

\- menjaga lifecycle system tetap effective, controlled, traceable, dan adaptive.

F9 berada setelah F8:

\`\`\`text

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

↓

Implementation Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Review / Findings

↓

F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity

↓

Improvement / Maturity Action

↓

Future Lifecycle Cycle

F9 tidak menggantikan F10.

F9 menjawab:

Seberapa baik dan seberapa matang lifecycle system PD-03, dan bagaimana lifecycle capability tersebut ditingkatkan?

F10 menjawab:

Kapan lifecycle architecture dianggap berhasil?

Source secara eksplisit memisahkan maturity assessment pada F9 dari lifecycle success pada F10.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

F9 merupakan child architecture dari F1 — Lifecycle Constitution.

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

↓

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

↓

F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity

↓

F10 — Lifecycle Success

F9 tidak boleh mengubah constitutional lifecycle boundary secara informal.

Jika improvement membutuhkan perubahan terhadap:

• authority;

• governance constitution;

• organizational ownership;

• lifecycle model;

• canonical state model;

• transition authority;

maka improvement harus kembali ke applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Principle

Lifecycle improvement shall be evidence-based, controlled, traceable, proportionate to lifecycle risk and materiality, and must preserve the authority and ownership boundaries established by the lifecycle architecture.

Lifecycle improvement bukan sekadar:

• menambah proses;

• menambah dokumen;

• menambah review;

• menambah approval;

• menaikkan maturity label.

Improvement harus menghasilkan peningkatan capability yang dapat dibuktikan.

⸻

4\. Improvement vs Maturity

F9 memiliki dua concern yang berhubungan tetapi berbeda:

Lifecycle Improvement

↓

What should become better?

dan:

Lifecycle Maturity

↓

How capable is the lifecycle system today?

Dengan demikian:

Improvement ≠ Maturity

Improvement adalah mekanisme perubahan capability.

Maturity adalah assessment terhadap capability tersebut.

⸻

5\. Improvement Boundary

F9 mencakup improvement terhadap:

• lifecycle model;

• lifecycle governance;

• lifecycle review;

• lifecycle control;

• lifecycle implementation;

• lifecycle evidence;

• lifecycle traceability;

• lifecycle decision quality;

• lifecycle transition quality;

• lifecycle learning;

• lifecycle capability.

F9 tidak otomatis mencakup:

• strategic objective redesign;

• organizational restructuring;

• technical implementation;

• performance KPI ownership;

• enterprise transformation;

• workforce policy.

Jika improvement tersebut membutuhkan perubahan domain lain, applicable domain architecture harus dilibatkan.

⸻

6\. Lifecycle Improvement Inputs

F9 menerima improvement signal dari:

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

↓

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

↓

Part E — Performance Architecture

↓

Compliance / Governance Findings

↓

Operational Feedback

↓

Lifecycle Evidence

Potential inputs:

• transition failure;

• implementation friction;

• control finding;

• repeated exception;

• state ambiguity;

• evidence weakness;

• traceability gap;

• review finding;

• resulting-state mismatch;

• lifecycle performance observation;

• recurring governance issue;

• maturity gap.

⸻

7\. Evidence Before Improvement

Lifecycle improvement tidak boleh dimulai hanya berdasarkan preference.

Canonical pattern:

Observation

↓

Evidence

↓

Finding

↓

Improvement Opportunity

↓

Assessment

↓

Improvement Decision

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

No Unsupported Improvement Claim

⸻

8\. Improvement Opportunity

Improvement opportunity adalah kondisi ketika evidence menunjukkan bahwa lifecycle capability dapat atau perlu ditingkatkan.

Opportunity dapat berasal dari:

• recurring failure;

• unnecessary friction;

• control weakness;

• transition inefficiency;

• evidence quality issue;

• unclear accountability;

• maturity gap;

• emerging lifecycle risk;

• cross-platform coordination issue;

• repeated exception.

Opportunity tidak otomatis berarti change harus dilakukan.

⸻

9\. Improvement Assessment

Setiap material improvement opportunity harus dinilai terhadap:

• materiality;

• impact;

• urgency;

• lifecycle risk;

• dependency;

• implementation complexity;

• authority;

• expected benefit;

• reversibility;

• cross-platform impact.

Assessment harus proporsional.

Tidak semua improvement membutuhkan formal architecture decision.

⸻

10\. Improvement Prioritization

Improvement dapat diprioritaskan berdasarkan:

Impact

Risk

Urgency

Evidence Strength

Dependency

Effort

Expected Benefit

Namun priority tidak boleh mengubah authority hierarchy.

Prioritas adalah input keputusan.

Bukan authority baru.

⸻

Canonical improvement lifecycle:

Jika improvement tidak membutuhkan material authorization, applicable lightweight path dapat digunakan.

Threshold formal harus direconcile oleh Claude Code.

F9 tidak menciptakan improvement authority.

Model:

13\. Improvement Implementation

Improvement implementation mengikuti F8.

14\. Improvement Validation

Improvement dianggap meaningful hanya apabila terdapat evidence bahwa improvement menghasilkan perubahan yang diharapkan.

Canonical pattern:

Baseline

↓

Improvement

↓

Observed Result

↓

Comparison

↓

Validation

Tidak cukup mengatakan:

"process sudah diperbaiki."

Harus dapat ditunjukkan:

"capability apa yang membaik dan berdasarkan evidence apa?"

⸻

15\. Improvement Effectiveness

Improvement effectiveness dapat dinilai terhadap:

• lifecycle correctness;

• transition reliability;

• governance clarity;

• evidence completeness;

• control effectiveness;

• traceability;

• operational continuity;

• decision quality;

• implementation quality.

Improvement tidak dianggap successful hanya karena selesai diimplementasikan.

Implemented ≠ Effective

⸻

16\. Lifecycle Learning

F9 harus menangkap learning dari lifecycle activity.

Learning dapat berasal dari:

• successful transition;

• failed transition;

• exception;

• control failure;

• implementation failure;

• review finding;

• maturity assessment;

• cross-platform event.

Learning harus dapat digunakan untuk future lifecycle decisions.

⸻

17\. Lessons Learned

Material lessons learned harus dapat dikaitkan dengan:

• lifecycle subject;

• lifecycle event;

• evidence;

• finding;

• root cause;

• improvement;

• validation.

Canonical relationship:

Lifecycle Event

↓

Evidence

↓

Finding

↓

Lesson

↓

Improvement

↓

Future Lifecycle Capability

⸻

18\. Recurring Failure Detection

F9 harus memperhatikan recurring lifecycle problems.

Contoh:

Exception

↓

Exception

↓

Exception

↓

Recurring Pattern

Recurring pattern dapat menjadi signal bahwa:

• process terlalu complex;

• authority unclear;

• control ineffective;

• lifecycle model incomplete;

• implementation mechanism weak;

• organizational dependency unresolved.

⸻

19\. Root Cause Principle

Improvement tidak boleh berhenti pada symptom.

Canonical pattern:

Observed Problem

↓

Immediate Cause

↓

Systemic Cause

↓

Root Cause

↓

Improvement

Root cause analysis harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

20\. Lifecycle Maturity

Maturity menjawab:

Seberapa capable lifecycle system dalam menjalankan lifecycle governance secara konsisten, controlled, evidence-based, dan adaptive?

Maturity tidak sama dengan jumlah:

• documents;

• approvals;

• controls;

• tools;

• meetings.

Maturity harus berdasarkan demonstrated capability.

⸻

21\. Maturity Assessment Boundary

F9 memiliki ownership atas lifecycle maturity assessment.

Assessment dapat mencakup:

• lifecycle clarity;

• lifecycle governance;

• lifecycle state integrity;

• transition quality;

• lifecycle control;

• implementation quality;

• evidence and traceability;

• learning;

• improvement capability;

• adaptability.

Source-fidelity qualification: dimensi lifecycle maturity tersebut merupakan bounded synthesis. Baseline hanya secara eksplisit menempatkan maturity assessment pada F9; baseline tidak memberikan definitive lifecycle maturity dimension catalogue.

⸻

22\. Proposed Lifecycle Maturity Dimensions

Sebagai working canonical synthesis, F9 menggunakan dimensi berikut:

1\. Lifecycle Intent

2\. Lifecycle Model

3\. Lifecycle State & Transition

4\. Governance & Authority

5\. Control & Evidence

6\. Implementation & Verification

7\. Learning & Improvement

8\. Adaptability

Dimensi ini harus direconcile terhadap canonical AIOS corpus oleh Claude Code sebelum menjadi immutable vocabulary.

⸻

23\. Lifecycle Intent Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle purpose dan boundaries:

• jelas;

• traceable;

• understood;

• aligned;

• stable;

• adaptable ketika context berubah.

⸻

24\. Lifecycle Model Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle model:

• defined;

• coherent;

• reusable;

• internally consistent;

• aligned dengan organization architecture;

• memiliki clear boundaries.

⸻

25\. State & Transition Maturity

Menilai apakah:

• current state dapat diidentifikasi;

• resulting state dapat diverifikasi;

• transition semantics jelas;

• transition authority jelas;

• invalid transition dapat dideteksi;

• historical state dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

26\. Governance & Authority Maturity

Menilai apakah:

• authority jelas;

• delegation dapat ditelusuri;

• accountability jelas;

• review mechanism berfungsi;

• escalation tersedia;

• authority boundaries dipatuhi.

F9 tidak memiliki authority lebih tinggi daripada Governance Architecture.

⸻

27\. Control & Evidence Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle memiliki:

• control;

• evidence;

• traceability;

• canonical records;

• exception handling;

• verification.

Evidence harus cukup untuk mendukung lifecycle claims.

⸻

28\. Implementation & Verification Maturity

Menilai apakah:

• authorized changes dapat diimplementasikan;

• implementation dapat dikendalikan;

• evidence dapat dikumpulkan;

• resulting state dapat diverifikasi;

• failures dapat ditangani;

• closure dapat dibuktikan.

⸻

29\. Learning & Improvement Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle system:

• menangkap lessons;

• mengidentifikasi recurring problems;

• melakukan improvement;

• memvalidasi improvement;

• mempertahankan learning;

• mengintegrasikan learning ke future lifecycle cycles.

⸻

30\. Adaptability Maturity

Menilai apakah lifecycle system mampu:

• mendeteksi changing context;

• mengidentifikasi emerging lifecycle needs;

• menyesuaikan mechanism;

• mempertahankan governance integrity;

• mengadaptasi tanpa architectural drift;

• merespons perubahan secara intentional.

Adaptive tidak berarti:

"system berubah terus-menerus."

Adaptive berarti:

system mampu berubah secara intentional, evidence-based, controlled, dan tepat waktu ketika context berubah.

⸻

31\. Proposed Maturity Levels

Baseline tidak menyediakan lifecycle maturity level taxonomy literal.

Untuk construction sementara, digunakan pola berikut:

Initial

↓

Defined

↓

Managed

↓

Optimized

↓

Adaptive

Status: BOUNDED SYNTHESIS — REQUIRES SOURCE RECONCILIATION

Pola tersebut mengikuti maturity architecture yang telah digunakan dalam Performance Architecture, tetapi tidak boleh dianggap otomatis canonical untuk lifecycle tanpa reconciliation.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

32\. Initial

Lifecycle capability berada dalam kondisi:

• reactive;

• inconsistent;

• fragmented;

• person-dependent;

• weakly documented;

• weakly traceable.

Typical condition:

Lifecycle Need

↓

Ad Hoc Response

↓

Manual Decision

↓

Limited Record

⸻

33\. Defined

Lifecycle mechanisms telah:

• documented;

• structured;

• assigned;

• repeatable in principle.

Namun consistency dan effectiveness belum sepenuhnya terbukti.

Primary risk:

Defined on paper, inconsistent in practice.

⸻

34\. Managed

Lifecycle system:

• berjalan consistently;

• memiliki evidence;

• memiliki ownership;

• memiliki controls;

• dapat direview;

• dapat ditelusuri;

• memiliki predictable transition mechanism.

⸻

35\. Optimized

Lifecycle system:

• systematically improved;

• friction reduced;

• control effectiveness improved;

• transition quality improved;

• evidence quality improved;

• learning incorporated.

Optimization harus tetap berada dalam authority boundary.

⸻

36\. Adaptive

Lifecycle system mampu:

• mendeteksi changing context;

• belajar dari lifecycle evidence;

• mengubah improvement priorities;

• menyesuaikan lifecycle mechanism;

• mempertahankan governance integrity;

• menjaga historical continuity;

• merespons material change secara controlled.

Adaptive tidak berarti uncontrolled flexibility.

⸻

37\. Maturity Evidence Principle

Maturity harus ditentukan berdasarkan:

Claim

↓

Criterion

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Conclusion

Tidak boleh:

Label

↓

Assumed Maturity

⸻

38\. Maturity Confidence

Setiap material maturity assessment harus mempertimbangkan confidence.

Confidence dipengaruhi oleh:

• evidence completeness;

• evidence quality;

• evidence recency;

• consistency;

• traceability;

• assessment coverage.

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Do not overclaim maturity.

⸻

39\. Maturity Ceiling

Overall maturity tidak boleh melampaui maturity capability paling critical apabila critical weakness menghambat lifecycle integrity.

Contoh:

Governance = Managed

Implementation = Managed

Evidence = Initial

Maturity keseluruhan tidak boleh sekadar menyatakan:

"Managed"

tanpa memperlihatkan critical evidence weakness.

⸻

40\. Maturity Profile

Maturity harus dapat ditampilkan sebagai profile:

Dimension Level Confidence

Lifecycle Intent Defined Medium

Lifecycle Model Managed High

State & Transition Defined Medium

Governance & Authority Managed High

Control & Evidence Defined Medium

Implementation & Verification Managed High

Learning & Improvement Initial Medium

Adaptability Initial Low

Catatan: tabel ini adalah contoh format, bukan assessment aktual PD-03.

⸻

41\. Maturity Gap

Maturity gap adalah perbedaan antara:

Current Capability

↓

Required / Desired Capability

Namun desired maturity harus memiliki rationale.

Tidak semua lifecycle capability harus mencapai Adaptive.

⸻

42\. Maturity Target

Target maturity harus mempertimbangkan:

• lifecycle criticality;

• risk;

• organizational complexity;

• regulatory/compliance need;

• cross-platform dependency;

• reversibility;

• cost;

• strategic importance.

Target maturity bukan ranking.

⸻

43\. Maturity Improvement Loop

Canonical loop:

Maturity Assessment

↓

Maturity Gap

↓

Improvement Opportunity

↓

Improvement Decision

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

↓

Re-assessment

↓

Updated Maturity Profile

Loop dapat berulang.

⸻

44\. Maturity Regression

Maturity dapat turun.

Possible causes:

• governance degradation;

• loss of capability;

• control failure;

• organizational change;

• evidence degradation;

• process fragmentation;

• dependency failure;

• context change.

Regression harus dicatat sebagai evidence, bukan disembunyikan.

⸻

45\. Maturity Sustainability

Maturity tidak dianggap durable hanya karena assessment sekali menghasilkan level tinggi.

Sustainability membutuhkan:

• repeated evidence;

• stable capability;

• consistent operation;

• continued control;

• continued learning.

High Maturity

↓

Sustained Evidence

↓

Sustainable Maturity

⸻

46\. Improvement Portfolio

Material improvement opportunities dapat dikelompokkan sebagai portfolio.

Potential categories:

• governance improvement;

• transition improvement;

• control improvement;

• evidence improvement;

• implementation improvement;

• learning improvement;

• adaptability improvement.

Portfolio harus mempertahankan prioritization dan ownership.

⸻

47\. Improvement Dependencies

Improvement dapat memiliki dependency:

Governance Improvement

↓

Control Improvement

↓

Implementation Improvement

↓

Maturity Improvement

Dependency harus terlihat.

Improvement tidak boleh dilakukan seolah-olah independent jika dependency material.

⸻

48\. Improvement Trade-Off

Tidak semua improvement harus dilakukan.

Assessment dapat menghasilkan:

Improve

Maintain

Defer

Reject

Monitor

Escalate

Keputusan harus memiliki rationale apabila material.

⸻

49\. Improvement and Lifecycle Stability

Improvement tidak boleh menciptakan unnecessary instability.

Prinsip:

Improve where evidence demonstrates meaningful benefit.

Jangan mengubah lifecycle mechanism hanya karena:

• novelty;

• preference;

• cosmetic simplification;

• trend;

• tool availability.

⸻

50\. Improvement and Governance Integrity

Improvement tidak boleh:

• bypass authority;

• remove mandatory controls;

• weaken traceability;

• obscure accountability;

• erase history;

• alter canonical state without authority.

Improvement harus meningkatkan lifecycle system tanpa merusak constitutional integrity.

⸻

51\. Improvement and Cross-Platform Coordination

Jika improvement memiliki cross-platform impact:

Lifecycle Improvement

↓

Affected Platform

↓

Coordination

↓

Applicable Domain Owner

↓

Implementation

PD-03 tetap mempertahankan Governance & Compliance ownership.

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih execution ownership Platform lain.

⸻

56\. Maturity Assessment Record

Material maturity assessment harus dapat mengidentifikasi:

Field Requirement

Assessment ID Required

Assessment Scope Required

Assessment Date Required

Assessor Required

Dimensions Required

Criteria Required

Evidence Required

Confidence Required

Findings Required

Maturity Profile Required

57\. Maturity Review

Maturity review harus:

• menggunakan evidence;

• mempertimbangkan lifecycle history;

• mempertimbangkan recurring findings;

• mempertimbangkan improvement effectiveness;

• mempertimbangkan regression;

• mempertimbangkan sustainability.

Maturity review tidak boleh menjadi ranking exercise.

⸻

58\. Maturity Reporting

Maturity reporting harus memberikan visibility terhadap:

• current maturity;

• maturity profile;

• confidence;

• bottleneck;

• maturity gap;

• trend;

• improvement priorities;

• regression;

• unresolved evidence gaps.

Reporting dapat diintegrasikan dengan Part E reporting mechanisms apabila applicable.

⸻

59\. Maturity and Executive Decision

Maturity information dapat menjadi input executive decision.

Namun:

Maturity Assessment

↓

Decision Input

bukan:

Maturity Assessment

↓

Automatic Authority

Maturity tidak dapat secara otomatis:

• approve;

• reject;

• restructure;

• retire;

• dissolve;

• reassign ownership.

⸻

60\. Maturity and Compliance

Compliance maturity dapat menjadi bagian dari lifecycle assessment apabila applicable.

Namun F9 tidak menggantikan Compliance function.

PD-03 tetap memiliki governance/compliance responsibility.

Domain owner tetap bertanggung jawab atas execution dalam domainnya.

⸻

61\. Lifecycle Learning Repository

Material lessons learned harus dapat dipertahankan secara traceable.

Learning repository dapat berisi:

• event;

• evidence;

• finding;

• decision;

• improvement;

• validation;

• lesson.

Historical learning harus dapat digunakan untuk future lifecycle assessment.

⸻

62\. Anti-Gaming Principle

Lifecycle maturity tidak boleh dimanipulasi melalui:

• documentation inflation;

• metric inflation;

• artificial compliance;

• selective evidence;

• hiding exceptions;

• hiding failures;

• relabeling states;

• redefining criteria after assessment.

Maturity harus merefleksikan observed capability.

⸻

63\. Improvement Failure

Jika improvement gagal:

Improvement Failure

↓

Record

↓

Assess Cause

↓

Learning

↓

Re-prioritize

↓

Retry / Replace / Stop

Failure bukan alasan untuk menghapus historical evidence.

⸻

64\. Improvement Stop Condition

Improvement dapat dihentikan apabila:

• expected benefit tidak lagi valid;

• evidence menunjukkan approach tidak efektif;

• risk meningkat;

• dependency berubah;

• authority berubah;

• improvement tidak lagi material;

• target maturity telah tercapai;

• cost-benefit tidak justified.

Stop harus memiliki rationale apabila material.

⸻

65\. Improvement Completion

Improvement dianggap complete apabila:

1\. authorized improvement telah implemented;

2\. evidence tersedia;

3\. validation selesai;

4\. expected outcome dinilai;

5\. maturity impact dinilai apabila applicable;

6\. lessons captured;

7\. record diperbarui.

Implemented

↓

Validated

↓

Recorded

↓

Learned

⸻

66\. Improvement vs Lifecycle Success

Improvement success tidak sama dengan lifecycle success.

Improvement Success

≠

Lifecycle Success

F9 menilai apakah capability meningkat.

F10 menentukan apakah lifecycle architecture secara keseluruhan memenuhi success conditions.

⸻

67\. Maturity vs Lifecycle Success

Maturity tinggi tidak otomatis berarti lifecycle success.

Sebaliknya, lifecycle success pada satu event tidak otomatis berarti maturity tinggi.

Maturity

↓

Capability Quality

Success

↓

Outcome Quality

Keduanya harus dipertahankan sebagai separate concepts.

⸻

• implementation learning;

• implementation maturity.

F8

Implementation

↓

Evidence

↓

F9

Learning / Improvement

⸻

72\. F9 Relationship to Part E

Part E dapat menyediakan:

• performance evidence;

• review evidence;

• improvement evidence;

• maturity evidence.

F9 menggunakan evidence tersebut untuk lifecycle maturity.

Namun F9 tidak mengubah Performance Architecture.

⸻

73\. Lifecycle Capability Improvement

F9 improvement target dapat mencakup:

Lifecycle Capability

├── Governance

├── Decision

├── Review

├── Control

├── Transition

├── Implementation

├── Verification

├── Evidence

├── Learning

└── Adaptation

Target improvement harus dipilih berdasarkan evidence.

⸻

74\. Lifecycle Maturity Profile

PD-03 dapat memiliki maturity profile yang berbeda antar-dimension.

Contoh:

Intent → Managed

Model → Defined

State & Transition → Defined

Governance → Managed

Control & Evidence → Managed

Implementation → Defined

Learning → Initial

Adaptability → Initial

Contoh tersebut bukan assessment aktual.

⸻

75\. Maturity Bottleneck

Jika satu critical dimension sangat rendah:

Governance → Managed

Control → Managed

Evidence → Initial

overall maturity tidak boleh menyembunyikan evidence bottleneck.

Maturity report harus menunjukkan:

Critical Bottleneck

apabila applicable.

⸻

76\. Maturity Progression

Progression harus didasarkan pada demonstrated capability.

Initial

↓

Evidence of Definition

↓

Defined

↓

Evidence of Consistent Operation

↓

Managed

↓

Evidence of Systematic Optimization

↓

Optimized

↓

Evidence of Controlled Adaptation

↓

Adaptive

Label maturity tidak boleh naik hanya karena target waktu telah tercapai.

⸻

77\. Maturity Regression

Jika evidence menunjukkan capability menurun:

Managed

↓

Regression Evidence

↓

Defined

Regression harus:

• dicatat;

• dianalisis;

• communicated;

• menjadi improvement input.

Regression bukan kegagalan governance dengan sendirinya.

⸻

78\. Maturity Sustainability

Maturity tinggi harus dipertahankan melalui:

• repeated evidence;

• control;

• review;

• learning;

• improvement;

• continuity.

Maturity tidak boleh dianggap permanent.

⸻

79\. Lifecycle Improvement Principles

F9 mengikuti prinsip:

LI-01 — Evidence Before Improvement

Tidak ada material improvement tanpa evidence.

LI-02 — Improve the System, Not the Label

Improvement harus meningkatkan capability, bukan sekadar maturity score.

LI-03 — Preserve Governance Integrity

Improvement tidak boleh bypass authority.

LI-04 — Preserve Historical Continuity

Improvement tidak boleh menghapus lifecycle history.

LI-05 — Proportional Improvement

Effort harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

LI-06 — Validate Improvement

Improvement harus diuji terhadap expected outcome.

LI-07 — Learn From Failure

Failure menjadi learning input.

LI-08 — Preserve Platform Autonomy

Cross-platform improvement tidak mengambil ownership execution.

LI-09 — No Automatic Authority

Maturity assessment tidak menciptakan authority.

LI-10 — Controlled Adaptability

Adaptability harus intentional, evidence-based, dan controlled.

⸻

80\. Out of Scope

F9 tidak menetapkan:

• lifecycle authority;

• definitive lifecycle state taxonomy;

• definitive transition matrix;

• governance constitution;

• organizational restructuring authority;

• technical implementation architecture;

• Platform execution ownership;

• enterprise performance KPI;

• workforce maturity;

• compensation;

• automatic maturity sanctions;

• automatic organizational consequences.

⸻

81\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Source yang tersedia secara eksplisit mendukung:

1\. F9 sebagai Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity;

2\. maturity assessment ditempatkan pada F9;

3\. lifecycle success ditempatkan pada F10;

4\. lifecycle system harus mempertahankan authority, ownership, evidence, traceability, boundary, dan resulting-state integrity;

5\. lifecycle improvement/maturity merupakan child concern setelah lifecycle implementation;

6\. maturity architecture di AIOS secara umum menggunakan evidence-based maturity dan maturity improvement loop.

Namun source tidak memberikan secara literal:

• F9 maturity dimensions;

• F9 maturity levels;

• F9 scoring model;

• F9 confidence formula;

• F9 maturity ceiling formula;

• F9 improvement portfolio schema;

• F9 maturity assessment record schema.

BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS — REQUIRES CLAUDE CODE SOURCE RECONCILIATION

82\. Claude Code Completion Directive

Claude Code wajib melakukan source reconciliation terhadap:

1\. PD-03 Governance Baseline;

2\. F1 — Lifecycle Constitution;

3\. F2 — Lifecycle Model;

4\. F3 — Lifecycle States;

5\. F4 — Lifecycle Transition;

6\. F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance;

7\. F6 — Lifecycle Review;

8\. F7 — Lifecycle Control;

9\. F8 — Lifecycle Implementation;

11\. Part C — Governance Architecture;

12\. Part D — Governance Operations;

13\. Part E — Performance Architecture;

14\. existing AIOS lifecycle architecture;

15\. existing AIOS maturity models;

16\. existing improvement mechanisms;

17\. applicable Founder / Co-Founder delegation;

18\. canonical architecture decision records.

Claude Code harus memverifikasi khususnya:

• apakah maturity levels Initial → Defined → Managed → Optimized → Adaptive memang canonical untuk lifecycle;

• apakah delapan maturity dimensions sesuai dengan canonical AIOS vocabulary;

• apakah maturity ceiling/bottleneck principle berlaku untuk lifecycle;

• apakah confidence model perlu digunakan;

• apakah maturity assessment cadence sudah ditetapkan;

• apakah maturity memiliki mandatory reviewer;

• apakah lifecycle improvement memiliki dedicated record schema;

• apakah improvement authority threshold telah ditentukan;

• apakah lifecycle maturity dapat diwariskan ke PD-01–PD-10;

• bagaimana lifecycle maturity berhubungan dengan Performance Maturity;

• bagaimana lifecycle maturity berhubungan dengan organizational maturity.

Claude Code tidak boleh menganggap Performance Maturity Model sebagai lifecycle maturity model hanya karena strukturnya serupa.

Jika terdapat existing canonical lifecycle maturity model, source tersebut harus menjadi prioritas.

Jika tidak ditemukan:

pertahankan F9 sebagai bounded canonical synthesis dan dokumentasikan gap sebagai Architecture Issue / ADR.

⸻

83\. Success Criteria

F9 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. lifecycle improvement dibedakan dari lifecycle maturity;

2\. improvement memiliki evidence basis;

3\. improvement memiliki identifiable opportunity;

4\. material improvement memiliki applicable authority;

5\. improvement memiliki owner;

6\. improvement memiliki validation;

7\. lifecycle learning dapat ditangkap;

8\. recurring failure dapat diidentifikasi;

9\. improvement failure dapat dipelajari;

10\. improvement tidak menghapus historical evidence;

11\. improvement tidak menciptakan authority baru;

12\. improvement tidak memperluas PD-03 ownership;

13\. cross-platform improvement mempertahankan Platform autonomy;

14\. maturity assessment berada pada F9;

15\. maturity dibedakan dari lifecycle success;

16\. maturity assessment berbasis evidence;

17\. maturity confidence dapat dinilai;

18\. insufficient evidence dapat mencegah overclaim;

19\. maturity profile dapat memperlihatkan dimension-level weakness;

20\. maturity gap dapat diidentifikasi;

21\. maturity improvement loop tersedia;

22\. maturity regression dapat dicatat;

23\. maturity sustainability dapat dinilai;

24\. maturity target memiliki rationale;

25\. maturity tidak menjadi ranking exercise;

26\. maturity tidak menciptakan automatic sanction;

27\. lifecycle improvement tidak digunakan untuk bypass governance;

28\. lifecycle improvement tetap terhubung dengan F8 implementation;

29\. lifecycle improvement dapat menggunakan F6 findings;

30\. lifecycle improvement dapat menggunakan Part E evidence;

31\. F9 tidak mengambil alih F10 success determination;

32\. source-backed content dan bounded synthesis dibedakan;

33\. Claude Code memiliki explicit reconciliation directive;

34\. tidak terdapat material contradiction dengan F1–F8;

35\. F9 dapat menjadi reusable lifecycle improvement/maturity pattern setelah domain adaptation.

85\. Canonical Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity Statement

F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity establishes the architecture through which PD-03 continuously learns from lifecycle evidence, identifies and validates improvement opportunities, and assesses the maturity of its lifecycle capability while preserving governance authority, organizational ownership, Platform autonomy, historical continuity, evidence integrity, and lifecycle control.

Lifecycle improvement shall improve demonstrated capability rather than merely improve documentation or maturity labels. Lifecycle maturity shall be determined from observable evidence and shall not create authority, organizational ownership, or automatic consequence beyond the applicable governance architecture.

Where source does not define a definitive lifecycle maturity taxonomy, the taxonomy shall remain explicitly bounded until reconciled against the canonical AIOS architecture corpus.

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part F — Lifecycle Architecture

\# F10 — Lifecycle Success

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* F10

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Lifecycle Success

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-03

\> \*\*Official Name:\*\* Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Volume:\*\* Volume 3

\> \*\*Part:\*\* Part F — Lifecycle Architecture

\> \*\*Predecessor:\*\* F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity

\> \*\*Primary Constitutional Parent:\*\* F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

\> \*\*Primary Dependencies:\*\* F2 — Lifecycle Model; F3 — Lifecycle States; F4 — Lifecycle Transition; F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance; F6 — Lifecycle Review; F7 — Lifecycle Control; F8 — Lifecycle Implementation; F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Classification:\*\* Lifecycle Success Architecture

\> \*\*Source-Fidelity Mode:\*\* Bounded Canonical Synthesis

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION

\---

\# 1. Purpose

F10 menetapkan architecture untuk menentukan apakah suatu lifecycle change, lifecycle transition, atau lifecycle outcome dapat dinyatakan \*\*successful\*\*.

F10 menjawab:

\> \*\*Apakah lifecycle outcome yang dihasilkan benar, sah, traceable, operationally ready, governance-compliant, dan tetap berada dalam boundary yang benar?\*\*

F10 tidak hanya menilai apakah transition selesai.

Baseline secara eksplisit menyatakan:

\> Lifecycle success tidak hanya berarti transition berhasil dieksekusi.

Lifecycle success harus mempertimbangkan:

\- correctness;

\- authority compliance;

\- boundary preservation;

\- traceability;

\- continuity;

\- operational readiness;

\- governance integrity;

\- resulting-state validity;

\- cross-platform integrity. Performance Architecture Review.txt

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

F10 merupakan child terakhir dari F1 — Lifecycle Constitution.

Relationship:

\`\`\`text

F1 — Lifecycle Constitution

↓

F2 — Lifecycle Model

↓

F3 — Lifecycle States

↓

F4 — Lifecycle Transition

↓

F5 — Lifecycle Change Governance

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

↓

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

↓

F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity

↓

F10 — Lifecycle Success

F10 tidak menggantikan F1–F9.

F10 menggunakan evidence yang dihasilkan oleh section-section tersebut untuk menentukan lifecycle outcome.

⸻

3\. Lifecycle Success Boundary

Lifecycle success berada pada boundary outcome validation.

Lifecycle Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

F10 — Lifecycle Success

F10 tidak menentukan:

• strategic success;

• organizational performance;

• technical implementation success;

• individual performance;

• enterprise business success.

F10 hanya menentukan lifecycle success dalam boundary PD-03 Lifecycle Architecture.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Lifecycle Success Principle

A lifecycle outcome shall be considered successful only when the authorized lifecycle intent has been achieved with valid authority, correct resulting state, preserved boundaries, sufficient evidence, operational readiness, governance integrity, and traceable continuity.

Dengan demikian:

Implemented

≠

Verified

≠

Successful

Implementation adalah aktivitas.

Verification adalah evidence-producing activity.

Success adalah lifecycle outcome determination.

⸻

5\. Lifecycle Success vs Transition Completion

Transition completion tidak otomatis berarti lifecycle success.

Contoh:

Transition Executed

↓

Resulting State Exists

↓

Authority Invalid

↓

Lifecycle Success = FAIL

Atau:

Transition Executed

↓

Authority Valid

↓

Resulting State Incorrect

↓

Lifecycle Success = FAIL

Atau:

Transition Executed

↓

Correct Resulting State

↓

Historical Traceability Lost

↓

Lifecycle Success = FAIL

⸻

6\. Lifecycle Success vs Lifecycle Maturity

F9 menilai lifecycle maturity.

F10 menilai lifecycle success.

F9

Lifecycle Capability

↓

Maturity

F10

Lifecycle Outcome

↓

Success

Maturity tinggi tidak otomatis berarti setiap lifecycle transition successful.

Sebaliknya, successful transition tidak otomatis berarti lifecycle system telah mencapai maturity tinggi.

F9 dan F10 harus tetap terpisah.

⸻

7\. Lifecycle Success vs Performance Success

Performance evidence dapat digunakan sebagai input lifecycle success assessment.

Namun:

Performance Result

↓

Lifecycle Evidence

bukan:

Performance Result

↓

Automatic Lifecycle Success

Performance Architecture tetap memiliki boundary sendiri.

⸻

8\. Lifecycle Success vs Governance Compliance

Governance compliance merupakan salah satu success dimension.

Namun compliance saja tidak cukup.

Compliance

-

Correctness

-

Resulting State

-

Operational Readiness

-

Traceability

-

Boundary Integrity

↓

Lifecycle Success

⸻

9\. Lifecycle Success Dimensions

Berdasarkan source, F10 harus mempertimbangkan sekurang-kurangnya:

0\. Correctness

0\. Authority Compliance

0\. Boundary Preservation

0\. Traceability

0\. Continuity

0\. Operational Readiness

0\. Governance Integrity

0\. Resulting-State Validity

0\. Cross-Platform Integrity

Kesembilan dimensi tersebut merupakan source-backed success boundary karena disebut secara eksplisit dalam lifecycle success boundary.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

10\. Correctness

Correctness menjawab:

Apakah lifecycle outcome benar-benar menghasilkan kondisi yang dimaksud?

Assessment harus membandingkan:

Authorized Intent

↓

Expected Result

↓

Observed Result

Jika observed result tidak sesuai expected result:

lifecycle success tidak boleh dinyatakan tanpa documented exception atau corrective disposition.

⸻

11\. Authority Compliance

Authority compliance menjawab:

Apakah lifecycle action dilakukan berdasarkan authority yang sah?

Assessment harus dapat menunjukkan:

• applicable authority;

• authorization;

• decision reference;

• delegation apabila applicable;

• approval apabila required;

• escalation apabila required.

Lifecycle architecture tidak menciptakan authority baru.

Authority tetap berasal dari applicable governance architecture.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

12\. Boundary Preservation

Lifecycle success membutuhkan preservation of boundary.

Boundary yang harus dipertahankan mencakup:

• organizational boundary;

• governance boundary;

• operational boundary;

• Platform boundary;

• cross-platform ownership.

Lifecycle change tidak boleh dianggap successful apabila keberhasilannya dicapai dengan mengambil alih domain Platform lain.

Source menegaskan bahwa cross-platform impact tidak memindahkan ownership Platform lain.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

13\. Traceability

Lifecycle success membutuhkan traceable lifecycle record.

Minimum chain:

Lifecycle Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Result

↓

Success Determination

Source menetapkan lifecycle traceability sebagai hubungan antara initiating matter, evidence, authority, decision, implementation, resulting state, dan canonical record.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

14\. Continuity

Lifecycle success harus mempertahankan historical continuity.

Retirement atau restructuring tidak otomatis menghapus:

• historical decisions;

• evidence;

• accountability records;

• governance records;

• architectural records;

• compliance records;

• performance records;

• lifecycle history.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian:

New State

-

Historical Continuity

↓

Lifecycle Integrity

⸻

15\. Operational Readiness

Resulting state harus siap untuk dioperasikan sesuai applicable operating model.

Relationship:

Lifecycle Transition

↓

Resulting State

↓

Operational Readiness

↓

Part D

Operational readiness tidak berarti F10 mengambil alih Part D.

F10 hanya menentukan apakah lifecycle outcome telah mencapai kondisi yang diperlukan agar resulting state dapat dioperasikan secara legitimate.

⸻

16\. Governance Integrity

Lifecycle success harus mempertahankan governance integrity.

Governance integrity mencakup:

• authority validity;

• accountability clarity;

• review compliance;

• delegation integrity;

• escalation integrity;

• compliance evidence;

• decision traceability.

Lifecycle outcome tidak successful apabila proses mencapai target dengan bypass terhadap governance architecture.

⸻

17\. Resulting-State Validity

Resulting state harus:

• identifiable;

• consistent dengan authorized decision;

• verifiable;

• recorded;

• owned;

• operationally meaningful;

• traceable.

Canonical pattern:

Authorized Target State

↓

Implemented State

↓

Verified State

↓

Recorded Resulting State

Jika keempatnya tidak konsisten, lifecycle success harus ditahan atau diklasifikasikan sesuai applicable exception.

⸻

18\. Cross-Platform Integrity

Lifecycle success harus mempertimbangkan material cross-platform impact.

Jika lifecycle change PD-03 memengaruhi:

• Architecture;

• Security;

• Quality;

• Runtime;

• Infrastructure;

• Knowledge;

• AI Engineering;

• Developer Experience;

maka applicable coordination and governance mechanism harus digunakan.

Cross-platform integrity berarti perubahan PD-03 tidak menghasilkan unintended ownership transfer atau architectural inconsistency pada Platform lain.

⸻

19\. Success Evidence

Lifecycle success harus evidence-based.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• authorization record;

• decision record;

• current state;

• target state;

• transition record;

• implementation record;

• verification result;

• resulting state;

• operational readiness evidence;

• compliance evidence;

• cross-platform impact evidence;

• review record.

Canonical lifecycle record baseline secara eksplisit mensyaratkan current state, target state apabila applicable, change type, rationale, authority, accountability, evidence, decision reference, transition record apabila applicable, verification result, resulting state, effective date, dan historical reference.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

20\. Evidence Sufficiency

Tidak semua lifecycle success assessment membutuhkan jumlah evidence yang sama.

Evidence harus proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• impact;

• scope;

• dependency;

• reversibility;

• risk.

Namun material lifecycle success tidak boleh ditentukan hanya berdasarkan assertion tanpa sufficient evidence.

⸻

21\. Success Assessment

Canonical assessment:

Expected Lifecycle Outcome

↓

Observed Lifecycle Outcome

↓

Evidence Assessment

↓

Success Dimension Assessment

↓

Overall Lifecycle Success Determination

Assessment harus dapat membedakan:

• success;

• partial success;

• unsuccessful;

• indeterminate;

• blocked;

• exception-based outcome.

Status taxonomy di atas adalah bounded synthesis dan harus direconcile oleh Claude Code apabila belum tersedia dalam canonical corpus.

⸻

22\. Success Determination

F10 tidak boleh menggunakan satu score tunggal sebagai satu-satunya basis success.

Success harus melihat evidence across dimensions.

Contoh:

Correctness PASS

Authority Compliance PASS

Traceability PASS

Continuity PASS

Operational Readiness PASS

Governance Integrity PASS

Resulting State PASS

Cross-Platform PASS

Jika terdapat critical failure pada authority atau resulting-state validity, lifecycle success tidak boleh dipaksakan menjadi PASS hanya karena dimensi lain berhasil.

⸻

23\. Critical Failure Principle

Certain failure conditions dapat menjadi lifecycle success blocker.

Contoh bounded principle:

Invalid Authority

↓

Success Blocker

Invalid Resulting State

↓

Success Blocker

Lost Material Traceability

↓

Success Blocker

Material Boundary Violation

↓

Success Blocker

Catatan: daftar blocker formal belum ditetapkan secara literal dalam source dan harus direconcile sebelum menjadi mandatory universal rule.

⸻

24\. Partial Success

Lifecycle outcome dapat menghasilkan partial success apabila:

• primary objective tercapai;

• tetapi non-critical supporting condition belum sepenuhnya tercapai;

• gap terdokumentasi;

• owner tersedia;

• remediation memiliki disposition;

• lifecycle integrity tetap terjaga.

Partial success tidak boleh digunakan untuk menutupi critical governance failure.

⸻

25\. Indeterminate Success

Success harus dapat diklasifikasikan sebagai indeterminate apabila:

• evidence tidak cukup;

• resulting state belum dapat diverifikasi;

• operational readiness belum dapat dibuktikan;

• cross-platform impact belum selesai dinilai;

• authority record belum lengkap.

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Success = Indeterminate

Bukan:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Assume Success

⸻

26\. Failed Lifecycle Outcome

Lifecycle outcome dapat dinyatakan unsuccessful apabila:

• authorized objective tidak tercapai;

• resulting state invalid;

• material authority violation terjadi;

• material boundary violation terjadi;

• evidence menunjukkan transition failure;

• governance integrity material rusak;

• operational readiness gagal;

• cross-platform integrity material terganggu.

Failure harus direkam dan menjadi input F9 improvement.

⸻

27\. Success and Exception

Lifecycle exception tidak otomatis membuat outcome unsuccessful.

Namun exception harus:

• authorized;

• documented;

• justified;

• mitigated;

• reviewed apabila material;

• reflected dalam success determination.

Relationship:

Exception

↓

Assessment

↓

Disposition

↓

Success Impact

⸻

28\. Success and Irreversible Transition

Untuk irreversible transition, success assessment membutuhkan additional scrutiny.

Source menetapkan bahwa jika transition irreversible:

• irreversibility harus diketahui sebelum authorization;

• consequence harus dinilai;

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• resulting state harus dapat diverifikasi.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

F10 harus menggunakan evidence tersebut dalam final success determination.

⸻

29\. Success and Lifecycle Change Impact

Material lifecycle success harus memperhitungkan impact assessment terhadap:

• organizational structure;

• authority;

• accountability;

• governance;

• compliance;

• operations;

• performance;

• dependencies;

• canonical artifacts;

• cross-platform interfaces.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

30\. Success and Implementation

F8 menghasilkan implementation evidence.

F10 menilai outcome implementation tersebut.

F8

Lifecycle Implementation

↓

Implementation Evidence

↓

Verification

↓

F10

Lifecycle Success

F10 tidak mengambil alih implementation authority.

31\. Success and Review

36\. Success Record

Material lifecycle success determination harus dapat direkam dengan minimum:

￼

37\. Success Determination Record

Success determination harus menyimpan rationale.

Minimum:

Expected Outcome

Observed Outcome

Evidence

Dimension Assessment

Exception

Determination

Rationale

Reviewer

Reference

Tidak boleh hanya menyimpan:

SUCCESS = TRUE

tanpa evidence dan rationale untuk material lifecycle outcomes.

⸻

38\. Lifecycle Success Reporting

Material lifecycle success dapat dilaporkan melalui applicable governance/performance reporting.

Reporting harus dapat menunjukkan:

• lifecycle subject;

• change;

• expected outcome;

• observed outcome;

• success determination;

• unresolved gaps;

• exceptions;

• improvement implications.

Reporting tidak boleh menghilangkan material negative findings.

⸻

39\. Historical Success Record

Success determination harus dipertahankan sebagai historical lifecycle evidence.

Perubahan subsequent terhadap lifecycle state tidak boleh menghapus historical success determination.

Historical Outcome

↓

Preserved

↓

Future Lifecycle Evidence

⸻

40\. Success Reassessment

Lifecycle success dapat direassess apabila:

• new material evidence muncul;

• resulting state berubah;

• hidden consequence ditemukan;

• authority issue ditemukan;

• cross-platform impact berubah;

• operational failure muncul;

• previous assessment terbukti tidak sufficient.

Reassessment harus menghasilkan traceable revision.

⸻

41\. Success Does Not Rewrite History

Jika lifecycle outcome awal dinyatakan successful kemudian terbukti memiliki material failure:

Original Determination

↓

New Evidence

↓

Reassessment

↓

Revised Determination

Historical determination tidak boleh dihapus.

⸻

42\. Lifecycle Success Integrity

Success determination harus menjaga:

• evidence integrity;

• record integrity;

• authority integrity;

• state integrity;

• boundary integrity;

• historical integrity.

Success reporting tidak boleh menjadi mechanism untuk mengubah evidence agar outcome terlihat successful.

⸻

43\. Anti-Gaming Principle

Lifecycle success tidak boleh dimanipulasi melalui:

• redefining target after transition;

• redefining success criteria after outcome;

• selective evidence;

• hiding exceptions;

• ignoring boundary impact;

• relabeling resulting state;

• deleting historical failure;

• treating implementation completion as success.

Success criteria harus diketahui sebelum material lifecycle action apabila feasible.

⸻

44\. Success Criteria Stability

Material lifecycle success criteria sebaiknya ditetapkan sebelum transition execution.

Jika criteria berubah setelah execution:

• perubahan harus memiliki rationale;

• authority harus sesuai;

• historical criteria harus dipertahankan;

• impact terhadap determination harus dicatat.

⸻

45\. Lifecycle Success Ownership

Success determination harus memiliki accountable owner.

Minimum distinction:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Implementation

≠

Assessment

Assessor dapat berbeda dari implementer apabila independence diperlukan.

⸻

46\. Assessment Independence

Material lifecycle outcome dapat membutuhkan independent review apabila:

• lifecycle change high-impact;

• authority conflict;

• irreversible;

• cross-platform;

• compliance-sensitive;

• failure consequence material.

Review intensity harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

47\. Success and Compliance

Compliance evidence harus digunakan untuk menilai:

• whether required governance conditions were satisfied;

• whether mandatory controls were followed;

• whether applicable certification/approval was achieved;

• whether exceptions were authorized.

Compliance pass tetap tidak otomatis berarti lifecycle success.

⸻

48\. Success and Boundary Integrity

Lifecycle success requires:

Authorized Scope

\=

Actual Scope

Jika actual scope melampaui authorized scope:

success determination harus mempertimbangkan material boundary violation.

⸻

49\. Success and Operational Continuity

Lifecycle outcome harus mempertimbangkan continuity.

Untuk material lifecycle changes:

• transition tidak boleh meninggalkan undefined operational state;

• resulting state harus memiliki ownership;

• historical state harus tetap traceable;

• handoff harus dapat diverifikasi;

• operational readiness harus dapat dibuktikan apabila applicable.

⸻

50\. Success and Cross-Platform Dependencies

Jika dependency Platform lain terdampak:

PD-03 Lifecycle Change

↓

Dependency Assessment

↓

Coordination

↓

Affected Platform Evidence

↓

F10 Success Assessment

PD-03 tidak boleh menyatakan cross-platform lifecycle success hanya berdasarkan evidence internal apabila material external dependency belum diverifikasi.

⸻

51\. Success and Resulting State

Resulting state adalah salah satu critical evidence object.

F10 harus menjawab:

Apakah resulting state yang tercatat benar-benar merupakan state yang dihasilkan?

Decision

↓

Target State

↓

Implementation

↓

Observed State

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

Mismatch harus menjadi finding.

⸻

52\. Success and Future Lifecycle

Success determination menjadi input untuk future lifecycle needs.

Lifecycle Success

↓

Performance Observation

↓

New Evidence

↓

Future Lifecycle Need

Karena lifecycle bersifat continuous, success bukan terminal state dari seluruh lifecycle architecture.

53\. Lifecycle Success Principles

LS-01 — Outcome Over Activity

Completion of activity is not sufficient.

LS-02 — Evidence Before Claim

No material success claim without sufficient evidence.

LS-03 — Authority Integrity

Success cannot legitimize unauthorized action.

LS-04 — Resulting-State Integrity

Success requires valid resulting state.

LS-05 — Boundary Integrity

Success cannot be achieved through unauthorized domain expansion.

LS-06 — Historical Continuity

Success assessment must preserve lifecycle history.

LS-07 — Operational Readiness

Material resulting state must be ready for legitimate operation.

LS-08 — Cross-Platform Integrity

Material dependencies must be accounted for.

LS-09 — Continuous Lifecycle

Success does not terminate lifecycle learning.

LS-10 — Reassessment

New material evidence may require reassessment.

⸻

F10 does not establish:

• enterprise business success;

• strategic success;

• organizational performance;

• technical performance;

• individual performance;

• compensation;

• universal KPI;

• enterprise-wide success scoring;

• lifecycle maturity scoring;

• governance authority;

• organizational restructuring authority;

• technical implementation authority;

• cross-platform ownership.

These remain within their applicable architectures.

59\. Canonical Lifecycle Success Statement

F10 — Lifecycle Success establishes the framework through which PD-03 determines whether an authorized lifecycle outcome has achieved its intended result correctly, within authority and boundary, with sufficient evidence, valid resulting state, operational readiness, governance integrity, historical continuity, and cross-platform integrity.

Lifecycle success shall not be inferred merely from transition completion, implementation completion, compliance status, performance result, or maturity level. Material success claims require evidence and traceability sufficient to support the determination.

Lifecycle success does not terminate the lifecycle. Success becomes historical evidence and may provide input to future lifecycle observation, review, improvement, maturity assessment, and subsequent lifecycle needs.

\# 1. Purpose

F10 menetapkan architecture untuk menentukan apakah suatu lifecycle change, lifecycle transition, atau lifecycle outcome dapat dinyatakan \*\*successful\*\*.

F10 menjawab:

\> \*\*Apakah lifecycle outcome yang dihasilkan benar, sah, traceable, operationally ready, governance-compliant, dan tetap berada dalam boundary yang benar?\*\*

F10 tidak hanya menilai apakah transition selesai.

Baseline secara eksplisit menyatakan:

\> Lifecycle success tidak hanya berarti transition berhasil dieksekusi.

Lifecycle success harus mempertimbangkan:

\- correctness;

\- authority compliance;

\- boundary preservation;

\- traceability;

\- continuity;

\- operational readiness;

\- governance integrity;

\- resulting-state validity;

\- cross-platform integrity. Performance Architecture Review.txt

\---

F10 merupakan child terakhir dari F1 — Lifecycle Constitution.

↓

↓

↓

F6 — Lifecycle Review

↓

F7 — Lifecycle Control

↓

F8 — Lifecycle Implementation

↓

F9 — Lifecycle Improvement & Maturity

↓

F10 — Lifecycle Success

F10 tidak menggantikan F1–F9.

F10 menggunakan evidence yang dihasilkan oleh section-section tersebut untuk menentukan lifecycle outcome.

3\. Lifecycle Success Boundary

Lifecycle success berada pada boundary outcome validation.

Lifecycle Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Transition

↓

Verification

F10 — Lifecycle Success

F10 tidak menentukan:

• strategic success;

• organizational performance;

• technical implementation success;

• individual performance;

• enterprise business success.

F10 hanya menentukan lifecycle success dalam boundary PD-03 Lifecycle Architecture.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Lifecycle Success Principle

A lifecycle outcome shall be considered successful only when the authorized lifecycle intent has been achieved with valid authority, correct resulting state, preserved boundaries, sufficient evidence, operational readiness, governance integrity, and traceable continuity.

Dengan demikian:

Implemented

≠

Verified

≠

Successful

Implementation adalah aktivitas.

Verification adalah evidence-producing activity.

Success adalah lifecycle outcome determination.

⸻

5\. Lifecycle Success vs Transition Completion

Transition completion tidak otomatis berarti lifecycle success.

Contoh:

Transition Executed

↓

Resulting State Exists

↓

Authority Invalid

↓

Lifecycle Success = FAIL

Atau:

Transition Executed

↓

Authority Valid

↓

Resulting State Incorrect

↓

Lifecycle Success = FAIL

Atau:

Transition Executed

↓

Correct Resulting State

↓

Historical Traceability Lost

↓

Lifecycle Success = FAIL

⸻

6\. Lifecycle Success vs Lifecycle Maturity

F9 menilai lifecycle maturity.

F10 menilai lifecycle success.

F9

Lifecycle Capability

↓

Maturity

F10

Lifecycle Outcome

↓

Success

Maturity tinggi tidak otomatis berarti setiap lifecycle transition successful.

Sebaliknya, successful transition tidak otomatis berarti lifecycle system telah mencapai maturity tinggi.

F9 dan F10 harus tetap terpisah.

⸻

7\. Lifecycle Success vs Performance Success

Performance evidence dapat digunakan sebagai input lifecycle success assessment.

Namun:

Performance Result

↓

Lifecycle Evidence

bukan:

Performance Result

↓

Automatic Lifecycle Success

Performance Architecture tetap memiliki boundary sendiri.

⸻

8\. Lifecycle Success vs Governance Compliance

Governance compliance merupakan salah satu success dimension.

Namun compliance saja tidak cukup.

Compliance

-

Correctness

-

Resulting State

-

Operational Readiness

-

Traceability

-

Boundary Integrity

↓

Lifecycle Success
