9\. Lifecycle Success Dimensions

Berdasarkan source, F10 harus mempertimbangkan sekurang-kurangnya:

1\. Correctness

2\. Authority Compliance

3\. Boundary Preservation

4\. Traceability

5\. Continuity

6\. Operational Readiness

7\. Governance Integrity

8\. Resulting-State Validity

9\. Cross-Platform Integrity

Kesembilan dimensi tersebut merupakan source-backed success boundary karena disebut secara eksplisit dalam lifecycle success boundary.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

10\. Correctness

Correctness menjawab:

Apakah lifecycle outcome benar-benar menghasilkan kondisi yang dimaksud?

Assessment harus membandingkan:

Authorized Intent

↓

Expected Result

↓

Observed Result

Jika observed result tidak sesuai expected result:

lifecycle success tidak boleh dinyatakan tanpa documented exception atau corrective disposition.

⸻

11\. Authority Compliance

Authority compliance menjawab:

Apakah lifecycle action dilakukan berdasarkan authority yang sah?

Assessment harus dapat menunjukkan:

• applicable authority;

• authorization;

• decision reference;

• delegation apabila applicable;

• approval apabila required;

• escalation apabila required.

Lifecycle architecture tidak menciptakan authority baru.

Authority tetap berasal dari applicable governance architecture.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

12\. Boundary Preservation

Lifecycle success membutuhkan preservation of boundary.

Boundary yang harus dipertahankan mencakup:

• organizational boundary;

• governance boundary;

• operational boundary;

• Platform boundary;

• cross-platform ownership.

Lifecycle change tidak boleh dianggap successful apabila keberhasilannya dicapai dengan mengambil alih domain Platform lain.

Source menegaskan bahwa cross-platform impact tidak memindahkan ownership Platform lain.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

13\. Traceability

Lifecycle success membutuhkan traceable lifecycle record.

Minimum chain:

Lifecycle Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Transition

↓

Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Result

↓

Success Determination

Source menetapkan lifecycle traceability sebagai hubungan antara initiating matter, evidence, authority, decision, implementation, resulting state, dan canonical record.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

14\. Continuity

Lifecycle success harus mempertahankan historical continuity.

Retirement atau restructuring tidak otomatis menghapus:

• historical decisions;

• evidence;

• accountability records;

• governance records;

• architectural records;

• compliance records;

• performance records;

• lifecycle history.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian:

New State

-

Historical Continuity

↓

Lifecycle Integrity

⸻

15\. Operational Readiness

Resulting state harus siap untuk dioperasikan sesuai applicable operating model.

Relationship:

Lifecycle Transition

↓

Resulting State

↓

Operational Readiness

↓

Part D

Operational readiness tidak berarti F10 mengambil alih Part D.

F10 hanya menentukan apakah lifecycle outcome telah mencapai kondisi yang diperlukan agar resulting state dapat dioperasikan secara legitimate.

⸻

16\. Governance Integrity

Lifecycle success harus mempertahankan governance integrity.

Governance integrity mencakup:

• authority validity;

• accountability clarity;

• review compliance;

• delegation integrity;

• escalation integrity;

• compliance evidence;

• decision traceability.

Lifecycle outcome tidak successful apabila proses mencapai target dengan bypass terhadap governance architecture.

⸻

17\. Resulting-State Validity

Resulting state harus:

• identifiable;

• consistent dengan authorized decision;

• verifiable;

• recorded;

• owned;

• operationally meaningful;

• traceable.

Canonical pattern:

Authorized Target State

↓

Implemented State

↓

Verified State

↓

Recorded Resulting State

Jika keempatnya tidak konsisten, lifecycle success harus ditahan atau diklasifikasikan sesuai applicable exception.

⸻

18\. Cross-Platform Integrity

Lifecycle success harus mempertimbangkan material cross-platform impact.

Jika lifecycle change PD-03 memengaruhi:

• Architecture;

• Security;

• Quality;

• Runtime;

• Infrastructure;

• Knowledge;

• AI Engineering;

• Developer Experience;

maka applicable coordination and governance mechanism harus digunakan.

Cross-platform integrity berarti perubahan PD-03 tidak menghasilkan unintended ownership transfer atau architectural inconsistency pada Platform lain.

⸻

19\. Success Evidence

Lifecycle success harus evidence-based.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• authorization record;

• decision record;

• current state;

• target state;

• transition record;

• implementation record;

• verification result;

• resulting state;

• operational readiness evidence;

• compliance evidence;

• cross-platform impact evidence;

• review record.

Canonical lifecycle record baseline secara eksplisit mensyaratkan current state, target state apabila applicable, change type, rationale, authority, accountability, evidence, decision reference, transition record apabila applicable, verification result, resulting state, effective date, dan historical reference.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

20\. Evidence Sufficiency

Tidak semua lifecycle success assessment membutuhkan jumlah evidence yang sama.

Evidence harus proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• impact;

• scope;

• dependency;

• reversibility;

• risk.

Namun material lifecycle success tidak boleh ditentukan hanya berdasarkan assertion tanpa sufficient evidence.

⸻

21\. Success Assessment

Canonical assessment:

Expected Lifecycle Outcome

↓

Observed Lifecycle Outcome

↓

Evidence Assessment

↓

Success Dimension Assessment

↓

Overall Lifecycle Success Determination

Assessment harus dapat membedakan:

• success;

• partial success;

• unsuccessful;

• indeterminate;

• blocked;

• exception-based outcome.

Status taxonomy di atas adalah bounded synthesis dan harus direconcile oleh Claude Code apabila belum tersedia dalam canonical corpus.

⸻

22\. Success Determination

F10 tidak boleh menggunakan satu score tunggal sebagai satu-satunya basis success.

Success harus melihat evidence across dimensions.

Contoh:

Correctness PASS

Authority Compliance PASS

Traceability PASS

Continuity PASS

Operational Readiness PASS

Governance Integrity PASS

Resulting State PASS

Cross-Platform PASS

Jika terdapat critical failure pada authority atau resulting-state validity, lifecycle success tidak boleh dipaksakan menjadi PASS hanya karena dimensi lain berhasil.

⸻

23\. Critical Failure Principle

Certain failure conditions dapat menjadi lifecycle success blocker.

Contoh bounded principle:

Invalid Authority

↓

Success Blocker

Invalid Resulting State

↓

Success Blocker

Lost Material Traceability

↓

Success Blocker

Material Boundary Violation

↓

Success Blocker

Catatan: daftar blocker formal belum ditetapkan secara literal dalam source dan harus direconcile sebelum menjadi mandatory universal rule.

⸻

24\. Partial Success

Lifecycle outcome dapat menghasilkan partial success apabila:

• primary objective tercapai;

• tetapi non-critical supporting condition belum sepenuhnya tercapai;

• gap terdokumentasi;

• owner tersedia;

• remediation memiliki disposition;

• lifecycle integrity tetap terjaga.

Partial success tidak boleh digunakan untuk menutupi critical governance failure.

⸻

25\. Indeterminate Success

Success harus dapat diklasifikasikan sebagai indeterminate apabila:

• evidence tidak cukup;

• resulting state belum dapat diverifikasi;

• operational readiness belum dapat dibuktikan;

• cross-platform impact belum selesai dinilai;

• authority record belum lengkap.

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Success = Indeterminate

Bukan:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Assume Success

⸻

26\. Failed Lifecycle Outcome

Lifecycle outcome dapat dinyatakan unsuccessful apabila:

• authorized objective tidak tercapai;

• resulting state invalid;

• material authority violation terjadi;

• material boundary violation terjadi;

• evidence menunjukkan transition failure;

• governance integrity material rusak;

• operational readiness gagal;

• cross-platform integrity material terganggu.

Failure harus direkam dan menjadi input F9 improvement.

⸻

27\. Success and Exception

Lifecycle exception tidak otomatis membuat outcome unsuccessful.

Namun exception harus:

• authorized;

• documented;

• justified;

• mitigated;

• reviewed apabila material;

• reflected dalam success determination.

Relationship:

Exception

↓

Assessment

↓

Disposition

↓

Success Impact

⸻

28\. Success and Irreversible Transition

Untuk irreversible transition, success assessment membutuhkan additional scrutiny.

Source menetapkan bahwa jika transition irreversible:

• irreversibility harus diketahui sebelum authorization;

• consequence harus dinilai;

• evidence harus memadai;

• authority harus sesuai;

• resulting state harus dapat diverifikasi.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

F10 harus menggunakan evidence tersebut dalam final success determination.

⸻

29\. Success and Lifecycle Change Impact

Material lifecycle success harus memperhitungkan impact assessment terhadap:

• organizational structure;

• authority;

• accountability;

• governance;

• compliance;

• operations;

• performance;

• dependencies;

• canonical artifacts;

• cross-platform interfaces.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

30\. Success and Implementation

F8 menghasilkan implementation evidence.

F10 menilai outcome implementation tersebut.

F8

Lifecycle Implementation

↓

Implementation Evidence

↓

Verification

↓

F10

Lifecycle Success

F10 tidak mengambil alih implementation authority.

⸻

31\. Success and Review

F6 menyediakan lifecycle review evidence.

F6

Lifecycle Review

↓

Finding / Assessment

↓

F10

Success Determination

F10 tidak menggantikan lifecycle review.

⸻

32\. Success and Control

F7 menyediakan lifecycle control evidence.

Success assessment harus mempertimbangkan apakah required controls:

• existed;

• operated;

• were followed;

• produced evidence;

• remained effective.

⸻

33\. Success and Improvement

F9 menerima hasil F10 sebagai input improvement ketika lifecycle outcome:

• unsuccessful;

• partially successful;

• recurring failure;

• degraded;

• produces unexpected consequence.

Canonical loop:

F10

Success Determination

↓

Finding

↓

F9

Improvement

↓

Future Lifecycle Cycle

⸻

34\. Success and Maturity

F9 maturity assessment dapat menggunakan historical F10 outcomes sebagai evidence.

Repeated F10 Outcomes

↓

Lifecycle Evidence

↓

F9 Maturity Assessment

Namun F10 tidak menetapkan maturity level.

⸻

35\. Lifecycle Success Loop

Lifecycle success harus tetap berada dalam continuous lifecycle.

Current State

↓

Lifecycle Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Governance Review

↓

Authority Decision

↓

Authorized Transition

↓

Implementation

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

↓

Operationalization

↓

Performance Observation

↓

Lifecycle Success Assessment

↓

Future Lifecycle Need

Source secara eksplisit menetapkan lifecycle sebagai continuous loop dan menghubungkan performance observation kembali kepada future lifecycle need.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

36\. Success Record

Material lifecycle success determination harus dapat direkam dengan minimum:

Field Requirement

Lifecycle Success ID Required

Lifecycle Subject Required

Lifecycle Change Reference Required

Authorized Objective Required

Expected Result Required

Resulting State Required

Evidence Required

Verification Result Required

Success Dimensions Required

Success Determination Required

Exceptions Required when applicable

Operational Readiness Required when applicable

Cross-Platform Impact Required when applicable

Reviewer Required when applicable

Decision Reference Required

Effective Date Required

Historical Reference Required

Source-fidelity qualification: schema F10 ini adalah bounded canonical synthesis yang diturunkan dari canonical lifecycle record requirements; source tidak memberikan schema F10-specific secara literal.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

37\. Success Determination Record

Success determination harus menyimpan rationale.

Minimum:

Expected Outcome

Observed Outcome

Evidence

Dimension Assessment

Exception

Determination

Rationale

Reviewer

Reference

Tidak boleh hanya menyimpan:

SUCCESS = TRUE

tanpa evidence dan rationale untuk material lifecycle outcomes.

⸻

38\. Lifecycle Success Reporting

Material lifecycle success dapat dilaporkan melalui applicable governance/performance reporting.

Reporting harus dapat menunjukkan:

• lifecycle subject;

• change;

• expected outcome;

• observed outcome;

• success determination;

• unresolved gaps;

• exceptions;

• improvement implications.

Reporting tidak boleh menghilangkan material negative findings.

⸻

39\. Historical Success Record

Success determination harus dipertahankan sebagai historical lifecycle evidence.

Perubahan subsequent terhadap lifecycle state tidak boleh menghapus historical success determination.

Historical Outcome

↓

Preserved

↓

Future Lifecycle Evidence

⸻

40\. Success Reassessment

Lifecycle success dapat direassess apabila:

• new material evidence muncul;

• resulting state berubah;

• hidden consequence ditemukan;

• authority issue ditemukan;

• cross-platform impact berubah;

• operational failure muncul;

• previous assessment terbukti tidak sufficient.

Reassessment harus menghasilkan traceable revision.

⸻

41\. Success Does Not Rewrite History

Jika lifecycle outcome awal dinyatakan successful kemudian terbukti memiliki material failure:

Original Determination

↓

New Evidence

↓

Reassessment

↓

Revised Determination

Historical determination tidak boleh dihapus.

⸻

42\. Lifecycle Success Integrity

Success determination harus menjaga:

• evidence integrity;

• record integrity;

• authority integrity;

• state integrity;

• boundary integrity;

• historical integrity.

Success reporting tidak boleh menjadi mechanism untuk mengubah evidence agar outcome terlihat successful.

⸻

43\. Anti-Gaming Principle

Lifecycle success tidak boleh dimanipulasi melalui:

• redefining target after transition;

• redefining success criteria after outcome;

• selective evidence;

• hiding exceptions;

• ignoring boundary impact;

• relabeling resulting state;

• deleting historical failure;

• treating implementation completion as success.

Success criteria harus diketahui sebelum material lifecycle action apabila feasible.

⸻

44\. Success Criteria Stability

Material lifecycle success criteria sebaiknya ditetapkan sebelum transition execution.

Jika criteria berubah setelah execution:

• perubahan harus memiliki rationale;

• authority harus sesuai;

• historical criteria harus dipertahankan;

• impact terhadap determination harus dicatat.

⸻

45\. Lifecycle Success Ownership

Success determination harus memiliki accountable owner.

Minimum distinction:

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Implementation

≠

Assessment

Assessor dapat berbeda dari implementer apabila independence diperlukan.

⸻

46\. Assessment Independence

Material lifecycle outcome dapat membutuhkan independent review apabila:

• lifecycle change high-impact;

• authority conflict;

• irreversible;

• cross-platform;

• compliance-sensitive;

• failure consequence material.

Review intensity harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

47\. Success and Compliance

Compliance evidence harus digunakan untuk menilai:

• whether required governance conditions were satisfied;

• whether mandatory controls were followed;

• whether applicable certification/approval was achieved;

• whether exceptions were authorized.

Compliance pass tetap tidak otomatis berarti lifecycle success.

⸻

48\. Success and Boundary Integrity

Lifecycle success requires:

Authorized Scope

\=

Actual Scope

Jika actual scope melampaui authorized scope:

success determination harus mempertimbangkan material boundary violation.

⸻

49\. Success and Operational Continuity

Lifecycle outcome harus mempertimbangkan continuity.

Untuk material lifecycle changes:

• transition tidak boleh meninggalkan undefined operational state;

• resulting state harus memiliki ownership;

• historical state harus tetap traceable;

• handoff harus dapat diverifikasi;

• operational readiness harus dapat dibuktikan apabila applicable.

⸻

50\. Success and Cross-Platform Dependencies

Jika dependency Platform lain terdampak:

PD-03 Lifecycle Change

↓

Dependency Assessment

↓

Coordination

↓

Affected Platform Evidence

↓

F10 Success Assessment

PD-03 tidak boleh menyatakan cross-platform lifecycle success hanya berdasarkan evidence internal apabila material external dependency belum diverifikasi.

⸻

51\. Success and Resulting State

Resulting state adalah salah satu critical evidence object.

F10 harus menjawab:

Apakah resulting state yang tercatat benar-benar merupakan state yang dihasilkan?

Decision

↓

Target State

↓

Implementation

↓

Observed State

↓

Verification

↓

Resulting State

Mismatch harus menjadi finding.

⸻

52\. Success and Future Lifecycle

Success determination menjadi input untuk future lifecycle needs.

Lifecycle Success

↓

Performance Observation

↓

New Evidence

↓

Future Lifecycle Need

Karena lifecycle bersifat continuous, success bukan terminal state dari seluruh lifecycle architecture.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

53\. Lifecycle Success Principles

LS-01 — Outcome Over Activity

Completion of activity is not sufficient.

LS-02 — Evidence Before Claim

No material success claim without sufficient evidence.

LS-03 — Authority Integrity

Success cannot legitimize unauthorized action.

LS-04 — Resulting-State Integrity

Success requires valid resulting state.

LS-05 — Boundary Integrity

Success cannot be achieved through unauthorized domain expansion.

LS-06 — Historical Continuity

Success assessment must preserve lifecycle history.

LS-07 — Operational Readiness

Material resulting state must be ready for legitimate operation.

LS-08 — Cross-Platform Integrity

Material dependencies must be accounted for.

LS-09 — Continuous Lifecycle

Success does not terminate lifecycle learning.

LS-10 — Reassessment

New material evidence may require reassessment.

⸻

54\. Out of Scope

F10 does not establish:

• enterprise business success;

• strategic success;

• organizational performance;

• technical performance;

• individual performance;

• compensation;

• universal KPI;

• enterprise-wide success scoring;

• lifecycle maturity scoring;

• governance authority;

• organizational restructuring authority;

• technical implementation authority;

• cross-platform ownership.

These remain within their applicable architectures.

⸻

57\. Success Criteria

F10 is considered structurally complete when:

1\. lifecycle success is clearly distinguished from transition completion;

2\. lifecycle success is clearly distinguished from lifecycle maturity;

3\. correctness is assessed;

4\. authority compliance is assessed;

5\. boundary preservation is assessed;

6\. traceability is assessed;

7\. continuity is assessed;

8\. operational readiness is assessed;

9\. governance integrity is assessed;

10\. resulting-state validity is assessed;

11\. cross-platform integrity is assessed;

12\. evidence is required for material success claims;

13\. insufficient evidence can prevent premature success determination;

14\. exceptions are visible;

15\. irreversible transitions receive appropriate scrutiny;

16\. failed outcomes become improvement inputs;

17\. successful outcomes remain historical evidence;

18\. reassessment is possible when material new evidence emerges;

19\. success does not create authority;

20\. success does not transfer organizational ownership;

21\. success does not expand PD-03 domain boundary;

22\. cross-platform ownership remains preserved;

23\. F10 does not replace F6 review;

24\. F10 does not replace F7 control;

25\. F10 does not replace F8 implementation;

26\. F10 does not replace F9 maturity assessment;

27\. lifecycle remains continuous after success determination;

28\. material success determination has traceable canonical record;

29\. source gaps are explicitly marked;

30\. no unsupported universal scoring mechanism is introduced.

59\. Canonical Lifecycle Success Statement

F10 — Lifecycle Success establishes the framework through which PD-03 determines whether an authorized lifecycle outcome has achieved its intended result correctly, within authority and boundary, with sufficient evidence, valid resulting state, operational readiness, governance integrity, historical continuity, and cross-platform integrity.

Lifecycle success shall not be inferred merely from transition completion, implementation completion, compliance status, performance result, or maturity level. Material success claims require evidence and traceability sufficient to support the determination.

Lifecycle success does not terminate the lifecycle. Success becomes historical evidence and may provide input to future lifecycle observation, review, improvement, maturity assessment, and subsequent lifecycle needs.
